> وَ أَنِ‌ احْکُمْ‌ بَيْنَهُمْ‌ بِمَا أَنْزَلَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ وَ لاَ تَتَّبِعْ‌ أَهْوَاءَهُمْ‌ وَ احْذَرْهُمْ‌ أَنْ‌ يَفْتِنُوکَ‌ عَنْ‌ بَعْضِ‌ مَا أَنْزَلَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ إِلَيْکَ‌ فَإِنْ‌ تَوَلَّوْا فَاعْلَمْ‌ أَنَّمَا يُرِيدُ اللَّهُ‌ أَنْ‌ يُصِيبَهُمْ‌ بِبَعْضِ‌ ذُنُوبِهِمْ‌ وَ إِنَ‌ کَثِيراً مِنَ‌ النَّاسِ‌ لَفَاسِقُونَ‌
>
> Judge between them by what Allah has sent down, and do not follow their desires. Beware of them lest they should beguile you from part of what Allah has sent down to you. But if they turn their backs [on you], then know that Allah desires to punish them for some of their sins, and indeed many of the people are transgressors.

### Qur'an · Al-Maaida (The Table) · 5:55
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/5/55
> إِنَّمَا وَلِيُّکُمُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ وَ رَسُولُهُ‌ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا الَّذِينَ‌ يُقِيمُونَ‌ الصَّلاَةَ وَ يُؤْتُونَ‌ الزَّکَاةَ وَ هُمْ‌ رَاکِعُونَ‌
>
> Your guardian is only Allah, His Apostle, and the faithful who maintain the prayer and give the zakat while bowing down.

### Qur'an · Al-Maaida (The Table) · 5:58
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/5/58
> وَ إِذَا نَادَيْتُمْ‌ إِلَى‌ الصَّلاَةِ اتَّخَذُوهَا هُزُواً وَ لَعِباً ذٰلِکَ‌ بِأَنَّهُمْ‌ قَوْمٌ‌ لاَ يَعْقِلُونَ‌
>
> When you call to prayer, they take it in derision and play. That is because they are a people who do not exercise their reason.

### Qur'an · Al-Maaida (The Table) · 5:71
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/5/71
> وَ حَسِبُوا أَلاَّ تَکُونَ‌ فِتْنَةٌ فَعَمُوا وَ صَمُّوا ثُمَ‌ تَابَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ ثُمَ‌ عَمُوا وَ صَمُّوا کَثِيرٌ مِنْهُمْ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ بَصِيرٌ بِمَا يَعْمَلُونَ‌
>
> They supposed there would be no testing, so they became blind and deaf. Thereafter Allah accepted their repentance, yet [again] many of them became blind and deaf, and Allah watches what they do.

### Qur'an · Al-Maaida (The Table) · 5:74
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/5/74
> أَ فَلاَ يَتُوبُونَ‌ إِلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ يَسْتَغْفِرُونَهُ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Will they not repent to Allah and plead to Him for forgiveness? Yet Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Maaida (The Table) · 5:89
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/5/89
> لاَ يُؤَاخِذُکُمُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ بِاللَّغْوِ فِي‌ أَيْمَانِکُمْ‌ وَ لٰکِنْ‌ يُؤَاخِذُکُمْ‌ بِمَا عَقَّدْتُمُ‌ الْأَيْمَانَ‌ فَکَفَّارَتُهُ‌ إِطْعَامُ‌ عَشَرَةِ مَسَاکِينَ‌ مِنْ‌ أَوْسَطِ مَا تُطْعِمُونَ‌ أَهْلِيکُمْ‌ أَوْ کِسْوَتُهُمْ‌ أَوْ تَحْرِيرُ رَقَبَةٍ فَمَنْ‌ لَمْ‌ يَجِدْ فَصِيَامُ‌ ثَلاَثَةِ أَيَّامٍ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ کَفَّارَةُ أَيْمَانِکُمْ‌ إِذَا حَلَفْتُمْ‌ وَ احْفَظُوا أَيْمَانَکُمْ‌ کَذٰلِکَ‌ يُبَيِّنُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ لَکُمْ‌ آيَاتِهِ‌ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> Allah shall not take you to task for what is frivolous in your oaths; but He shall take you to task for what you pledge in earnest. The atonement for it is to feed ten needy persons with the average food you give to your families, or their clothing, or the freeing of a slave. He who cannot afford [any of these] shall fast for three days. That is the atonement for your oaths when you vow. But keep your oaths. Thus does Allah clarify His signs for you so that you may give thanks.

### Qur'an · Al-Maaida (The Table) · 5:91
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/5/91
> إِنَّمَا يُرِيدُ الشَّيْطَانُ‌ أَنْ‌ يُوقِعَ‌ بَيْنَکُمُ‌ الْعَدَاوَةَ وَ الْبَغْضَاءَ فِي‌ الْخَمْرِ وَ الْمَيْسِرِ وَ يَصُدَّکُمْ‌ عَنْ‌ ذِکْرِ اللَّهِ‌ وَ عَنِ‌ الصَّلاَةِ فَهَلْ‌ أَنْتُمْ‌ مُنْتَهُونَ‌
>
> Indeed Satan seeks to cast enmity and hatred among you through wine and gambling, and to hinder you from the remembrance of Allah and from prayer. Will you, then, relinquish?

### Qur'an · Al-Maaida (The Table) · 5:98
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/5/98
> اعْلَمُوا أَنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ شَدِيدُ الْعِقَابِ‌ وَ أَنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Know that Allah is severe in retribution, and that Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Maaida (The Table) · 5:101
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/5/101
> يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا لاَ تَسْأَلُوا عَنْ‌ أَشْيَاءَ إِنْ‌ تُبْدَ لَکُمْ‌ تَسُؤْکُمْ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ تَسْأَلُوا عَنْهَا حِينَ‌ يُنَزَّلُ‌ الْقُرْآنُ‌ تُبْدَ لَکُمْ‌ عَفَا اللَّهُ‌ عَنْهَا وَ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ حَلِيمٌ‌
>
> O you who have faith! Do not ask about things, which, if they are disclosed to you, will upset you. Yet if you ask about them while the Quran is being sent down, they shall be disclosed to you. Allah has excused it, and Allah is all-forgiving, all-forbearing.

### Qur'an · Al-Maaida (The Table) · 5:113
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/5/113
> قَالُوا نُرِيدُ أَنْ‌ نَأْکُلَ‌ مِنْهَا وَ تَطْمَئِنَ‌ قُلُوبُنَا وَ نَعْلَمَ‌ أَنْ‌ قَدْ صَدَقْتَنَا وَ نَکُونَ‌ عَلَيْهَا مِنَ‌ الشَّاهِدِينَ‌
>
> They said, ‘We desire to eat from it, and our hearts will be at rest: we shall know that you have told us the truth, and we shall be among the witnesses to it.’

### Qur'an · Al-Maaida (The Table) · 5:118
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/5/118
> إِنْ‌ تُعَذِّبْهُمْ‌ فَإِنَّهُمْ‌ عِبَادُکَ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ تَغْفِرْ لَهُمْ‌ فَإِنَّکَ‌ أَنْتَ‌ الْعَزِيزُ الْحَکِيمُ‌
>
> If You punish them, they are indeed Your creatures; but if You forgive them, You are indeed the All-mighty, the All-wise.’

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:34
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/34
> وَ لَقَدْ کُذِّبَتْ‌ رُسُلٌ‌ مِنْ‌ قَبْلِکَ‌ فَصَبَرُوا عَلَى‌ مَا کُذِّبُوا وَ أُوذُوا حَتَّى‌ أَتَاهُمْ‌ نَصْرُنَا وَ لاَ مُبَدِّلَ‌ لِکَلِمَاتِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ لَقَدْ جَاءَکَ‌ مِنْ‌ نَبَإِ الْمُرْسَلِينَ‌
>
> Apostles were certainly denied before you, yet they patiently bore being denied and tormented until Our help came to them. Nothing can change the words of Allah, and there have certainly come to you some of the accounts of the apostles.

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:40
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/40
> قُلْ‌ أَ رَأَيْتَکُمْ‌ إِنْ‌ أَتَاکُمْ‌ عَذَابُ‌ اللَّهِ‌ أَوْ أَتَتْکُمُ‌ السَّاعَةُ أَ غَيْرَ اللَّهِ‌ تَدْعُونَ‌ إِنْ‌ کُنْتُمْ‌ صَادِقِينَ‌
>
> Say, ‘Tell me, should Allah’s punishment overtake you, or should the Hour overtake you, will you supplicate anyone other than Allah, should you be truthful?

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:41
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/41
> بَلْ‌ إِيَّاهُ‌ تَدْعُونَ‌ فَيَکْشِفُ‌ مَا تَدْعُونَ‌ إِلَيْهِ‌ إِنْ‌ شَاءَ وَ تَنْسَوْنَ‌ مَا تُشْرِکُونَ‌
>
> No, Him you will supplicate, and He will remove that for which you supplicated Him, if He wishes, and you will forget what you ascribe [to Him] as [His] partners.’

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:52
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/52
> وَ لاَ تَطْرُدِ الَّذِينَ‌ يَدْعُونَ‌ رَبَّهُمْ‌ بِالْغَدَاةِ وَ الْعَشِيِ‌ يُرِيدُونَ‌ وَجْهَهُ‌ مَا عَلَيْکَ‌ مِنْ‌ حِسَابِهِمْ‌ مِنْ‌ شَيْ‌ءٍ وَ مَا مِنْ‌ حِسَابِکَ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ مِنْ‌ شَيْ‌ءٍ فَتَطْرُدَهُمْ‌ فَتَکُونَ‌ مِنَ‌ الظَّالِمِينَ‌
>
> Do not drive away those who supplicate their Lord morning and evening desiring His face. Neither are you accountable for them in any way, nor are they accountable for you in any way, so that you may drive them away and thus become one of the wrongdoers.

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:53
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/53
> وَ کَذٰلِکَ‌ فَتَنَّا بَعْضَهُمْ‌ بِبَعْضٍ‌ لِيَقُولُوا أَ هٰؤُلاَءِ مَنَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ مِنْ‌ بَيْنِنَا أَ لَيْسَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ بِأَعْلَمَ‌ بِالشَّاکِرِينَ‌
>
> Thus do We test them by means of one another so that they should say, ‘Are these the ones whom Allah has favoured from among us?!’ Does not Allah know best the grateful?!

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:54
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/54
> وَ إِذَا جَاءَکَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ يُؤْمِنُونَ‌ بِآيَاتِنَا فَقُلْ‌ سَلاَمٌ‌ عَلَيْکُمْ‌ کَتَبَ‌ رَبُّکُمْ‌ عَلَى‌ نَفْسِهِ‌ الرَّحْمَةَ أَنَّهُ‌ مَنْ‌ عَمِلَ‌ مِنْکُمْ‌ سُوءاً بِجَهَالَةٍ ثُمَ‌ تَابَ‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِهِ‌ وَ أَصْلَحَ‌ فَأَنَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> When those who have faith in Our signs come to you, say, ‘Peace to you! Your Lord has made mercy incumbent upon Himself: whoever of you commits an evil [deed] out of ignorance and then repents after that and reforms, then He is indeed all-forgiving, all-merciful.’

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:56
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/56
> قُلْ‌ إِنِّي‌ نُهِيتُ‌ أَنْ‌ أَعْبُدَ الَّذِينَ‌ تَدْعُونَ‌ مِنْ‌ دُونِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ قُلْ‌ لاَ أَتَّبِعُ‌ أَهْوَاءَکُمْ‌ قَدْ ضَلَلْتُ‌ إِذاً وَ مَا أَنَا مِنَ‌ الْمُهْتَدِينَ‌
>
> Say, ‘I have been forbidden to worship those whom you invoke besides Allah.’ Say, ‘I do not follow your desires, for then I will have gone astray, and I will not be among the [rightly] guided.’

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:63
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/63
> قُلْ‌ مَنْ‌ يُنَجِّيکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ ظُلُمَاتِ‌ الْبَرِّ وَ الْبَحْرِ تَدْعُونَهُ‌ تَضَرُّعاً وَ خُفْيَةً لَئِنْ‌ أَنْجَانَا مِنْ‌ هٰذِهِ‌ لَنَکُونَنَ‌ مِنَ‌ الشَّاکِرِينَ‌
>
> Say, ‘Who delivers you from the darkness of land and sea, [when] You invoke Him suppliantly and secretly: ‘‘If He delivers us from this, we will surely be among the grateful’’?’

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:72
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/72
> وَ أَنْ‌ أَقِيمُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ اتَّقُوهُ‌ وَ هُوَ الَّذِي‌ إِلَيْهِ‌ تُحْشَرُونَ‌
>
> and that ‘‘Maintain the prayer and be wary of Him, and it is He toward whom you will be gathered.’’ ’

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:92
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/92
> وَ هٰذَا کِتَابٌ‌ أَنْزَلْنَاهُ‌ مُبَارَکٌ‌ مُصَدِّقُ‌ الَّذِي‌ بَيْنَ‌ يَدَيْهِ‌ وَ لِتُنْذِرَ أُمَ‌ الْقُرَى‌ وَ مَنْ‌ حَوْلَهَا وَ الَّذِينَ‌ يُؤْمِنُونَ‌ بِالْآخِرَةِ يُؤْمِنُونَ‌ بِهِ‌ وَ هُمْ‌ عَلَى‌ صَلاَتِهِمْ‌ يُحَافِظُونَ‌
>
> Blessed is this Book, which We have sent down, confirming what was [revealed] before it, so that you may warn the Mother of Cities and those around it. Those who believe in the Hereafter believe in it, and they are watchful of their prayers.

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:98
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/98
> وَ هُوَ الَّذِي‌ أَنْشَأَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ نَفْسٍ‌ وَاحِدَةٍ فَمُسْتَقَرٌّ وَ مُسْتَوْدَعٌ‌ قَدْ فَصَّلْنَا الْآيَاتِ‌ لِقَوْمٍ‌ يَفْقَهُونَ‌
>
> It is He who created you from a single soul, then there is the [enduring] abode and the place of temporary lodging. We have certainly elaborated the signs for a people who understand.

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:125
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/125
> فَمَنْ‌ يُرِدِ اللَّهُ‌ أَنْ‌ يَهْدِيَهُ‌ يَشْرَحْ‌ صَدْرَهُ‌ لِلْإِسْلاَمِ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ يُرِدْ أَنْ‌ يُضِلَّهُ‌ يَجْعَلْ‌ صَدْرَهُ‌ ضَيِّقاً حَرَجاً کَأَنَّمَا يَصَّعَّدُ فِي‌ السَّمَاءِ کَذٰلِکَ‌ يَجْعَلُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ الرِّجْسَ‌ عَلَى‌ الَّذِينَ‌ لاَ يُؤْمِنُونَ‌
>
> Whomever Allah desires to guide, He opens his breast to Islam, and whomever He desires to lead astray, He makes his breast narrow and straitened as if he were climbing to a height. Thus does Allah lay [spiritual] defilement on those who do not have faith.

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:145
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/145
> قُلْ‌ لاَ أَجِدُ فِي‌ مَا أُوحِيَ‌ إِلَيَ‌ مُحَرَّماً عَلَى‌ طَاعِمٍ‌ يَطْعَمُهُ‌ إِلاَّ أَنْ‌ يَکُونَ‌ مَيْتَةً أَوْ دَماً مَسْفُوحاً أَوْ لَحْمَ‌ خِنزِيرٍ فَإِنَّهُ‌ رِجْسٌ‌ أَوْ فِسْقاً أُهِلَ‌ لِغَيْرِ اللَّهِ‌ بِهِ‌ فَمَنِ‌ اضْطُرَّ غَيْرَ بَاغٍ‌ وَ لاَ عَادٍ فَإِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Say, ‘I do not find in what has been revealed to me that anyone be forbidden to eat anything except carrion or spilt blood, or the flesh of swine—for that is indeed unclean—or an impiety offered to other than Allah.’ But should someone be compelled, without being rebellious or aggressive, indeed your Lord is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:150
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/150
> قُلْ‌ هَلُمَ‌ شُهَدَاءَکُمُ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ يَشْهَدُونَ‌ أَنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ حَرَّمَ‌ هٰذَا فَإِنْ‌ شَهِدُوا فَلاَ تَشْهَدْ مَعَهُمْ‌ وَ لاَ تَتَّبِعْ‌ أَهْوَاءَ الَّذِينَ‌ کَذَّبُوا بِآيَاتِنَا وَ الَّذِينَ‌ لاَ يُؤْمِنُونَ‌ بِالْآخِرَةِ وَ هُمْ‌ بِرَبِّهِمْ‌ يَعْدِلُونَ‌
>
> Say, ‘Bring your witnesses who may testify that Allah has forbidden this.’ So if they testify, do not testify with them, and do not follow the desires of those who deny Our signs, and those who do not believe in the Hereafter and equate [others] with their Lord.

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:162
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/162
> قُلْ‌ إِنَ‌ صَلاَتِي‌ وَ نُسُکِي‌ وَ مَحْيَايَ‌ وَ مَمَاتِي‌ لِلَّهِ‌ رَبِ‌ الْعَالَمِينَ‌
>
> Say, ‘Indeed my prayer and my worship, my life and my death are for the sake of Allah, the Lord of all the worlds.

### Qur'an · Al-An'aam (The Cattle) · 6:165
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/6/165
> وَ هُوَ الَّذِي‌ جَعَلَکُمْ‌ خَلاَئِفَ‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ رَفَعَ‌ بَعْضَکُمْ فَوْقَ‌ بَعْضٍ‌ دَرَجَاتٍ‌ لِيَبْلُوَکُمْ‌ فِي‌ مَا آتَاکُمْ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ سَرِيعُ‌ الْعِقَابِ‌ وَ إِنَّهُ‌ لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> It is He who has made you successors on the earth, and raised some of you in rank above others so that He may test you in respect to what He has given you. Indeed your Lord is swift in retribution, and indeed, He is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:10
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/10
> وَ لَقَدْ مَکَّنَّاکُمْ‌ فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ جَعَلْنَا لَکُمْ‌ فِيهَا مَعَايِشَ‌ قَلِيلاً مَا تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> Certainly We have established you on the earth, and made in it [various] means of livelihood for you. Little do you thank.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:17
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/17
> ثُمَ‌ لَآتِيَنَّهُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ بَيْنِ‌ أَيْدِيهِمْ‌ وَ مِنْ‌ خَلْفِهِمْ‌ وَ عَنْ‌ أَيْمَانِهِمْ‌ وَ عَنْ‌ شَمَائِلِهِمْ‌ وَ لاَ تَجِدُ أَکْثَرَهُمْ‌ شَاکِرِينَ‌
>
> Then I will come at them from their front and from their rear, and from their right and their left, and You will not find most of them to be grateful.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:23
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/23
> قَالاَ رَبَّنَا ظَلَمْنَا أَنْفُسَنَا وَ إِنْ‌ لَمْ‌ تَغْفِرْ لَنَا وَ تَرْحَمْنَا لَنَکُونَنَ‌ مِنَ‌ الْخَاسِرِينَ‌
>
> They said, ‘Our Lord, we have wronged ourselves! If You do not forgive us and have mercy upon us, we will surely be among the losers.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:29
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/29
> قُلْ‌ أَمَرَ رَبِّي‌ بِالْقِسْطِ وَ أَقِيمُوا وُجُوهَکُمْ‌ عِنْدَ کُلِ‌ مَسْجِدٍ وَ ادْعُوهُ‌ مُخْلِصِينَ‌ لَهُ‌ الدِّينَ‌ کَمَا بَدَأَکُمْ‌ تَعُودُونَ‌
>
> Say, ‘My Lord has enjoined justice,’ and [He has enjoined,] ‘Set your heart [on Him] at every occasion of prayer, and invoke Him, putting your exclusive faith in Him. Even as He brought you forth in the beginning, so will you return.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:31
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/31
> يَا بَنِي‌ آدَمَ‌ خُذُوا زِينَتَکُمْ‌ عِنْدَ کُلِ‌ مَسْجِدٍ وَ کُلُوا وَ اشْرَبُوا وَ لاَ تُسْرِفُوا إِنَّهُ‌ لاَ يُحِبُ‌ الْمُسْرِفِينَ‌
>
> O Children of Adam! Put on your adornment on every occasion of prayer, and eat and drink, but do not waste; indeed, He does not like the wasteful.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:55
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/55
> ادْعُوا رَبَّکُمْ‌ تَضَرُّعاً وَ خُفْيَةً إِنَّهُ‌ لاَ يُحِبُ‌ الْمُعْتَدِينَ‌
>
> Supplicate your Lord, beseechingly and secretly. Indeed, He does not like the transgressors.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:56
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/56
> وَ لاَ تُفْسِدُوا فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ بَعْدَ إِصْلاَحِهَا وَ ادْعُوهُ‌ خَوْفاً وَ طَمَعاً إِنَ‌ رَحْمَةَ اللَّهِ‌ قَرِيبٌ‌ مِنَ‌ الْمُحْسِنِينَ‌
>
> And do not cause corruption on the earth after its restoration, and supplicate Him with fear and hope: indeed Allah’s mercy is close to the virtuous.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:58
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/58
> وَ الْبَلَدُ الطَّيِّبُ‌ يَخْرُجُ‌ نَبَاتُهُ‌ بِإِذْنِ‌ رَبِّهِ‌ وَ الَّذِي‌ خَبُثَ‌ لاَ يَخْرُجُ‌ إِلاَّ نَکِداً کَذٰلِکَ‌ نُصَرِّفُ‌ الْآيَاتِ‌ لِقَوْمٍ‌ يَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> The good land—its vegetation comes out by the permission of its Lord, and as for that which is bad, it does not come out except sparsely. Thus do We paraphrase the signs variously for a people who give thanks.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:81
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/81
> إِنَّکُمْ‌ لَتَأْتُونَ‌ الرِّجَالَ‌ شَهْوَةً مِنْ‌ دُونِ‌ النِّسَاءِ بَلْ‌ أَنْتُمْ‌ قَوْمٌ‌ مُسْرِفُونَ‌
>
> Indeed you come to men with desire instead of women! Indeed, you are a profligate lot.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:87
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/87
> وَ إِنْ‌ کَانَ‌ طَائِفَةٌ مِنْکُمْ‌ آمَنُوا بِالَّذِي‌ أُرْسِلْتُ‌ بِهِ‌ وَ طَائِفَةٌ لَمْ‌ يُؤْمِنُوا فَاصْبِرُوا حَتَّى‌ يَحْکُمَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ بَيْنَنَا وَ هُوَ خَيْرُ الْحَاکِمِينَ‌
>
> If a group of you have believed in what I have been sent with, and a group have not believed, be patient until Allah judges between us, and He is the best of judges.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:94
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/94
> وَ مَا أَرْسَلْنَا فِي‌ قَرْيَةٍ مِنْ‌ نَبِيٍ‌ إِلاَّ أَخَذْنَا أَهْلَهَا بِالْبَأْسَاءِ وَ الضَّرَّاءِ لَعَلَّهُمْ‌ يَضَّرَّعُونَ‌
>
> We did not send a prophet to any town without visiting its people with stress and distress so that they might entreat [for Allah’s forgiveness].

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:126
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/126
> وَ مَا تَنْقِمُ‌ مِنَّا إِلاَّ أَنْ‌ آمَنَّا بِآيَاتِ‌ رَبِّنَا لَمَّا جَاءَتْنَا رَبَّنَا أَفْرِغْ‌ عَلَيْنَا صَبْراً وَ تَوَفَّنَا مُسْلِمِينَ‌
>
> You are vindictive toward us only because we have believed in the signs of our Lord, when they came to us.’ ‘Our Lord! Pour patience upon us, and grant us to die as Muslims.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:128
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/128
> قَالَ‌ مُوسَى‌ لِقَوْمِهِ‌ اسْتَعِينُوا بِاللَّهِ‌ وَ اصْبِرُوا إِنَ‌ الْأَرْضَ‌ لِلَّهِ‌ يُورِثُهَا مَنْ‌ يَشَاءُ مِنْ‌ عِبَادِهِ‌ وَ الْعَاقِبَةُ لِلْمُتَّقِينَ‌
>
> Moses said to his people, ‘Turn to Allah for help and be patient. The earth indeed belongs to Allah, and He gives its inheritance to whomever He wishes of His servants, and the outcome will be in favour of the Godwary.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:137
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/137
> وَ أَوْرَثْنَا الْقَوْمَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ کَانُوا يُسْتَضْعَفُونَ‌ مَشَارِقَ‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ مَغَارِبَهَا الَّتِي‌ بَارَکْنَا فِيهَا وَ تَمَّتْ‌ کَلِمَةُ رَبِّکَ‌ الْحُسْنَى‌ عَلَى‌ بَنِي‌ إِسْرَائِيلَ‌ بِمَا صَبَرُوا وَ دَمَّرْنَا مَا کَانَ‌ يَصْنَعُ‌ فِرْعَوْنُ‌ وَ قَوْمُهُ‌ وَ مَا کَانُوا يَعْرِشُونَ‌
>
> We made the people who were abased the heirs to the east and west of the land which We had blessed, and your Lord’s best word [of promise] was fulfilled for the Children of Israel because of their patience, and We destroyed what Pharaoh and his people had built and what they used to erect.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:144
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/144
> قَالَ‌ يَا مُوسَى‌ إِنِّي‌ اصْطَفَيْتُکَ‌ عَلَى‌ النَّاسِ‌ بِرِسَالاَتِي‌ وَ بِکَلاَمِي‌ فَخُذْ مَا آتَيْتُکَ‌ وَ کُنْ‌ مِنَ‌ الشَّاکِرِينَ‌
>
> He said, ‘O Moses, I have chosen you over the people with My messages and My speech. So take what I give you, and be among the grateful.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:149
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/149
> وَ لَمَّا سُقِطَ فِي‌ أَيْدِيهِمْ‌ وَ رَأَوْا أَنَّهُمْ‌ قَدْ ضَلُّوا قَالُوا لَئِنْ‌ لَمْ‌ يَرْحَمْنَا رَبُّنَا وَ يَغْفِرْ لَنَا لَنَکُونَنَ‌ مِنَ‌ الْخَاسِرِينَ‌
>
> But when they became remorseful and realised they had gone astray, they said, ‘Should our Lord have no mercy on us, and forgive us, we will be surely among the losers.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:150
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/150
> وَ لَمَّا رَجَعَ‌ مُوسَى‌ إِلَى‌ قَوْمِهِ‌ غَضْبَانَ‌ أَسِفاً قَالَ‌ بِئْسَمَا خَلَفْتُمُونِي‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِي‌ أَ عَجِلْتُمْ‌ أَمْرَ رَبِّکُمْ‌ وَ أَلْقَى‌ الْأَلْوَاحَ‌ وَ أَخَذَ بِرَأْسِ‌ أَخِيهِ‌ يَجُرُّهُ‌ إِلَيْهِ‌ قَالَ‌ ابْنَ‌ أُمَ‌ إِنَ‌ الْقَوْمَ‌ اسْتَضْعَفُونِي‌ وَ کَادُوا يَقْتُلُونَنِي‌ فَلاَ تُشْمِتْ‌ بِيَ‌ الْأَعْدَاءَ وَ لاَ تَجْعَلْنِي‌ مَعَ‌ الْقَوْمِ‌ الظَّالِمِينَ‌
>
> When Moses returned to his people, angry and indignant, he said, ‘Evil has been your conduct in my absence! Would you hasten on the edict of your Lord?’ He threw down the tablets and seized his brother by the head, pulling him towards himself. He said, ‘Son of my mother, indeed this people thought me to be weak, and they were about to kill me. So do not let the enemies gloat over me, and do not take me with the wrongdoing lot.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:151
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/151
> قَالَ‌ رَبِ‌ اغْفِرْ لِي‌ وَ لِأَخِي‌ وَ أَدْخِلْنَا فِي‌ رَحْمَتِکَ‌ وَ أَنْتَ‌ أَرْحَمُ‌ الرَّاحِمِينَ‌
>
> He said, ‘My Lord, forgive me and my brother, and admit us into Your mercy, for You are the most merciful of the merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:153
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/153
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ عَمِلُوا السَّيِّئَاتِ‌ ثُمَ‌ تَابُوا مِنْ‌ بَعْدِهَا وَ آمَنُوا إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِهَا لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Yet [to] those who commit misdeeds but repent after that, and believe—indeed, after that, your Lord shall surely be all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:155
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/155
> وَ اخْتَارَ مُوسَى‌ قَوْمَهُ‌ سَبْعِينَ‌ رَجُلاً لِمِيقَاتِنَا فَلَمَّا أَخَذَتْهُمُ‌ الرَّجْفَةُ قَالَ‌ رَبِ‌ لَوْ شِئْتَ‌ أَهْلَکْتَهُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ قَبْلُ‌ وَ إِيَّايَ‌ أَ تُهْلِکُنَا بِمَا فَعَلَ‌ السُّفَهَاءُ مِنَّا إِنْ‌ هِيَ‌ إِلاَّ فِتْنَتُکَ‌ تُضِلُ‌ بِهَا مَنْ‌ تَشَاءُ وَ تَهْدِي‌ مَنْ‌ تَشَاءُ أَنْتَ‌ وَلِيُّنَا فَاغْفِرْ لَنَا وَ ارْحَمْنَا وَ أَنْتَ‌ خَيْرُ الْغَافِرِينَ‌
>
> Moses chose seventy men from his people for Our tryst, and when the earthquake seized them, he said, ‘My Lord, had You wished, You would have destroyed them and me before. Will You destroy us because of what the fools amongst us have done? It is only Your test, by which You lead astray whomever You wish and guide whomever You wish. You are our master, so forgive us and have mercy on us, for You are the best of those who forgive.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:161
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/161
> وَ إِذْ قِيلَ‌ لَهُمُ‌ اسْکُنُوا هٰذِهِ‌ الْقَرْيَةَ وَ کُلُوا مِنْهَا حَيْثُ‌ شِئْتُمْ‌ وَ قُولُوا حِطَّةٌ وَ ادْخُلُوا الْبَابَ‌ سُجَّداً نَغْفِرْ لَکُمْ‌ خَطِيئَاتِکُمْ‌ سَنَزِيدُ الْمُحْسِنِينَ‌
>
> And when they were told, ‘Settle in this town and eat thereof whence you wish; and say, ‘‘Relieve [us of the burden of our sins],’’ and enter prostrating at the gate, that We may forgive your iniquities, and soon We shall enhance the virtuous.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:164
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/164
> وَ إِذْ قَالَتْ‌ أُمَّةٌ مِنْهُمْ‌ لِمَ‌ تَعِظُونَ‌ قَوْماً اللَّهُ‌ مُهْلِکُهُمْ‌ أَوْ مُعَذِّبُهُمْ‌ عَذَاباً شَدِيداً قَالُوا مَعْذِرَةً إِلَى‌ رَبِّکُمْ‌ وَ لَعَلَّهُمْ‌ يَتَّقُونَ‌
>
> When a group of them said, ‘Why do you advise a people whom Allah will destroy, or punish with a severe punishment?’ They said, ‘As an excuse before your Lord, and [with the hope] that they may be Godwary.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:167
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/167
> وَ إِذْ تَأَذَّنَ‌ رَبُّکَ‌ لَيَبْعَثَنَ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ إِلَى‌ يَوْمِ‌ الْقِيَامَةِ مَنْ‌ يَسُومُهُمْ‌ سُوءَ الْعَذَابِ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ لَسَرِيعُ‌ الْعِقَابِ‌ وَ إِنَّهُ‌ لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> And when your Lord proclaimed that He would surely send against them, until the Day of Resurrection, those who would inflict on them a terrible punishment. Indeed your Lord is swift in retribution, and indeed, He is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:169
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/169
> فَخَلَفَ‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِهِمْ‌ خَلْفٌ‌ وَرِثُوا الْکِتَابَ‌ يَأْخُذُونَ‌ عَرَضَ‌ هٰذَا الْأَدْنَى‌ وَ يَقُولُونَ‌ سَيُغْفَرُ لَنَا وَ إِنْ‌ يَأْتِهِمْ‌ عَرَضٌ‌ مِثْلُهُ‌ يَأْخُذُوهُ‌ أَ لَمْ‌ يُؤْخَذْ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ مِيثَاقُ‌ الْکِتَابِ‌ أَنْ‌ لاَ يَقُولُوا عَلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ إِلاَّ الْحَقَ‌ وَ دَرَسُوا مَا فِيهِ‌ وَ الدَّارُ الْآخِرَةُ خَيْرٌ لِلَّذِينَ‌ يَتَّقُونَ‌ أَ فَلاَ تَعْقِلُونَ‌
>
> Then they were succeeded by an evil posterity, which inherited the Book: they grab the transitory gains of this lower world, and say, ‘It will be forgiven us.’ And if similar transitory gains were to come their way, they would grab them too. Was not the covenant of the Book taken with them that they shall not attribute anything to Allah except the truth? They have studied what is in it, and [know that] the abode of the Hereafter is better for those who are Godwary. Do you not exercise your reason?

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:170
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/170
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ يُمَسِّکُونَ‌ بِالْکِتَابِ‌ وَ أَقَامُوا الصَّلاَةَ إِنَّا لاَ نُضِيعُ‌ أَجْرَ الْمُصْلِحِينَ‌
>
> As for those who hold fast to the Book and maintain the prayer—indeed, We do not waste the reward of those who bring about reform.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:176
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/176
> وَ لَوْ شِئْنَا لَرَفَعْنَاهُ‌ بِهَا وَ لٰکِنَّهُ‌ أَخْلَدَ إِلَى‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ اتَّبَعَ‌ هَوَاهُ‌ فَمَثَلُهُ‌ کَمَثَلِ‌ الْکَلْبِ‌ إِنْ‌ تَحْمِلْ‌ عَلَيْهِ‌ يَلْهَثْ‌ أَوْ تَتْرُکْهُ‌ يَلْهَثْ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ مَثَلُ‌ الْقَوْمِ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ کَذَّبُوا بِآيَاتِنَا فَاقْصُصِ‌ الْقَصَصَ‌ لَعَلَّهُمْ‌ يَتَفَکَّرُونَ‌
>
> Had We wished, We would have surely raised him by their means, but he clung to the earth and followed his [base] desires. So his parable is that of a dog: if you make for it, it lolls out its tongue, and if you let it alone, it lolls out its tongue. Such is the parable of the people who deny Our signs. So recount these narratives, so that they may reflect.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:180
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/180
> وَ لِلَّهِ‌ الْأَسْمَاءُ الْحُسْنَى‌ فَادْعُوهُ‌ بِهَا وَ ذَرُوا الَّذِينَ‌ يُلْحِدُونَ‌ فِي‌ أَسْمَائِهِ‌ سَيُجْزَوْنَ‌ مَا کَانُوا يَعْمَلُونَ‌
>
> To Allah belong the Best Names, so supplicate Him by them, and abandon those who commit sacrilege in His names. Soon they shall be requited for what they used to do.

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:189
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/7/189
> هُوَ الَّذِي‌ خَلَقَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ نَفْسٍ‌ وَاحِدَةٍ وَ جَعَلَ‌ مِنْهَا زَوْجَهَا لِيَسْکُنَ‌ إِلَيْهَا فَلَمَّا تَغَشَّاهَا حَمَلَتْ‌ حَمْلاً خَفِيفاً فَمَرَّتْ‌ بِهِ‌ فَلَمَّا أَثْقَلَتْ‌ دَعَوَا اللَّهَ‌ رَبَّهُمَا لَئِنْ‌ آتَيْتَنَا صَالِحاً لَنَکُونَنَ‌ مِنَ‌ الشَّاکِرِينَ‌
>
> It is He who created you from a single soul, and made from it its mate, that he might find comfort with her. So when he had covered her, she bore a light burden and passed [some time] with it. When she had grown heavy, they both invoked Allah, their Lord: ‘If You give us a healthy [child], we will be surely grateful.’

### Qur'an · Al-A'raaf (The Heights) · 7:200

>
> Should a temptation from Satan disturb you, invoke the protection of Allah; indeed He is all-hearing, all-knowing.
