>
> maintain the prayer and spend out of what We have provided them.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:4
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/4
> أُولٰئِکَ‌ هُمُ‌ الْمُؤْمِنُونَ‌ حَقّاً لَهُمْ‌ دَرَجَاتٌ‌ عِنْدَ رَبِّهِمْ‌ وَ مَغْفِرَةٌ وَ رِزْقٌ‌ کَرِيمٌ‌
>
> It is they who are truly the faithful. They shall have ranks near their Lord, forgiveness and a noble provision.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:7
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/7
> وَ إِذْ يَعِدُکُمُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ إِحْدَى‌ الطَّائِفَتَيْنِ‌ أَنَّهَا لَکُمْ‌ وَ تَوَدُّونَ‌ أَنَ‌ غَيْرَ ذَاتِ‌ الشَّوْکَةِ تَکُونُ‌ لَکُمْ‌ وَ يُرِيدُ اللَّهُ‌ أَنْ‌ يُحِقَ‌ الْحَقَ‌ بِکَلِمَاتِهِ‌ وَ يَقْطَعَ‌ دَابِرَ الْکَافِرِينَ‌
>
> When Allah promised you [victory over] one of the two companies, [saying], ‘It is for you,’ you were eager that it should be the one that was unarmed. But Allah desires to confirm the truth with His words, and to root out the faithless,

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:26
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/26
> وَ اذْکُرُوا إِذْ أَنْتُمْ‌ قَلِيلٌ‌ مُسْتَضْعَفُونَ‌ فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ تَخَافُونَ‌ أَنْ‌ يَتَخَطَّفَکُمُ‌ النَّاسُ‌ فَآوَاکُمْ‌ وَ أَيَّدَکُمْ‌ بِنَصْرِهِ‌ وَ رَزَقَکُمْ‌ مِنَ‌ الطَّيِّبَاتِ‌ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> Remember when you were few, abased in the land, and feared lest the people should despoil you, and He gave you refuge, and strengthened you with His help, and provided you with all the good things so that you may give thanks.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:29
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/29
> يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا إِنْ‌ تَتَّقُوا اللَّهَ‌ يَجْعَلْ‌ لَکُمْ‌ فُرْقَاناً وَ يُکَفِّرْ عَنْکُمْ‌ سَيِّئَاتِکُمْ‌ وَ يَغْفِرْ لَکُمْ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ ذُو الْفَضْلِ‌ الْعَظِيمِ‌
>
> O you who have faith! If you are wary of Allah, He shall appoint a criterion for you, and absolve you of your misdeeds, and forgive you, for Allah is dispenser of a great grace.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:33
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/33
> وَ مَا کَانَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ لِيُعَذِّبَهُمْ‌ وَ أَنْتَ‌ فِيهِمْ‌ وَ مَا کَانَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ مُعَذِّبَهُمْ‌ وَ هُمْ‌ يَسْتَغْفِرُونَ‌
>
> But Allah will not punish them while you are in their midst, nor will Allah punish them while they plead for forgiveness.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:35
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/35
> وَ مَا کَانَ‌ صَلاَتُهُمْ‌ عِنْدَ الْبَيْتِ‌ إِلاَّ مُکَاءً وَ تَصْدِيَةً فَذُوقُوا الْعَذَابَ‌ بِمَا کُنْتُمْ‌ تَکْفُرُونَ‌
>
> Their prayer at the House is nothing but whistling and clapping. So taste the punishment because of what you used to defy.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:36
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/36
> إِنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا يُنْفِقُونَ‌ أَمْوَالَهُمْ‌ لِيَصُدُّوا عَنْ‌ سَبِيلِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ فَسَيُنْفِقُونَهَا ثُمَ‌ تَکُونُ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ حَسْرَةً ثُمَ‌ يُغْلَبُونَ‌ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا إِلَى‌ جَهَنَّمَ‌ يُحْشَرُونَ‌
>
> Indeed the faithless spend their wealth to bar from the way of Allah. Soon they will have spent it, then it will be a cause of regret to them, then they will be overcome, and the faithless will be gathered toward Hell,

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:38
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/38
> قُلْ‌ لِلَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا إِنْ‌ يَنْتَهُوا يُغْفَرْ لَهُمْ‌ مَا قَدْ سَلَفَ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ يَعُودُوا فَقَدْ مَضَتْ‌ سُنَّةُ الْأَوَّلِينَ‌
>
> Say to the faithless, if they cease [their belligerence against the Muslims], what is already past shall be forgiven them. But if they resume [their hostilities], then the precedent of the predecessors has already come to pass.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:46
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/46
> وَ أَطِيعُوا اللَّهَ‌ وَ رَسُولَهُ‌ وَ لاَ تَنَازَعُوا فَتَفْشَلُوا وَ تَذْهَبَ‌ رِيحُکُمْ‌ وَ اصْبِرُوا إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ مَعَ‌ الصَّابِرِينَ‌
>
> And obey Allah and His Apostle, and do not dispute, or you will lose heart and your power will be gone. And be patient; indeed Allah is with the patient.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:62
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/62
> وَ إِنْ‌ يُرِيدُوا أَنْ‌ يَخْدَعُوکَ‌ فَإِنَ‌ حَسْبَکَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ هُوَ الَّذِي‌ أَيَّدَکَ‌ بِنَصْرِهِ‌ وَ بِالْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌
>
> But if they desire to deceive you, Allah is indeed sufficient for you. It is He who strengthened you with His help and with the means of the faithful,

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:65
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/65
> يَا أَيُّهَا النَّبِيُ‌ حَرِّضِ‌ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌ عَلَى‌ الْقِتَالِ‌ إِنْ‌ يَکُنْ‌ مِنْکُمْ‌ عِشْرُونَ‌ صَابِرُونَ‌ يَغْلِبُوا مِائَتَيْنِ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ يَکُنْ‌ مِنْکُمْ‌ مِائَةٌ يَغْلِبُوا أَلْفاً مِنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا بِأَنَّهُمْ‌ قَوْمٌ‌ لاَ يَفْقَهُونَ‌
>
> O Prophet! Urge on the faithful to fight: If there be twenty patient men among you, they shall overcome two hundred; and if there be a hundred of you, they shall overcome a thousand of the faithless, for they are a lot who do not understand.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:66
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/66
> الْآنَ‌ خَفَّفَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَنْکُمْ‌ وَ عَلِمَ‌ أَنَ‌ فِيکُمْ‌ ضَعْفاً فَإِنْ‌ يَکُنْ‌ مِنْکُمْ‌ مِائَةٌ صَابِرَةٌ يَغْلِبُوا مِائَتَيْنِ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ يَکُنْ‌ مِنْکُمْ‌ أَلْفٌ‌ يَغْلِبُوا أَلْفَيْنِ‌ بِإِذْنِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ مَعَ‌ الصَّابِرِينَ‌
>
> Now Allah has lightened your burden, knowing that there is weakness in you. So if there be a hundred patient men among you, they shall overcome two hundred; and if there be a thousand, they shall overcome two thousand, by Allah’s leave; and Allah is with the patient.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:67
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/67
> مَا کَانَ‌ لِنَبِيٍ‌ أَنْ‌ يَکُونَ‌ لَهُ‌ أَسْرَى‌ حَتَّى‌ يُثْخِنَ‌ فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ تُرِيدُونَ‌ عَرَضَ‌ الدُّنْيَا وَ اللَّهُ‌ يُرِيدُ الْآخِرَةَ وَ اللَّهُ‌ عَزِيزٌ حَکِيمٌ‌
>
> A prophet may not take captives until he has thoroughly decimated [the enemy] in the land. You desire the transitory gains of this world, while Allah desires [for you] [the reward of] the Hereafter, and Allah is all-mighty, all-wise.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:69
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/69
> فَکُلُوا مِمَّا غَنِمْتُمْ‌ حَلاَلاً طَيِّباً وَ اتَّقُوا اللَّهَ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Avail yourselves of the spoils you have taken as lawful and good, and be wary of Allah. Indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:70
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/70
> يَا أَيُّهَا النَّبِيُ‌ قُلْ‌ لِمَنْ‌ فِي‌ أَيْدِيکُمْ‌ مِنَ‌ الْأَسْرَى‌ إِنْ‌ يَعْلَمِ‌ اللَّهُ‌ فِي‌ قُلُوبِکُمْ‌ خَيْراً يُؤْتِکُمْ‌ خَيْراً مِمَّا أُخِذَ مِنْکُمْ‌ وَ يَغْفِرْ لَکُمْ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> O Prophet! Say to the captives who are in your hands, ‘If Allah finds any good in your hearts, He will give you [something which is] better than what has been taken away from you, and He will forgive you, and Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.’

### Qur'an · Al-Anfaal (The Spoils of War) · 8:74
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/8/74
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا وَ هَاجَرُوا وَ جَاهَدُوا فِي‌ سَبِيلِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ آوَوْا وَ نَصَرُوا أُولٰئِکَ‌ هُمُ‌ الْمُؤْمِنُونَ‌ حَقّاً لَهُمْ‌ مَغْفِرَةٌ وَ رِزْقٌ‌ کَرِيمٌ‌
>
> Those who have believed, migrated, and waged jihad in the way of Allah, and those who gave them shelter and help, it is they who are truly the faithful. For them shall be forgiveness and a noble provision.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:3
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/3
> وَ أَذَانٌ‌ مِنَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ رَسُولِهِ‌ إِلَى‌ النَّاسِ‌ يَوْمَ‌ الْحَجِ‌ الْأَکْبَرِ أَنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ بَرِي‌ءٌ مِنَ‌ الْمُشْرِکِينَ‌ وَ رَسُولُهُ‌ فَإِنْ‌ تُبْتُمْ‌ فَهُوَ خَيْرٌ لَکُمْ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ تَوَلَّيْتُمْ‌ فَاعْلَمُوا أَنَّکُمْ‌ غَيْرُ مُعْجِزِي‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ بَشِّرِ الَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا بِعَذَابٍ‌ أَلِيمٍ‌
>
> [This is] an announcement from Allah and His Apostle to all the people on the day of the greater Hajj: that Allah and His Apostle repudiate the polytheists: If you repent that is better for you; but if you turn your backs [on Allah], know that you cannot frustrate Allah, and inform the faithless of a painful punishment

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:5
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/5
> فَإِذَا انْسَلَخَ‌ الْأَشْهُرُ الْحُرُمُ‌ فَاقْتُلُوا الْمُشْرِکِينَ‌ حَيْثُ‌ وَجَدْتُمُوهُمْ‌ وَ خُذُوهُمْ‌ وَ احْصُرُوهُمْ‌ وَ اقْعُدُوا لَهُمْ‌ کُلَ‌ مَرْصَدٍ فَإِنْ‌ تَابُوا وَ أَقَامُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ آتَوُا الزَّکَاةَ فَخَلُّوا سَبِيلَهُمْ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Then, when the sacred months have passed, kill the polytheists wherever you find them, capture them and besiege them, and lie in wait for them at every ambush. But if they repent, and maintain the prayer and give the zakat, then let them alone. Indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:7
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/7
> کَيْفَ‌ يَکُونُ‌ لِلْمُشْرِکِينَ‌ عَهْدٌ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ‌ وَ عِنْدَ رَسُولِهِ‌ إِلاَّ الَّذِينَ‌ عَاهَدْتُمْ‌ عِنْدَ الْمَسْجِدِ الْحَرَامِ‌ فَمَا اسْتَقَامُوا لَکُمْ‌ فَاسْتَقِيمُوا لَهُمْ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ يُحِبُ‌ الْمُتَّقِينَ‌
>
> How shall the polytheists have any [valid] treaty with Allah and His Apostle?! (Barring those with whom you made a treaty at the Holy Mosque; so long as they are steadfast with you, be steadfast with them. Indeed Allah loves the Godwary.)

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:11
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/11
> فَإِنْ‌ تَابُوا وَ أَقَامُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ آتَوُا الزَّکَاةَ فَإِخْوَانُکُمْ‌ فِي‌ الدِّينِ‌ وَ نُفَصِّلُ‌ الْآيَاتِ‌ لِقَوْمٍ‌ يَعْلَمُونَ‌
>
> Yet if they repent and maintain the prayer and give the zakat, then they are your brethren in faith. We elaborate the signs for a people who have knowledge.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:18
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/18
> إِنَّمَا يَعْمُرُ مَسَاجِدَ اللَّهِ‌ مَنْ‌ آمَنَ‌ بِاللَّهِ‌ وَ الْيَوْمِ‌ الْآخِرِ وَ أَقَامَ‌ الصَّلاَةَ وَ آتَى‌ الزَّکَاةَ وَ لَمْ‌ يَخْشَ‌ إِلاَّ اللَّهَ‌ فَعَسَى‌ أُولٰئِکَ‌ أَنْ‌ يَکُونُوا مِنَ‌ الْمُهْتَدِينَ‌
>
> Only those shall maintain Allah’s mosques who believe in Allah and the Last Day, and maintain the prayer, and give the zakat, and fear no one except Allah. They, hopefully, will be among the guided.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:27
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/27
> ثُمَ‌ يَتُوبُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِ ذٰلِکَ‌ عَلَى‌ مَنْ‌ يَشَاءُ وَ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Then Allah shall turn clemently after that to whomever He wishes. Indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:32
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/32
> يُرِيدُونَ‌ أَنْ‌ يُطْفِئُوا نُورَ اللَّهِ‌ بِأَفْوَاهِهِمْ‌ وَ يَأْبَى‌ اللَّهُ‌ إِلاَّ أَنْ‌ يُتِمَ‌ نُورَهُ‌ وَ لَوْ کَرِهَ‌ الْکَافِرُونَ‌
>
> They desire to put out the light of Allah with their mouths, but Allah is intent on perfecting His light though the faithless should be averse.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:49
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/49
> وَ مِنْهُمْ‌ مَنْ‌ يَقُولُ‌ ائْذَنْ‌ لِي‌ وَ لاَ تَفْتِنِّي‌ أَلاَ فِي‌ الْفِتْنَةِ سَقَطُوا وَ إِنَ‌ جَهَنَّمَ‌ لَمُحِيطَةٌ بِالْکَافِرِينَ‌
>
> Among them there are some who say, ‘Give me leave, and do not put me to temptation.’ Look! They have already fallen into temptation and indeed hell besieges the faithless.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:54
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/54
> وَ مَا مَنَعَهُمْ‌ أَنْ‌ تُقْبَلَ‌ مِنْهُمْ‌ نَفَقَاتُهُمْ‌ إِلاَّ أَنَّهُمْ‌ کَفَرُوا بِاللَّهِ‌ وَ بِرَسُولِهِ‌ وَ لاَ يَأْتُونَ‌ الصَّلاَةَ إِلاَّ وَ هُمْ‌ کُسَالَى‌ وَ لاَ يُنْفِقُونَ‌ إِلاَّ وَ هُمْ‌ کَارِهُونَ‌
>
> Nothing stops their charities from being accepted except that they have no faith in Allah and His Apostle and do not perform the prayer but lazily, and do not spend but reluctantly.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:55
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/55
> فَلاَ تُعْجِبْکَ‌ أَمْوَالُهُمْ‌ وَ لاَ أَوْلاَدُهُمْ‌ إِنَّمَا يُرِيدُ اللَّهُ‌ لِيُعَذِّبَهُمْ‌ بِهَا فِي‌ الْحَيَاةِ الدُّنْيَا وَ تَزْهَقَ‌ أَنْفُسُهُمْ‌ وَ هُمْ‌ کَافِرُونَ‌
>
> So let not their wealth and children impress you: Allah only desires to punish them with these in the life of this world, and that their souls may depart while they are faithless.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:66
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/66
> لاَ تَعْتَذِرُوا قَدْ کَفَرْتُمْ‌ بَعْدَ إِيمَانِکُمْ‌ إِنْ‌ نَعْفُ‌ عَنْ‌ طَائِفَةٍ مِنْکُمْ‌ نُعَذِّبْ‌ طَائِفَةً بِأَنَّهُمْ‌ کَانُوا مُجْرِمِينَ‌
>
> Do not make excuses. You have disbelieved after your faith.’ If We forgive a group among you, We will punish another group, for they have been guilty.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:71
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/71
> وَ الْمُؤْمِنُونَ‌ وَ الْمُؤْمِنَاتُ‌ بَعْضُهُمْ‌ أَوْلِيَاءُ بَعْضٍ‌ يَأْمُرُونَ‌ بِالْمَعْرُوفِ‌ وَ يَنْهَوْنَ‌ عَنِ‌ الْمُنْکَرِ وَ يُقِيمُونَ‌ الصَّلاَةَ وَ يُؤْتُونَ‌ الزَّکَاةَ وَ يُطِيعُونَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ وَ رَسُولَهُ‌ أُولٰئِکَ‌ سَيَرْحَمُهُمُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ عَزِيزٌ حَکِيمٌ‌
>
> But the faithful, men and women, are comrades of one another: they bid what is right and forbid what is wrong and maintain the prayer, give the zakat, and obey Allah and His Apostle. It is they to whom Allah will soon grant His mercy. Indeed Allah is all-mighty, all-wise.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:74
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/74
> يَحْلِفُونَ‌ بِاللَّهِ‌ مَا قَالُوا وَ لَقَدْ قَالُوا کَلِمَةَ الْکُفْرِ وَ کَفَرُوا بَعْدَ إِسْلاَمِهِمْ‌ وَ هَمُّوا بِمَا لَمْ‌ يَنَالُوا وَ مَا نَقَمُوا إِلاَّ أَنْ‌ أَغْنَاهُمُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ وَ رَسُولُهُ‌ مِنْ‌ فَضْلِهِ‌ فَإِنْ‌ يَتُوبُوا يَکُ‌ خَيْراً لَهُمْ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ يَتَوَلَّوْا يُعَذِّبْهُمُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَذَاباً أَلِيماً فِي‌ الدُّنْيَا وَ الْآخِرَةِ وَ مَا لَهُمْ‌ فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ مِنْ‌ وَلِيٍ‌ وَ لاَ نَصِيرٍ
>
> They swear by Allah that they did not say it. But they certainly did utter the word of unfaith and renounced faith after their Islam. They contemplated what they could not achieve, and they were vindictive only because Allah and His Apostle had enriched them out of His grace. Yet if they repent, it will be better for them; but if they turn away, Allah shall punish them with a painful punishment in this world and the Hereafter, and they shall not find on the earth any guardian or helper.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:80
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/80
> اسْتَغْفِرْ لَهُمْ‌ أَوْ لاَ تَسْتَغْفِرْ لَهُمْ‌ إِنْ‌ تَسْتَغْفِرْ لَهُمْ‌ سَبْعِينَ‌ مَرَّةً فَلَنْ‌ يَغْفِرَ اللَّهُ‌ لَهُمْ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ بِأَنَّهُمْ‌ کَفَرُوا بِاللَّهِ‌ وَ رَسُولِهِ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ لاَ يَهْدِي‌ الْقَوْمَ‌ الْفَاسِقِينَ‌
>
> Whether you plead forgiveness for them or do not plead forgiveness for them, even if you plead forgiveness for them seventy times, Allah shall never forgive them because they defied Allah and His Apostle; and Allah does not guide the transgressing lot.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:84
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/84
> وَ لاَ تُصَلِ‌ عَلَى‌ أَحَدٍ مِنْهُمْ‌ مَاتَ‌ أَبَداً وَ لاَ تَقُمْ‌ عَلَى‌ قَبْرِهِ‌ إِنَّهُمْ‌ کَفَرُوا بِاللَّهِ‌ وَ رَسُولِهِ‌ وَ مَاتُوا وَ هُمْ‌ فَاسِقُونَ‌
>
> And never pray over any of them when he dies, nor stand at his graveside. They indeed defied Allah and His Apostle and died as transgressors.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:85
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/85
> وَ لاَ تُعْجِبْکَ‌ أَمْوَالُهُمْ‌ وَ أَوْلاَدُهُمْ‌ إِنَّمَا يُرِيدُ اللَّهُ‌ أَنْ‌ يُعَذِّبَهُمْ‌ بِهَا فِي‌ الدُّنْيَا وَ تَزْهَقَ‌ أَنْفُسُهُمْ‌ وَ هُمْ‌ کَافِرُونَ‌
>
> Let not their possessions or their children impress you. Allah only desires to punish them with these in this world, and that their souls may depart while they are faithless.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:91
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/91
> لَيْسَ‌ عَلَى‌ الضُّعَفَاءِ وَ لاَ عَلَى‌ الْمَرْضَى‌ وَ لاَ عَلَى‌ الَّذِينَ‌ لاَ يَجِدُونَ‌ مَا يُنْفِقُونَ‌ حَرَجٌ‌ إِذَا نَصَحُوا لِلَّهِ‌ وَ رَسُولِهِ‌ مَا عَلَى‌ الْمُحْسِنِينَ‌ مِنْ‌ سَبِيلٍ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> There is no blame on the weak, nor on the sick, nor on those who do not find anything to spend, so long as they are sincere to Allah and His Apostle. There is no [cause for] blaming the virtuous, and Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:99
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/99
> وَ مِنَ‌ الْأَعْرَابِ‌ مَنْ‌ يُؤْمِنُ‌ بِاللَّهِ‌ وَ الْيَوْمِ‌ الْآخِرِ وَ يَتَّخِذُ مَا يُنْفِقُ‌ قُرُبَاتٍ‌ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ‌ وَ صَلَوَاتِ‌ الرَّسُولِ‌ أَلاَ إِنَّهَا قُرْبَةٌ لَهُمْ‌ سَيُدْخِلُهُمُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ فِي‌ رَحْمَتِهِ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Yet among the Bedouins are [also] those who believe in Allah and the Last Day, and regard what they spend as [a means of attaining] nearness to Allah and the blessings of the Apostle. Now, it shall indeed bring them nearness, and Allah will admit them into His mercy. Indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:102
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/102
> وَ آخَرُونَ‌ اعْتَرَفُوا بِذُنُوبِهِمْ‌ خَلَطُوا عَمَلاً صَالِحاً وَ آخَرَ سَيِّئاً عَسَى‌ اللَّهُ‌ أَنْ‌ يَتُوبَ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> [There are] others who have confessed to their sins, having mixed up righteous conduct with other that was evil. Maybe Allah will accept their repentance. Indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:104
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/104
> أَ لَمْ‌ يَعْلَمُوا أَنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ هُوَ يَقْبَلُ‌ التَّوْبَةَ عَنْ‌ عِبَادِهِ‌ وَ يَأْخُذُ الصَّدَقَاتِ‌ وَ أَنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ هُوَ التَّوَّابُ‌ الرَّحِيمُ‌
>
> Do they not know that it is Allah who accepts the repentance of His servants and receives the charities, and that it is Allah who is the All-clement, the All-merciful?

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:108
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/108
> لاَ تَقُمْ‌ فِيهِ‌ أَبَداً لَمَسْجِدٌ أُسِّسَ‌ عَلَى‌ التَّقْوَى‌ مِنْ‌ أَوَّلِ‌ يَوْمٍ‌ أَحَقُ‌ أَنْ‌ تَقُومَ‌ فِيهِ‌ فِيهِ‌ رِجَالٌ‌ يُحِبُّونَ‌ أَنْ‌ يَتَطَهَّرُوا وَ اللَّهُ‌ يُحِبُ‌ الْمُطَّهِّرِينَ‌
>
> Do not stand in it ever! A mosque founded on Godwariness from the [very] first day is worthier that you stand in it [for prayer]. Therein are men who love to keep pure, and Allah loves those who keep pure.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:112
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/112
> التَّائِبُونَ‌ الْعَابِدُونَ‌ الْحَامِدُونَ‌ السَّائِحُونَ‌ الرَّاکِعُونَ‌ السَّاجِدُونَ‌ الْآمِرُونَ‌ بِالْمَعْرُوفِ‌ وَ النَّاهُونَ‌ عَنِ‌ الْمُنْکَرِ وَ الْحَافِظُونَ‌ لِحُدُودِ اللَّهِ‌ وَ بَشِّرِ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌
>
> [The faithful are] penitent, devout, celebrators of Allah’s praise, wayfarers, who bow [and] prostrate [in prayer], bid what is right and forbid what is wrong, and keep Allah’s bounds—and give good news to the faithful.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:113
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/113
> مَا کَانَ‌ لِلنَّبِيِ‌ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا أَنْ‌ يَسْتَغْفِرُوا لِلْمُشْرِکِينَ‌ وَ لَوْ کَانُوا أُولِي‌ قُرْبَى‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِ مَا تَبَيَّنَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ أَنَّهُمْ‌ أَصْحَابُ‌ الْجَحِيمِ‌
>
> The Prophet and the faithful may not plead for the forgiveness of the polytheists, even if they should be [their] relatives, after it has become clear to them that they will be the inmates of hell.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:114
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/114
> وَ مَا کَانَ‌ اسْتِغْفَارُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ‌ لِأَبِيهِ‌ إِلاَّ عَنْ‌ مَوْعِدَةٍ وَعَدَهَا إِيَّاهُ‌ فَلَمَّا تَبَيَّنَ‌ لَهُ‌ أَنَّهُ‌ عَدُوٌّ لِلَّهِ‌ تَبَرَّأَ مِنْهُ‌ إِنَ‌ إِبْرَاهِيمَ‌ لَأَوَّاهٌ‌ حَلِيمٌ‌
>
> Abraham’s pleading forgiveness for his father was only to fulfill a promise he had made him. So when it became manifest to him that he was an enemy of God, he repudiated him. Indeed Abraham was most plaintive and forbearing.

### Qur'an · At-Tawba (The Repentance) · 9:126
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/9/126
> أَ وَ لاَ يَرَوْنَ‌ أَنَّهُمْ‌ يُفْتَنُونَ‌ فِي‌ کُلِ‌ عَامٍ‌ مَرَّةً أَوْ مَرَّتَيْنِ‌ ثُمَ‌ لاَ يَتُوبُونَ‌ وَ لاَ هُمْ‌ يَذَّکَّرُونَ‌
>
> Do they not see that they are tried once or twice every year? Yet they neither repent, nor do they take admonition.

### Qur'an · Yunus (Jonas) · 10:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/10/12
> وَ إِذَا مَسَ‌ الْإِنْسَانَ‌ الضُّرُّ دَعَانَا لِجَنْبِهِ‌ أَوْ قَاعِداً أَوْ قَائِماً فَلَمَّا کَشَفْنَا عَنْهُ‌ ضُرَّهُ‌ مَرَّ کَأَنْ‌ لَمْ‌ يَدْعُنَا إِلَى‌ ضُرٍّ مَسَّهُ‌ کَذٰلِکَ‌ زُيِّنَ‌ لِلْمُسْرِفِينَ‌ مَا کَانُوا يَعْمَلُونَ‌
>
> When distress befalls man, he supplicates Us, [lying] on his side, sitting, or standing; but when We remove his distress, he passes on as if he had never supplicated Us concerning the distress that had befallen him. What they have been doing is thus presented as decorous to the transgressors.

### Qur'an · Yunus (Jonas) · 10:22
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/10/22
> هُوَ الَّذِي‌ يُسَيِّرُکُمْ‌ فِي‌ الْبَرِّ وَ الْبَحْرِ حَتَّى‌ إِذَا کُنْتُمْ‌ فِي‌ الْفُلْکِ‌ وَ جَرَيْنَ‌ بِهِمْ‌ بِرِيحٍ‌ طَيِّبَةٍ وَ فَرِحُوا بِهَا جَاءَتْهَا رِيحٌ‌ عَاصِفٌ‌ وَ جَاءَهُمُ‌ الْمَوْجُ‌ مِنْ‌ کُلِ‌ مَکَانٍ‌ وَ ظَنُّوا أَنَّهُمْ‌ أُحِيطَ بِهِمْ‌ دَعَوُا اللَّهَ‌ مُخْلِصِينَ‌ لَهُ‌ الدِّينَ‌ لَئِنْ‌ أَنْجَيْتَنَا مِنْ‌ هٰذِهِ‌ لَنَکُونَنَ‌ مِنَ‌ الشَّاکِرِينَ‌
>
> It is He who carries you across land and sea. When you are in the ships, and they sail along with them with a favourable wind, exulting in it, there comes upon them a tempestuous wind and waves assail them from every side, and they think that they are besieged, they invoke Allah putting exclusive faith in Him, ‘If You deliver us from this, we will surely be among the grateful.’

### Qur'an · Yunus (Jonas) · 10:60
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/10/60
> وَ مَا ظَنُ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ يَفْتَرُونَ‌ عَلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ الْکَذِبَ‌ يَوْمَ‌ الْقِيَامَةِ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لَذُو فَضْلٍ‌ عَلَى‌ النَّاسِ‌ وَ لٰکِنَ‌ أَکْثَرَهُمْ‌ لاَ يَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> What is the idea of those who fabricate lies against Allah [concerning their situation] on the Day of Resurrection? Indeed Allah is gracious to mankind, but most of them do not give thanks.

### Qur'an · Yunus (Jonas) · 10:87
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/10/87
> وَ أَوْحَيْنَا إِلَى‌ مُوسَى‌ وَ أَخِيهِ‌ أَنْ‌ تَبَوَّءَا لِقَوْمِکُمَا بِمِصْرَ بُيُوتاً وَ اجْعَلُوا بُيُوتَکُمْ‌ قِبْلَةً وَ أَقِيمُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ بَشِّرِ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌
>
> We revealed to Moses and his brother [saying], ‘Settle your people in the city, and let your houses face each other, and maintain the prayer, and give good news to the faithful.’

### Qur'an · Yunus (Jonas) · 10:89
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/10/89
> قَالَ‌ قَدْ أُجِيبَتْ‌ دَعْوَتُکُمَا فَاسْتَقِيمَا وَ لاَ تَتَّبِعَانِ‌ سَبِيلَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ لاَ يَعْلَمُونَ‌
>
> Said He, ‘Your supplication has already been granted. So be steadfast, and do not follow the way of those who do not know.’

### Qur'an · Yunus (Jonas) · 10:107
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance, self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/10/107
> وَ إِنْ‌ يَمْسَسْکَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ بِضُرٍّ فَلاَ کَاشِفَ‌ لَهُ‌ إِلاَّ هُوَ وَ إِنْ‌ يُرِدْکَ‌ بِخَيْرٍ فَلاَ رَادَّ لِفَضْلِهِ‌ يُصِيبُ‌ بِهِ‌ مَنْ‌ يَشَاءُ مِنْ‌ عِبَادِهِ‌ وَ هُوَ الْغَفُورُ الرَّحِيمُ‌
>
> Should Allah visit you with some distress, there is no one to remove it except Him; and should He desire any good for you, none can stand in the way of His grace: He grants it to whomever He wishes of His servants, and He is the All-forgiving, the All-merciful.

### Qur'an · Yunus (Jonas) · 10:109
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/10/109
> وَ اتَّبِعْ‌ مَا يُوحَى‌ إِلَيْکَ‌ وَ اصْبِرْ حَتَّى‌ يَحْکُمَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ وَ هُوَ خَيْرُ الْحَاکِمِينَ‌
>
> And follow that which is revealed to you, and be patient until Allah issues [His] judgement, and He is the best of judges.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:3
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/3
> وَ أَنِ‌ اسْتَغْفِرُوا رَبَّکُمْ‌ ثُمَ‌ تُوبُوا إِلَيْهِ‌ يُمَتِّعْکُمْ‌ مَتَاعاً حَسَناً إِلَى‌ أَجَلٍ‌ مُسَمًّى‌ وَ يُؤْتِ‌ کُلَ‌ ذِي‌ فَضْلٍ‌ فَضْلَهُ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ تَوَلَّوْا فَإِنِّي‌ أَخَافُ‌ عَلَيْکُمْ‌ عَذَابَ‌ يَوْمٍ‌ کَبِيرٍ
>
> Plead with your Lord for forgiveness, then turn to Him penitently. He will provide you with a good provision for a specified term and grant His grace to every meritorious person. But if you turn your backs [on Him], indeed I fear for you the punishment of a terrible day.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:6
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/6
> وَ مَا مِنْ‌ دَابَّةٍ فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ إِلاَّ عَلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ رِزْقُهَا وَ يَعْلَمُ‌ مُسْتَقَرَّهَا وَ مُسْتَوْدَعَهَا کُلٌ‌ فِي‌ کِتَابٍ‌ مُبِينٍ‌
>
> There is no animal on the earth, but that its sustenance lies with Allah, and He knows its [enduring] abode and its temporary place of lodging. Everything is in a manifest Book.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:11
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/11
> إِلاَّ الَّذِينَ‌ صَبَرُوا وَ عَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ‌ أُولٰئِکَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ مَغْفِرَةٌ وَ أَجْرٌ کَبِيرٌ
>
> excepting those who are patient and do righteous deeds. For such there will be forgiveness and a great reward.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:15
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/15
> مَنْ‌ کَانَ‌ يُرِيدُ الْحَيَاةَ الدُّنْيَا وَ زِينَتَهَا نُوَفِ‌ إِلَيْهِمْ‌ أَعْمَالَهُمْ‌ فِيهَا وَ هُمْ‌ فِيهَا لاَ يُبْخَسُونَ‌
>
> As for those who desire the life of this world and its glitter, We will recompense them fully for their works therein, and they shall not be underpaid in it.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:34
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/34
> وَ لاَ يَنْفَعُکُمْ‌ نُصْحِي‌ إِنْ‌ أَرَدْتُ‌ أَنْ‌ أَنْصَحَ‌ لَکُمْ‌ إِنْ‌ کَانَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ يُرِيدُ أَنْ‌ يُغْوِيَکُمْ‌ هُوَ رَبُّکُمْ‌ وَ إِلَيْهِ‌ تُرْجَعُونَ‌
>
> My exhorting will not benefit you, much as I may seek to exhort you, if Allah desires to consign you to perversity. He is your Lord, and to Him you shall be brought back.’

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:41
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/41
> وَ قَالَ‌ ارْکَبُوا فِيهَا بِسْمِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ مَجْرَاهَا وَ مُرْسَاهَا إِنَ‌ رَبِّي‌ لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> He said, ‘Board it: In the Name of Allah it shall set sail and cast anchor. Indeed my Lord is all-forgiving, all-merciful.’

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:47
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/47
> قَالَ‌ رَبِ‌ إِنِّي‌ أَعُوذُ بِکَ‌ أَنْ‌ أَسْأَلَکَ‌ مَا لَيْسَ‌ لِي‌ بِهِ‌ عِلْمٌ‌ وَ إِلاَّ تَغْفِرْ لِي‌ وَ تَرْحَمْنِي‌ أَکُنْ‌ مِنَ‌ الْخَاسِرِينَ‌
>
> He said, ‘My Lord! I seek Your protection lest I should ask You something of which I have no knowledge. If You do not forgive me and have mercy upon me I shall be among the losers.’

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:49
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/49
> تِلْکَ‌ مِنْ‌ أَنْبَاءِ الْغَيْبِ‌ نُوحِيهَا إِلَيْکَ‌ مَا کُنْتَ‌ تَعْلَمُهَا أَنْتَ‌ وَ لاَ قَوْمُکَ‌ مِنْ‌ قَبْلِ‌ هٰذَا فَاصْبِرْ إِنَ‌ الْعاقِبَةَ لِلْمُتَّقِينَ‌
>
> These are accounts of the Unseen, which We reveal to you. Neither you nor your people used to know them before this. So be patient. Indeed the outcome will be in favour of the Godwary.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:52
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/52
> وَ يَا قَوْمِ‌ اسْتَغْفِرُوا رَبَّکُمْ‌ ثُمَ‌ تُوبُوا إِلَيْهِ‌ يُرْسِلِ‌ السَّمَاءَ عَلَيْکُمْ‌ مِدْرَاراً وَ يَزِدْکُمْ‌ قُوَّةً إِلَى‌ قُوَّتِکُمْ‌ وَ لاَ تَتَوَلَّوْا مُجْرِمِينَ‌
>
> ‘O my people! Plead with your Lord for forgiveness, then turn to Him penitently: He will send copious rains for you from the sky, and add power to your [present] power. So do not turn your backs [on Him] as guilty ones.’

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:61
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/61
> وَ إِلَى‌ ثَمُودَ أَخَاهُمْ‌ صَالِحاً قَالَ‌ يَا قَوْمِ‌ اعْبُدُوا اللَّهَ‌ مَا لَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ إِلٰهٍ‌ غَيْرُهُ‌ هُوَ أَنْشَأَکُمْ‌ مِنَ‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ اسْتَعْمَرَکُمْ‌ فِيهَا فَاسْتَغْفِرُوهُ‌ ثُمَ‌ تُوبُوا إِلَيْهِ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبِّي‌ قَرِيبٌ‌ مُجِيبٌ‌
>
> And to Thamud [We sent] Salih, their brother. He said, ‘O my people! Worship Allah. You have no other god besides Him. He brought you forth from the earth and made it your habitation. So plead with Him for forgiveness, then turn to Him penitently. My Lord is indeed nearmost [and] responsive.’

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:62
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/62
> قَالُوا يَا صَالِحُ‌ قَدْ کُنْتَ‌ فِينَا مَرْجُوّاً قَبْلَ‌ هٰذَا أَ تَنْهَانَا أَنْ‌ نَعْبُدَ مَا يَعْبُدُ آبَاؤُنَا وَ إِنَّنَا لَفِي‌ شَکٍ‌ مِمَّا تَدْعُونَا إِلَيْهِ‌ مُرِيبٍ‌
>
> They said, ‘O Salih! You were a source of hope to us before this. Do you forbid us to worship what our fathers have been worshiping? Indeed we have grave doubts concerning that to which you invite us.’

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:75
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/75
> إِنَ‌ إِبْرَاهِيمَ‌ لَحَلِيمٌ‌ أَوَّاهٌ‌ مُنِيبٌ‌
>
> Abraham was indeed most forbearing, plaintive, [and] penitent.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:88
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/88
> قَالَ‌ يَا قَوْمِ‌ أَ رَأَيْتُمْ‌ إِنْ‌ کُنْتُ‌ عَلَى‌ بَيِّنَةٍ مِنْ‌ رَبِّي‌ وَ رَزَقَنِي‌ مِنْهُ‌ رِزْقاً حَسَناً وَ مَا أُرِيدُ أَنْ‌ أُخَالِفَکُمْ‌ إِلَى‌ مَا أَنْهَاکُمْ‌ عَنْهُ‌ إِنْ‌ أُرِيدُ إِلاَّ الْإِصْلاَحَ‌ مَا اسْتَطَعْتُ‌ وَ مَا تَوْفِيقِي‌ إِلاَّ بِاللَّهِ‌ عَلَيْهِ‌ تَوَکَّلْتُ‌ وَ إِلَيْهِ‌ أُنِيبُ‌
>
> He said, ‘O my people! Have you considered, should I stand on a manifest proof from my Lord, who has provided me a good provision from Himself? I do not wish to oppose you by what I forbid you. I only desire to put things in order, as far as I can, and my success lies only with Allah: in Him I have put my trust, and to Him I turn penitently.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:90
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/90
> وَ اسْتَغْفِرُوا رَبَّکُمْ‌ ثُمَ‌ تُوبُوا إِلَيْهِ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبِّي‌ رَحِيمٌ‌ وَدُودٌ
>
> Plead with your Lord for forgiveness, then turn to Him penitently. My Lord is indeed all-merciful, all-affectionate.’

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:107
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/107
> خَالِدِينَ‌ فِيهَا مَا دَامَتِ‌ السَّمَاوَاتُ‌ وَ الْأَرْضُ‌ إِلاَّ مَا شَاءَ رَبُّکَ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ فَعَّالٌ‌ لِمَا يُرِيدُ
>
> They shall remain in it for as long as the heavens and the earth endure—except what your Lord may wish; indeed your Lord does whatever He desires.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:108
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/108
> وَ أَمَّا الَّذِينَ‌ سُعِدُوا فَفِي‌ الْجَنَّةِ خَالِدِينَ‌ فِيهَا مَا دَامَتِ‌ السَّمَاوَاتُ‌ وَ الْأَرْضُ‌ إِلاَّ مَا شَاءَ رَبُّکَ‌ عَطَاءً غَيْرَ مَجْذُوذٍ
>
> As for the felicitous, they will be in paradise. They will remain in it for as long as the heavens and the earth endure—except what your Lord may wish—an endless bounty.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:112
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/112
> فَاسْتَقِمْ‌ کَمَا أُمِرْتَ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ تَابَ‌ مَعَکَ‌ وَ لاَ تَطْغَوْا إِنَّهُ‌ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ‌ بَصِيرٌ
>
> So be steadfast, just as you have been commanded—[you] and whoever has turned [to Allah] with you—and do not overstep the bounds. Indeed, He watches what you do.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:114
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/114
> وَ أَقِمِ‌ الصَّلاَةَ طَرَفَيِ‌ النَّهَارِ وَ زُلَفاً مِنَ‌ اللَّيْلِ‌ إِنَ‌ الْحَسَنَاتِ‌ يُذْهِبْنَ‌ السَّيِّئَاتِ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ ذِکْرَى‌ لِلذَّاکِرِينَ‌
>
> Maintain the prayer at the two ends of the day, and during the early hours of the night. Indeed good deeds efface misdeeds. That is an admonition for the mindful.

### Qur'an · Hud (Hud) · 11:115
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/11/115
> وَ اصْبِرْ فَإِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لاَ يُضِيعُ‌ أَجْرَ الْمُحْسِنِينَ‌
>
> And be patient; indeed Allah does not waste the reward of the virtuous.

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:18
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/18
> وَ جَاءُوا عَلَى‌ قَمِيصِهِ‌ بِدَمٍ‌ کَذِبٍ‌ قَالَ‌ بَلْ‌ سَوَّلَتْ‌ لَکُمْ‌ أَنْفُسُکُمْ‌ أَمْراً فَصَبْرٌ جَمِيلٌ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ الْمُسْتَعَانُ‌ عَلَى‌ مَا تَصِفُونَ‌
>
> And they produced sham blood on his shirt. He said, ‘No, your souls have made a matter seem decorous to you. Yet patience is graceful, and Allah is my resort against what you allege.’

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:29
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/29
> يُوسُفُ‌ أَعْرِضْ‌ عَنْ‌ هٰذَا وَ اسْتَغْفِرِي‌ لِذَنْبِکِ‌ إِنَّکِ‌ کُنْتِ‌ مِنَ‌ الْخَاطِئِينَ‌
>
> Joseph, let this matter alone, and you, woman, plead for forgiveness for your sin, for you have indeed been erring.’

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:38
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/38
> وَ اتَّبَعْتُ‌ مِلَّةَ آبَائِي‌ إِبْرَاهِيمَ‌ وَ إِسْحَاقَ‌ وَ يَعْقُوبَ‌ مَا کَانَ‌ لَنَا أَنْ‌ نُشْرِکَ‌ بِاللَّهِ‌ مِنْ‌ شَيْ‌ءٍ ذٰلِکَ‌ مِنْ‌ فَضْلِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ عَلَيْنَا وَ عَلَى‌ النَّاسِ‌ وَ لٰکِنَ‌ أَکْثَرَ النَّاسِ‌ لاَ يَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> I follow the creed of my fathers, Abraham, Isaac and Jacob. It is not for us to ascribe any partner to Allah. That is by virtue of Allah’s grace upon us and upon all mankind, but most people do not give thanks.

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:53
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/53
> وَ مَا أُبَرِّئُ‌ نَفْسِي‌ إِنَ‌ النَّفْسَ‌ لَأَمَّارَةٌ بِالسُّوءِ إِلاَّ مَا رَحِمَ‌ رَبِّي‌ إِنَ‌ رَبِّي‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> ‘Yet I do not absolve my [own carnal] soul, for the [carnal] soul indeed prompts [men] to evil, except inasmuch as my Lord has mercy. Indeed my Lord is all-forgiving, all-merciful.’

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:80
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/80
> فَلَمَّا اسْتَيْأَسُوا مِنْهُ‌ خَلَصُوا نَجِيّاً قَالَ‌ کَبِيرُهُمْ‌ أَ لَمْ‌ تَعْلَمُوا أَنَ‌ أَبَاکُمْ‌ قَدْ أَخَذَ عَلَيْکُمْ‌ مَوْثِقاً مِنَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ مِنْ‌ قَبْلُ‌ مَا فَرَّطْتُمْ‌ فِي‌ يُوسُفَ‌ فَلَنْ‌ أَبْرَحَ‌ الْأَرْضَ‌ حَتَّى‌ يَأْذَنَ‌ لِي‌ أَبِي‌ أَوْ يَحْکُمَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ لِي‌ وَ هُوَ خَيْرُ الْحَاکِمِينَ‌
>
> When they had despaired of [moving] him, they withdrew to confer privately. The eldest of them said, ‘Don’t you know that your father has taken a [solemn] pledge from you by Allah, and earlier you have neglected your duty in regard to Joseph? So I will never leave this land until my father permits me, or Allah passes a judgement for me, and He is the best of judges.

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:83
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/83
> قَالَ‌ بَلْ‌ سَوَّلَتْ‌ لَکُمْ‌ أَنْفُسُکُمْ‌ أَمْراً فَصَبْرٌ جَمِيلٌ‌ عَسَى‌ اللَّهُ‌ أَنْ‌ يَأْتِيَنِي‌ بِهِمْ‌ جَمِيعاً إِنَّهُ‌ هُوَ الْعَلِيمُ‌ الْحَکِيمُ‌
>
> He said, ‘No, your souls have made a matter seem decorous to you. Yet patience is graceful. Maybe Allah will bring them all [back] to me. Indeed He is the All-knowing, the All-wise.’

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:87
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/87
> يَا بَنِيَ‌ اذْهَبُوا فَتَحَسَّسُوا مِنْ‌ يُوسُفَ‌ وَ أَخِيهِ‌ وَ لاَ تَيْأَسُوا مِنْ‌ رَوْحِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ إِنَّهُ‌ لاَ يَيْأَسُ‌ مِنْ‌ رَوْحِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ إِلاَّ الْقَوْمُ‌ الْکَافِرُونَ‌
>
> ‘Go, my sons, and look for Joseph and his brother, and do not despair of Allah’s mercy. Indeed no one despairs of Allah’s mercy except the faithless lot.’

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:90
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/90
> قَالُوا أَ إِنَّکَ‌ لَأَنْتَ‌ يُوسُفُ‌ قَالَ‌ أَنَا يُوسُفُ‌ وَ هٰذَا أَخِي‌ قَدْ مَنَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَلَيْنَا إِنَّهُ‌ مَنْ‌ يَتَّقِ‌ وَ يَصْبِرْ فَإِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لاَ يُضِيعُ‌ أَجْرَ الْمُحْسِنِينَ‌
>
> They said, ‘Are you really Joseph?!’ He said, ‘I am Joseph, and this is my brother. Certainly Allah has shown us favour. Indeed if one is Godwary and patient Allah does not waste the reward of the virtuous.’

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:92
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/92
> قَالَ‌ لاَ تَثْرِيبَ‌ عَلَيْکُمُ‌ الْيَوْمَ‌ يَغْفِرُ اللَّهُ‌ لَکُمْ‌ وَ هُوَ أَرْحَمُ‌ الرَّاحِمِينَ‌
>
> He said, ‘There shall be no reproach on you today. Allah will forgive you, and He is the most merciful of the merciful.

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:95
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/95
> قَالُوا تَاللَّهِ‌ إِنَّکَ‌ لَفِي‌ ضَلاَلِکَ‌ الْقَدِيمِ‌
>
> They said, ‘By God, you persist in your inveterate error.’

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:97
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/97
> قَالُوا يَا أَبَانَا اسْتَغْفِرْ لَنَا ذُنُوبَنَا إِنَّا کُنَّا خَاطِئِينَ‌
>
> They said, ‘Father! Plead [with Allah] for forgiveness of our sins! We have indeed been erring.’

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:98
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/98
> قَالَ‌ سَوْفَ‌ أَسْتَغْفِرُ لَکُمْ‌ رَبِّي‌ إِنَّهُ‌ هُوَ الْغَفُورُ الرَّحِيمُ‌
>
> He said, ‘I shall plead with my Lord to forgive you; indeed He is the All-forgiving, the All-merciful.’

### Qur'an · Yusuf (Joseph) · 12:110
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/12/110
> حَتَّى‌ إِذَا اسْتَيْأَسَ‌ الرُّسُلُ‌ وَ ظَنُّوا أَنَّهُمْ‌ قَدْ کُذِبُوا جَاءَهُمْ‌ نَصْرُنَا فَنُجِّيَ‌ مَنْ‌ نَشَاءُ وَ لاَ يُرَدُّ بَأْسُنَا عَنِ‌ الْقَوْمِ‌ الْمُجْرِمِينَ‌
>
> When the apostles lost hope and they thought that they had been told lies, Our help came to them, and We delivered whomever We wished, and Our punishment will not be averted from the guilty lot.

### Qur'an · Ar-Ra'd (The Thunder) · 13:6
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/13/6
> وَ يَسْتَعْجِلُونَکَ‌ بِالسَّيِّئَةِ قَبْلَ‌ الْحَسَنَةِ وَ قَدْ خَلَتْ‌ مِنْ‌ قَبْلِهِمُ‌ الْمَثُلاَتُ‌ وَ إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ لَذُو مَغْفِرَةٍ لِلنَّاسِ‌ عَلَى‌ ظُلْمِهِمْ‌ وَ إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ لَشَدِيدُ الْعِقَابِ‌
>
> They would press you for evil sooner than for good, though there have already gone by before them exemplary punishments. Indeed your Lord is forgiving to mankind despite their wrongdoing, and indeed your Lord is severe in retribution.

### Qur'an · Ar-Ra'd (The Thunder) · 13:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/13/12
> هُوَ الَّذِي‌ يُرِيکُمُ‌ الْبَرْقَ‌ خَوْفاً وَ طَمَعاً وَ يُنْشِئُ‌ السَّحَابَ‌ الثِّقَالَ‌
>
> It is He who shows you the lightning, inspiring fear and hope, and He produces the clouds heavy [with rain].

### Qur'an · Ar-Ra'd (The Thunder) · 13:22
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/13/22
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ صَبَرُوا ابْتِغَاءَ وَجْهِ‌ رَبِّهِمْ‌ وَ أَقَامُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ أَنْفَقُوا مِمَّا رَزَقْنَاهُمْ‌ سِرّاً وَ عَلاَنِيَةً وَ يَدْرَءُونَ‌ بِالْحَسَنَةِ السَّيِّئَةَ أُولٰئِکَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ عُقْبَى‌ الدَّارِ
>
> —those who are patient for the sake of their Lord’s pleasure, maintain the prayer, and spend secretly and openly out of what We have provided them, and repel evil [conduct] with good. For such will be the reward of the [ultimate] abode:

### Qur'an · Ar-Ra'd (The Thunder) · 13:24
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/13/24
> سَلاَمٌ‌ عَلَيْکُمْ‌ بِمَا صَبَرْتُمْ‌ فَنِعْمَ‌ عُقْبَى‌ الدَّارِ
>
> ‘Peace be to you, for your patience.’ How excellent is the reward of the [ultimate] abode!

### Qur'an · Ar-Ra'd (The Thunder) · 13:37
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/13/37
> وَ کَذٰلِکَ‌ أَنْزَلْنَاهُ‌ حُکْماً عَرَبِيّاً وَ لَئِنِ‌ اتَّبَعْتَ‌ أَهْوَاءَهُمْ‌ بَعْدَ مَا جَاءَکَ‌ مِنَ‌ الْعِلْمِ‌ مَا لَکَ‌ مِنَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ مِنْ‌ وَلِيٍ‌ وَ لاَ وَاقٍ‌
>
> Thus We have sent it down as a dispensation in Arabic; and should you follow their desires after the knowledge that has come to you, you shall have against Allah neither any guardian nor defender.

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:5
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/5
> وَ لَقَدْ أَرْسَلْنَا مُوسَى‌ بِآيَاتِنَا أَنْ‌ أَخْرِجْ‌ قَوْمَکَ‌ مِنَ‌ الظُّلُمَاتِ‌ إِلَى‌ النُّورِ وَ ذَکِّرْهُمْ‌ بِأَيَّامِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ إِنَ‌ فِي‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ لَآيَاتٍ‌ لِکُلِ‌ صَبَّارٍ شَکُورٍ
>
> Certainly We sent Moses with Our signs: ‘Bring your people out from darkness into light and remind them of Allah’s [holy] days. There are indeed signs in that for every patient and grateful [servant].’

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:7
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/7
> وَ إِذْ تَأَذَّنَ‌ رَبُّکُمْ‌ لَئِنْ‌ شَکَرْتُمْ‌ لَأَزِيدَنَّکُمْ‌ وَ لَئِنْ‌ کَفَرْتُمْ‌ إِنَ‌ عَذَابِي‌ لَشَدِيدٌ
>
> And when your Lord proclaimed, “If you are grateful, I will surely enhance you [in blessing], but if you are ungrateful, My punishment is indeed severe.” ’

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:10
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance, self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/10
> قَالَتْ‌ رُسُلُهُمْ‌ أَ فِي‌ اللَّهِ‌ شَکٌ‌ فَاطِرِ السَّمَاوَاتِ‌ وَ الْأَرْضِ‌ يَدْعُوکُمْ‌ لِيَغْفِرَ لَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ ذُنُوبِکُمْ‌ وَ يُؤَخِّرَکُمْ‌ إِلَى‌ أَجَلٍ‌ مُسَمًّى‌ قَالُوا إِنْ‌ أَنْتُمْ‌ إِلاَّ بَشَرٌ مِثْلُنَا تُرِيدُونَ‌ أَنْ‌ تَصُدُّونَا عَمَّا کَانَ‌ يَعْبُدُ آبَاؤُنَا فَأْتُونَا بِسُلْطَانٍ‌ مُبِينٍ‌
>
> Their apostles said, ‘Is there any doubt about Allah, the originator of the heavens and the earth?! He calls you to forgive you a part of your sins, and grants you respite until a specified time.’ They said, ‘You are nothing but humans like us who desire to bar us from what our fathers used to worship. So bring us a manifest authority.’

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/12
> وَ مَا لَنَا أَلاَّ نَتَوَکَّلَ‌ عَلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ قَدْ هَدَانَا سُبُلَنَا وَ لَنَصْبِرَنَ‌ عَلَى‌ مَا آذَيْتُمُونَا وَ عَلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ فَلْيَتَوَکَّلِ‌ الْمُتَوَکِّلُونَ‌
>
> And why should we not put our trust in Allah, seeing that He has guided us in our ways? Surely, we will put up patiently with whatever torment you may inflict upon us, and in Allah let all the trusting put their trust.’

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:15
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/15
> وَ اسْتَفْتَحُوا وَ خَابَ‌ کُلُ‌ جَبَّارٍ عَنِيدٍ
>
> They prayed for victory [against the infidels], and every obstinate tyrant was defeated,

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:21
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/21
> وَ بَرَزُوا لِلَّهِ‌ جَمِيعاً فَقَالَ‌ الضُّعَفَاءُ لِلَّذِينَ‌ اسْتَکْبَرُوا إِنَّا کُنَّا لَکُمْ‌ تَبَعاً فَهَلْ‌ أَنْتُمْ‌ مُغْنُونَ‌ عَنَّا مِنْ‌ عَذَابِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ مِنْ‌ شَيْ‌ءٍ قَالُوا لَوْ هَدَانَا اللَّهُ‌ لَهَدَيْنَاکُمْ‌ سَوَاءٌ عَلَيْنَا أَ جَزِعْنَا أَمْ‌ صَبَرْنَا مَا لَنَا مِنْ‌ مَحِيصٍ‌
>
> Together they will be presented before Allah. Then those who were weak will say to the arrogant [leaders], ‘Indeed we were your followers. So will you avail us against Allah’s punishment in any wise?’ They will say, ‘Had Allah guided us, surely we would have guided you. It is the same to us whether we are restless or patient: there is no escape for us.’

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:27
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/27
> يُثَبِّتُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا بِالْقَوْلِ‌ الثَّابِتِ‌ فِي‌ الْحَيَاةِ الدُّنْيَا وَ فِي‌ الْآخِرَةِ وَ يُضِلُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ الظَّالِمِينَ‌ وَ يَفْعَلُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ مَا يَشَاءُ
>
> Allah fortifies those who have faith with a constant creed in the life of this world and in the Hereafter, and Allah leads astray the wrongdoers, and Allah does whatever He wishes.

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:28
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/28
> أَ لَمْ‌ تَرَ إِلَى‌ الَّذِينَ‌ بَدَّلُوا نِعْمَةَ اللَّهِ‌ کُفْراً وَ أَحَلُّوا قَوْمَهُمْ‌ دَارَ الْبَوَارِ
>
> Have you not regarded those who have changed Allah’s blessing with ingratitude, and landed their people in the house of ruin?

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:31
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/31
> قُلْ‌ لِعِبَادِيَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا يُقِيمُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ يُنْفِقُوا مِمَّا رَزَقْنَاهُمْ‌ سِرّاً وَ عَلاَنِيَةً مِنْ‌ قَبْلِ‌ أَنْ‌ يَأْتِيَ‌ يَوْمٌ‌ لاَ بَيْعٌ‌ فِيهِ‌ وَ لاَ خِلاَلٌ‌
>
> Tell My servants who have faith to maintain the prayer and to spend out of what We have provided them with, secretly and openly, before there comes a day on which there will be neither any bargaining nor friendship.

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:33
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/33
> وَ سَخَّرَ لَکُمُ‌ الشَّمْسَ‌ وَ الْقَمَرَ دَائِبَيْنِ‌ وَ سَخَّرَ لَکُمُ‌ اللَّيْلَ‌ وَ النَّهَارَ
>
> He disposed the sun and the moon for you, constant [in their courses], and He disposed the night and the day,

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:36
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/36
> رَبِ‌ إِنَّهُنَ‌ أَضْلَلْنَ‌ کَثِيراً مِنَ‌ النَّاسِ‌ فَمَنْ‌ تَبِعَنِي‌ فَإِنَّهُ‌ مِنِّي‌ وَ مَنْ‌ عَصَانِي‌ فَإِنَّکَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> My Lord! Indeed they have misled many people. So whoever follows me indeed belongs to me, and as for those who disobey me, well, You are indeed all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:37
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/37
> رَبَّنَا إِنِّي‌ أَسْکَنْتُ‌ مِنْ‌ ذُرِّيَّتِي‌ بِوَادٍ غَيْرِ ذِي‌ زَرْعٍ‌ عِنْدَ بَيْتِکَ‌ الْمُحَرَّمِ‌ رَبَّنَا لِيُقِيمُوا الصَّلاَةَ فَاجْعَلْ‌ أَفْئِدَةً مِنَ‌ النَّاسِ‌ تَهْوِي‌ إِلَيْهِمْ‌ وَ ارْزُقْهُمْ‌ مِنَ‌ الثَّمَرَاتِ‌ لَعَلَّهُمْ‌ يَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> Our Lord! I have settled part of my descendants in a barren valley, by Your sacred House, our Lord, that they may maintain the prayer. So make the hearts of a part of the people fond of them, and provide them with fruits, so that they may give thanks.

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:39
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/39
> الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ‌ الَّذِي‌ وَهَبَ‌ لِي‌ عَلَى‌ الْکِبَرِ إِسْمَاعِيلَ‌ وَ إِسْحَاقَ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبِّي‌ لَسَمِيعُ‌ الدُّعَاءِ
>
> All praise belongs to Allah, who gave me Ishmael and Isaac despite [my] old age. Indeed my Lord hears all supplications.

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:40
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/40
> رَبِ‌ اجْعَلْنِي‌ مُقِيمَ‌ الصَّلاَةِ وَ مِنْ‌ ذُرِّيَّتِي‌ رَبَّنَا وَ تَقَبَّلْ‌ دُعَاءِ
>
> My Lord! Make me a maintainer of prayer, and my descendants [as well]. Our Lord, accept my supplication.

### Qur'an · Ibrahim (Abraham) · 14:41
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/14/41
> رَبَّنَا اغْفِرْ لِي‌ وَ لِوَالِدَيَ‌ وَ لِلْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌ يَوْمَ‌ يَقُومُ‌ الْحِسَابُ‌
>
> Our Lord! Forgive me and my parents, and all the faithful, on the day when the reckoning is held.’

### Qur'an · Al-Hijr (The Rock) · 15:49
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī

>
> Inform my servants that I am indeed the All-forgiving, the All-merciful,

### Qur'an · Al-Hijr (The Rock) · 15:56
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/15/56
> قَالَ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ يَقْنَطُ مِنْ‌ رَحْمَةِ رَبِّهِ‌ إِلاَّ الضَّالُّونَ‌
>
> He said, ‘Who despairs of his Lord’s mercy except the astray?!’

### Qur'an · Al-Hijr (The Rock) · 15:85
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/15/85
> وَ مَا خَلَقْنَا السَّمَاوَاتِ‌ وَ الْأَرْضَ‌ وَ مَا بَيْنَهُمَا إِلاَّ بِالْحَقِ‌ وَ إِنَ‌ السَّاعَةَ لَآتِيَةٌ فَاصْفَحِ‌ الصَّفْحَ‌ الْجَمِيلَ‌
>
> We did not create the heavens and the earth and whatever is between them except with consummate wisdom, and indeed the Hour is bound to come. So forbear with a graceful forbearance.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/14
> وَ هُوَ الَّذِي‌ سَخَّرَ الْبَحْرَ لِتَأْکُلُوا مِنْهُ‌ لَحْماً طَرِيّاً وَ تَسْتَخْرِجُوا مِنْهُ‌ حِلْيَةً تَلْبَسُونَهَا وَ تَرَى‌ الْفُلْکَ‌ مَوَاخِرَ فِيهِ‌ وَ لِتَبْتَغُوا مِنْ‌ فَضْلِهِ‌ وَ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> It is He who disposed the sea [for your benefit] that you may eat from it fresh meat, and obtain from it ornaments which you wear, and you see the ships plowing through it, that you may seek His bounty and that you may give thanks.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:18
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/18
> وَ إِنْ‌ تَعُدُّوا نِعْمَةَ اللَّهِ‌ لاَ تُحْصُوهَا إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> If you enumerate Allah’s blessings, you will not be able to count them. Indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful,

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:42
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/42
> الَّذِينَ‌ صَبَرُوا وَ عَلَى‌ رَبِّهِمْ‌ يَتَوَکَّلُونَ‌
>
> —those who are patient and who put their trust in their Lord.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:52
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/52
> وَ لَهُ‌ مَا فِي‌ السَّمَاوَاتِ‌ وَ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ لَهُ‌ الدِّينُ‌ وَاصِباً أَ فَغَيْرَ اللَّهِ‌ تَتَّقُونَ‌
>
> To Him belongs whatever is in the heavens and the earth, and to Him belongs the enduring religion. Will you, then, be wary of other than Allah?

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:57
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/57
> وَ يَجْعَلُونَ‌ لِلَّهِ‌ الْبَنَاتِ‌ سُبْحَانَهُ‌ وَ لَهُمْ‌ مَا يَشْتَهُونَ‌
>
> And they attribute daughters to Allah—immaculate is He—while they will have what they desire!

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:78
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/78
> وَ اللَّهُ‌ أَخْرَجَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ بُطُونِ‌ أُمَّهَاتِکُمْ‌ لاَ تَعْلَمُونَ‌ شَيْئاً وَ جَعَلَ‌ لَکُمُ‌ السَّمْعَ‌ وَ الْأَبْصَارَ وَ الْأَفْئِدَةَ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> Allah has brought you forth from the bellies of your mothers while you did not know anything. He made for you hearing, eyesight, and hearts so that you may give thanks.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:80
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/80
> وَ اللَّهُ‌ جَعَلَ‌ لَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ بُيُوتِکُمْ‌ سَکَناً وَ جَعَلَ‌ لَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ جُلُودِ الْأَنْعَامِ‌ بُيُوتاً تَسْتَخِفُّونَهَا يَوْمَ‌ ظَعْنِکُمْ‌ وَ يَوْمَ‌ إِقَامَتِکُمْ‌ وَ مِنْ‌ أَصْوَافِهَا وَ أَوْبَارِهَا وَ أَشْعَارِهَا أَثَاثاً وَ مَتَاعاً إِلَى‌ حِينٍ‌
>
> It is Allah who has made your homes as a place of rest for you and He made for you homes out of the skins of the cattle which you find portable on the day of your shifting and on the day of your halt, and out of their wool, fur and hair [He has appointed] furniture and wares [enduring] for a while.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:96
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/96
> مَا عِنْدَکُمْ‌ يَنْفَدُ وَ مَا عِنْدَ اللَّهِ‌ بَاقٍ‌ وَ لَنَجْزِيَنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ صَبَرُوا أَجْرَهُمْ‌ بِأَحْسَنِ‌ مَا کَانُوا يَعْمَلُونَ‌
>
> That which is with you will be spent [and gone], but what is with Allah shall last, and We will surely pay the patient their reward by the best of what they used to do.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:110
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance, perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/110
> ثُمَ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ لِلَّذِينَ‌ هَاجَرُوا مِنْ‌ بَعْدِ مَا فُتِنُوا ثُمَ‌ جَاهَدُوا وَ صَبَرُوا إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِهَا لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Thereafter your Lord will indeed be forgiving and merciful to those who migrated after they were persecuted, waged jihad and remained steadfast.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:114
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/114
> فَکُلُوا مِمَّا رَزَقَکُمُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ حَلاَلاً طَيِّباً وَ اشْکُرُوا نِعْمَةَ اللَّهِ‌ إِنْ‌ کُنْتُمْ‌ إِيَّاهُ‌ تَعْبُدُونَ‌
>
> So eat out of what Allah has provided you as lawful and good, and give thanks for Allah’s blessing, if it is Him that you worship.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:115
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/115
> إِنَّمَا حَرَّمَ‌ عَلَيْکُمُ‌ الْمَيْتَةَ وَ الدَّمَ‌ وَ لَحْمَ‌ الْخِنْزِيرِ وَ مَا أُهِلَ‌ لِغَيْرِ اللَّهِ‌ بِهِ‌ فَمَنِ‌ اضْطُرَّ غَيْرَ بَاغٍ‌ وَ لاَ عَادٍ فَإِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> He has forbidden you only carrion, blood, the flesh of swine, and that which has been offered to other than Allah. But if someone is compelled [to eat any of that], without being rebellious or aggressive, indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:119
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/119
> ثُمَ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ لِلَّذِينَ‌ عَمِلُوا السُّوءَ بِجَهَالَةٍ ثُمَ‌ تَابُوا مِنْ‌ بَعْدِ ذٰلِکَ‌ وَ أَصْلَحُوا إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِهَا لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Moreover, your Lord will indeed be forgiving and merciful to those who repent after they having committed evil out of ignorance and reform themselves.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:121
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/121
> شَاکِراً لِأَنْعُمِهِ‌ اجْتَبَاهُ‌ وَ هَدَاهُ‌ إِلَى‌ صِرَاطٍ مُسْتَقِيمٍ‌
>
> Grateful [as he was] for His blessings, He chose him and guided him to a straight path.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:126
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/126
> وَ إِنْ‌ عَاقَبْتُمْ‌ فَعَاقِبُوا بِمِثْلِ‌ مَا عُوقِبْتُمْ‌ بِهِ‌ وَ لَئِنْ‌ صَبَرْتُمْ‌ لَهُوَ خَيْرٌ لِلصَّابِرِينَ‌
>
> Should you retaliate, retaliate with the like of what you have been made to suffer, but if you are patient that is surely better for the steadfast.

### Qur'an · An-Nahl (The Bee) · 16:127
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/16/127
> وَ اصْبِرْ وَ مَا صَبْرُکَ‌ إِلاَّ بِاللَّهِ‌ وَ لاَ تَحْزَنْ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ وَ لاَ تَکُ‌ فِي‌ ضَيْقٍ‌ مِمَّا يَمْکُرُونَ‌
>
> So be steadfast, and you cannot be steadfast except with Allah [’s help]. And do not grieve for them, nor be upset by their guile.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:3
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/3
> ذُرِّيَّةَ مَنْ‌ حَمَلْنَا مَعَ‌ نُوحٍ‌ إِنَّهُ‌ کَانَ‌ عَبْداً شَکُوراً
>
> descendants of those whom We carried [in the ark] with Noah. Indeed, he was a grateful servant.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:11
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/11
> وَ يَدْعُ‌ الْإِنْسَانُ‌ بِالشَّرِّ دُعَاءَهُ‌ بِالْخَيْرِ وَ کَانَ‌ الْإِنْسَانُ‌ عَجُولاً
>
> Man prays for ill as [avidly as] he prays for good, and man is overhasty.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:16
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/16
> وَ إِذَا أَرَدْنَا أَنْ‌ نُهْلِکَ‌ قَرْيَةً أَمَرْنَا مُتْرَفِيهَا فَفَسَقُوا فِيهَا فَحَقَ‌ عَلَيْهَا الْقَوْلُ‌ فَدَمَّرْنَاهَا تَدْمِيراً
>
> And when We desire to destroy a town We command its affluent ones [to obey Allah]. But they commit transgression in it, and so the word becomes due against it, and We destroy it utterly.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:18
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/18
> مَنْ‌ کَانَ‌ يُرِيدُ الْعَاجِلَةَ عَجَّلْنَا لَهُ‌ فِيهَا مَا نَشَاءُ لِمَنْ‌ نُرِيدُ ثُمَ‌ جَعَلْنَا لَهُ‌ جَهَنَّمَ‌ يَصْلاَهَا مَذْمُوماً مَدْحُوراً
>
> Whoever desires this transitory life, We expedite for him therein whatever We wish, for whomever We desire. Then We appoint hell for him, to enter it, blameful and spurned.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:19
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control, perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/19
> وَ مَنْ‌ أَرَادَ الْآخِرَةَ وَ سَعَى‌ لَهَا سَعْيَهَا وَ هُوَ مُؤْمِنٌ‌ فَأُولٰئِکَ‌ کَانَ‌ سَعْيُهُمْ‌ مَشْکُوراً
>
> Whoever desires the Hereafter and strives for it with an endeavour worthy of it, should he be faithful,—the endeavour of such will be well-appreciated.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:25
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/25
> رَبُّکُمْ‌ أَعْلَمُ‌ بِمَا فِي‌ نُفُوسِکُمْ‌ إِنْ‌ تَکُونُوا صَالِحِينَ‌ فَإِنَّهُ‌ کَانَ‌ لِلْأَوَّابِينَ‌ غَفُوراً
>
> Your Lord knows best what is in your hearts. Should you be righteous, He is indeed most forgiving toward penitents.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:44
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/44
> تُسَبِّحُ‌ لَهُ‌ السَّمَاوَاتُ‌ السَّبْعُ‌ وَ الْأَرْضُ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ فِيهِنَ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ مِنْ‌ شَيْ‌ءٍ إِلاَّ يُسَبِّحُ‌ بِحَمْدِهِ‌ وَ لٰکِنْ‌ لاَ تَفْقَهُونَ‌ تَسْبِيحَهُمْ‌ إِنَّهُ‌ کَانَ‌ حَلِيماً غَفُوراً
>
> The seven heavens glorify Him, and the earth [too], and whoever is in them. There is not a thing but celebrates His praise, but you do not understand their glorification. Indeed, He is all-forbearing, all-forgiving.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:57
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/57
> أُولٰئِکَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ يَدْعُونَ‌ يَبْتَغُونَ‌ إِلَى‌ رَبِّهِمُ‌ الْوَسِيلَةَ أَيُّهُمْ‌ أَقْرَبُ‌ وَ يَرْجُونَ‌ رَحْمَتَهُ‌ وَ يَخَافُونَ‌ عَذَابَهُ‌ إِنَ‌ عَذَابَ‌ رَبِّکَ‌ کَانَ‌ مَحْذُوراً
>
> They [themselves] are the ones who supplicate, seeking a recourse to their Lord, whoever is nearer [to Him], expecting His mercy and fearing His punishment.’ Indeed your Lord’s punishment is a thing to beware of.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:78
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/78
> أَقِمِ‌ الصَّلاَةَ لِدُلُوکِ‌ الشَّمْسِ‌ إِلَى‌ غَسَقِ‌ اللَّيْلِ‌ وَ قُرْآنَ‌ الْفَجْرِ إِنَ‌ قُرْآنَ‌ الْفَجْرِ کَانَ‌ مَشْهُوداً
>
> Maintain the prayer [during the period] from the sun’s decline till the darkness of the night, and [observe particularly] the dawn recital. Indeed the dawn recital is attended [by angels].

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:89
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/89
> وَ لَقَدْ صَرَّفْنَا لِلنَّاسِ‌ فِي‌ هٰذَا الْقُرْآنِ‌ مِنْ‌ کُلِ‌ مَثَلٍ‌ فَأَبَى‌ أَکْثَرُ النَّاسِ‌ إِلاَّ کُفُوراً
>
> We have certainly interspersed this Quran with every [kind of] parable for the people, but most people are only intent on ingratitude.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:99
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/99
> أَ وَ لَمْ‌ يَرَوْا أَنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ الَّذِي‌ خَلَقَ‌ السَّمَاوَاتِ‌ وَ الْأَرْضَ‌ قَادِرٌ عَلَى‌ أَنْ‌ يَخْلُقَ‌ مِثْلَهُمْ‌ وَ جَعَلَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ أَجَلاً لاَ رَيْبَ‌ فِيهِ‌ فَأَبَى‌ الظَّالِمُونَ‌ إِلاَّ کُفُوراً
>
> Do they not see that Allah, who created the heavens and the earth, is able to create the like of them? He has appointed for them a term, in which there is no doubt; yet the wrongdoers are only intent on ingratitude.

### Qur'an · Al-Israa (The Night Journey) · 17:110
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/17/110
> قُلِ‌ ادْعُوا اللَّهَ‌ أَوِ ادْعُوا الرَّحْمٰنَ‌ أَيّاً مَا تَدْعُوا فَلَهُ‌ الْأَسْمَاءُ الْحُسْنَى‌ وَ لاَ تَجْهَرْ بِصَلاَتِکَ‌ وَ لاَ تُخَافِتْ‌ بِهَا وَ ابْتَغِ‌ بَيْنَ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ سَبِيلاً
>
> Say, ‘Invoke ‘‘Allah’’ or invoke ‘‘the All-beneficent.’’ Whichever [of His Names] you may invoke, to Him belong the Best Names.’ Be neither loud in your prayer, nor murmur it, but follow a middle course between these,

### Qur'an · Al-Kahf (The Cave) · 18:28
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/18/28
> وَ اصْبِرْ نَفْسَکَ‌ مَعَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ يَدْعُونَ‌ رَبَّهُمْ‌ بِالْغَدَاةِ وَ الْعَشِيِ‌ يُرِيدُونَ‌ وَجْهَهُ‌ وَ لاَ تَعْدُ عَيْنَاکَ‌ عَنْهُمْ‌ تُرِيدُ زِينَةَ الْحَيَاةِ الدُّنْيَا وَ لاَ تُطِعْ‌ مَنْ‌ أَغْفَلْنَا قَلْبَهُ‌ عَنْ‌ ذِکْرِنَا وَ اتَّبَعَ‌ هَوَاهُ‌ وَ کَانَ‌ أَمْرُهُ‌ فُرُطاً
>
> Content yourself with the company of those who supplicate their Lord morning and evening, desiring His Face, and do not lose sight of them, desiring the glitter of the life of this world. And do not obey him whose heart We have made oblivious to Our remembrance, and who follows his own desires, and whose conduct is [mere] profligacy.

### Qur'an · Al-Kahf (The Cave) · 18:46
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/18/46
> الْمَالُ‌ وَ الْبَنُونَ‌ زِينَةُ الْحَيَاةِ الدُّنْيَا وَ الْبَاقِيَاتُ‌ الصَّالِحَاتُ‌ خَيْرٌ عِنْدَ رَبِّکَ‌ ثَوَاباً وَ خَيْرٌ أَمَلاً
>
> Wealth and children are an adornment of the life of the world, but lasting righteous deeds are better with your Lord in reward and better in hope.

### Qur'an · Al-Kahf (The Cave) · 18:55
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/18/55
> وَ مَا مَنَعَ‌ النَّاسَ‌ أَنْ‌ يُؤْمِنُوا إِذْ جَاءَهُمُ‌ الْهُدَى‌ وَ يَسْتَغْفِرُوا رَبَّهُمْ‌ إِلاَّ أَنْ‌ تَأْتِيَهُمْ‌ سُنَّةُ الْأَوَّلِينَ‌ أَوْ يَأْتِيَهُمُ‌ الْعَذَابُ‌ قُبُلاً
>
> Nothing has kept these people from believing and pleading to their Lord for forgiveness when guidance came to them, except [their demand] that the precedent of the ancients come to pass for them, or that the punishment come to them, face to face.

### Qur'an · Al-Kahf (The Cave) · 18:58
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/18/58
> وَ رَبُّکَ‌ الْغَفُورُ ذُو الرَّحْمَةِ لَوْ يُؤَاخِذُهُمْ‌ بِمَا کَسَبُوا لَعَجَّلَ‌ لَهُمُ‌ الْعَذَابَ‌ بَلْ‌ لَهُمْ‌ مَوْعِدٌ لَنْ‌ يَجِدُوا مِنْ‌ دُونِهِ‌ مَوْئِلاً
>
> Your Lord is the All-forgiving dispenser of mercy. Were He to take them to task because of what they have committed, He would have surely hastened their punishment. But they have a tryst, [when] they will not find a refuge besides Him.

### Qur'an · Al-Kahf (The Cave) · 18:67

>
> He said, ‘Indeed you cannot have patience with me!

### Qur'an · Al-Kahf (The Cave) · 18:68
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/18/68

>
> And how can you have patience about something you do not comprehend?’

### Qur'an · Al-Kahf (The Cave) · 18:69
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/18/69

>
> He said, ‘You will find me, God willing, to be patient, and I will not disobey you in any matter.’

### Qur'an · Al-Kahf (The Cave) · 18:72
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience

>
> He said, ‘Did I not say that you cannot have patience with me?’

> قَالَ‌ أَ لَمْ‌ أَقُلْ‌ لَکَ‌ إِنَّکَ‌ لَنْ‌ تَسْتَطِيعَ‌ مَعِيَ‌ صَبْراً
>
