### Qur'an · Al-Kahf (The Cave) · 18:78
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/18/78
> قَالَ‌ هٰذَا فِرَاقُ‌ بَيْنِي‌ وَ بَيْنِکَ‌ سَأُنَبِّئُکَ‌ بِتَأْوِيلِ‌ مَا لَمْ‌ تَسْتَطِعْ‌ عَلَيْهِ‌ صَبْراً
>
> He said, ‘This is where you and I shall part. I will inform you about the interpretation of that over which you could not maintain patience.

### Qur'an · Al-Kahf (The Cave) · 18:82
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/18/82
> وَ أَمَّا الْجِدَارُ فَکَانَ‌ لِغُلاَمَيْنِ‌ يَتِيمَيْنِ‌ فِي‌ الْمَدِينَةِ وَ کَانَ‌ تَحْتَهُ‌ کَنْزٌ لَهُمَا وَ کَانَ‌ أَبُوهُمَا صَالِحاً فَأَرَادَ رَبُّکَ‌ أَنْ‌ يَبْلُغَا أَشُدَّهُمَا وَ يَسْتَخْرِجَا کَنْزَهُمَا رَحْمَةً مِنْ‌ رَبِّکَ‌ وَ مَا فَعَلْتُهُ‌ عَنْ‌ أَمْرِي‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ تَأْوِيلُ‌ مَا لَمْ‌ تَسْطِعْ‌ عَلَيْهِ‌ صَبْراً
>
> As for the wall, it belonged to two boy orphans in the city. Under it there was a treasure belonging to them. Their father had been a righteous man. So your Lord desired that they should come of age and take out their treasure—as a mercy from your Lord. I did not do that out of my own accord. This is the interpretation of that over which you could not maintain patience.’

### Qur'an · Maryam (Mary) · 19:4
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/19/4
> قَالَ‌ رَبِ‌ إِنِّي‌ وَهَنَ‌ الْعَظْمُ‌ مِنِّي‌ وَ اشْتَعَلَ‌ الرَّأْسُ‌ شَيْباً وَ لَمْ‌ أَکُنْ‌ بِدُعَائِکَ‌ رَبِ‌ شَقِيّاً
>
> He said, ‘My Lord! Indeed my bones have become feeble, and my head has turned white with age, yet never have I, my Lord, been disappointed in supplicating You!

### Qur'an · Maryam (Mary) · 19:31
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/19/31
> وَ جَعَلَنِي‌ مُبَارَکاً أَيْنَ‌ مَا کُنْتُ‌ وَ أَوْصَانِي‌ بِالصَّلاَةِ وَ الزَّکَاةِ مَا دُمْتُ‌ حَيّاً
>
> He has made me blessed, wherever I may be, and He has enjoined me to [maintain] the prayer and to [pay] the zakat as long as I live,

### Qur'an · Maryam (Mary) · 19:39
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/19/39
> وَ أَنْذِرْهُمْ‌ يَوْمَ‌ الْحَسْرَةِ إِذْ قُضِيَ‌ الْأَمْرُ وَ هُمْ‌ فِي‌ غَفْلَةٍ وَ هُمْ‌ لاَ يُؤْمِنُونَ‌
>
> Warn them of the Day of Regret, when the matter will be decided, while they are [yet] heedless and do not have faith.

### Qur'an · Maryam (Mary) · 19:47
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/19/47
> قَالَ‌ سَلاَمٌ‌ عَلَيْکَ‌ سَأَسْتَغْفِرُ لَکَ‌ رَبِّي‌ إِنَّهُ‌ کَانَ‌ بِي‌ حَفِيّاً
>
> He said, ‘Peace be to you! I shall plead with my Lord to forgive you. Indeed, He is gracious to me.

### Qur'an · Maryam (Mary) · 19:48
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/19/48
> وَ أَعْتَزِلُکُمْ‌ وَ مَا تَدْعُونَ‌ مِنْ‌ دُونِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ أَدْعُو رَبِّي‌ عَسَى‌ أَلاَّ أَکُونَ‌ بِدُعَاءِ رَبِّي‌ شَقِيّاً
>
> I dissociate myself from you and whatever you invoke besides Allah. I will supplicate my Lord. Hopefully, I will not be disappointed in supplicating my Lord.’

### Qur'an · Maryam (Mary) · 19:55
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/19/55
> وَ کَانَ‌ يَأْمُرُ أَهْلَهُ‌ بِالصَّلاَةِ وَ الزَّکَاةِ وَ کَانَ‌ عِنْدَ رَبِّهِ‌ مَرْضِيّاً
>
> He used to bid his family to [maintain] the prayer and to [pay] the zakat, and was pleasing to his Lord.

### Qur'an · Maryam (Mary) · 19:59
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/19/59
> فَخَلَفَ‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِهِمْ‌ خَلْفٌ‌ أَضَاعُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ اتَّبَعُوا الشَّهَوَاتِ‌ فَسَوْفَ‌ يَلْقَوْنَ‌ غَيّاً
>
> But they were succeeded by an evil posterity who neglected the prayer, and followed [their base] appetites. So they will soon encounter [the reward of] perversity,

### Qur'an · Maryam (Mary) · 19:60
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/19/60
> إِلاَّ مَنْ‌ تَابَ‌ وَ آمَنَ‌ وَ عَمِلَ‌ صَالِحاً فَأُولٰئِکَ‌ يَدْخُلُونَ‌ الْجَنَّةَ وَ لاَ يُظْلَمُونَ‌ شَيْئاً
>
> barring those who repent, believe, and act righteously. Such will enter paradise, and they will not be wronged in the least.

### Qur'an · Maryam (Mary) · 19:65
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/19/65
> رَبُ‌ السَّمَاوَاتِ‌ وَ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ مَا بَيْنَهُمَا فَاعْبُدْهُ‌ وَ اصْطَبِرْ لِعِبَادَتِهِ‌ هَلْ‌ تَعْلَمُ‌ لَهُ‌ سَمِيّاً
>
> —the Lord of the heavens and the earth and whatever is between them. So worship Him and be steadfast in His worship. Do you know anyone who could be His namesake?’

### Qur'an · Taa-Haa (Taa-Haa) · 20:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/20/14
> إِنَّنِي‌ أَنَا اللَّهُ‌ لاَ إِلٰهَ‌ إِلاَّ أَنَا فَاعْبُدْنِي‌ وَ أَقِمِ‌ الصَّلاَةَ لِذِکْرِي‌
>
> Indeed I am Allah—there is no god except Me. So worship Me, and maintain the prayer for My remembrance.

### Qur'an · Taa-Haa (Taa-Haa) · 20:16
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/20/16
> فَلاَ يَصُدَّنَّکَ‌ عَنْهَا مَنْ‌ لاَ يُؤْمِنُ‌ بِهَا وَ اتَّبَعَ‌ هَوَاهُ‌ فَتَرْدَى‌
>
> So do not let yourself be distracted from it by those who do not believe in it and who follow their desires, or you will perish.’

### Qur'an · Taa-Haa (Taa-Haa) · 20:73
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/20/73
> إِنَّا آمَنَّا بِرَبِّنَا لِيَغْفِرَ لَنَا خَطَايَانَا وَ مَا أَکْرَهْتَنَا عَلَيْهِ‌ مِنَ‌ السِّحْرِ وَ اللَّهُ‌ خَيْرٌ وَ أَبْقَى‌
>
> We have indeed believed in our Lord that He may forgive us our offences and the magic you compelled us to perform. Allah is better and more lasting.’

### Qur'an · Taa-Haa (Taa-Haa) · 20:82
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/20/82
> وَ إِنِّي‌ لَغَفَّارٌ لِمَنْ‌ تَابَ‌ وَ آمَنَ‌ وَ عَمِلَ‌ صَالِحاً ثُمَ‌ اهْتَدَى‌
>
> Indeed I forgive those who repent, become faithful, act righteously, and follow guidance.’

### Qur'an · Taa-Haa (Taa-Haa) · 20:86
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/20/86
> فَرَجَعَ‌ مُوسَى‌ إِلَى‌ قَوْمِهِ‌ غَضْبَانَ‌ أَسِفاً قَالَ‌ يَا قَوْمِ‌ أَ لَمْ‌ يَعِدْکُمْ‌ رَبُّکُمْ‌ وَعْداً حَسَناً أَ فَطَالَ‌ عَلَيْکُمُ‌ الْعَهْدُ أَمْ‌ أَرَدْتُمْ‌ أَنْ‌ يَحِلَ‌ عَلَيْکُمْ‌ غَضَبٌ‌ مِنْ‌ رَبِّکُمْ‌ فَأَخْلَفْتُمْ‌ مَوْعِدِي‌
>
> Thereupon Moses returned to his people, indignant and grieved. He said, ‘O my people! Did your Lord not give you a true promise? Did the period [of my absence] seem too long to you? Or did you desire that your Lord’s wrath should descend on you and so you failed your tryst with me?’

### Qur'an · Taa-Haa (Taa-Haa) · 20:130
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/20/130
> فَاصْبِرْ عَلَى‌ مَا يَقُولُونَ‌ وَ سَبِّحْ‌ بِحَمْدِ رَبِّکَ‌ قَبْلَ‌ طُلُوعِ‌ الشَّمْسِ‌ وَ قَبْلَ‌ غُرُوبِهَا وَ مِنْ‌ آنَاءِ اللَّيْلِ‌ فَسَبِّحْ‌ وَ أَطْرَافَ‌ النَّهَارِ لَعَلَّکَ‌ تَرْضَى‌
>
> So be patient with what they say, and celebrate the praise of your Lord before the rising of the sun and before the sunset, and glorify Him in watches of the night and at the day’s ends, that you may be pleased.

### Qur'an · Taa-Haa (Taa-Haa) · 20:132
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/20/132
> وَ أْمُرْ أَهْلَکَ‌ بِالصَّلاَةِ وَ اصْطَبِرْ عَلَيْهَا لاَ نَسْأَلُکَ‌ رِزْقاً نَحْنُ‌ نَرْزُقُکَ‌ وَ الْعَاقِبَةُ لِلتَّقْوَى‌
>
> And bid your family to prayer and be steadfast in maintaining it. We do not ask any provision of you: it is We who provide for you, and to Godwariness belongs the ultimate outcome [in the Hereafter].

### Qur'an · Al-Anbiyaa (The Prophets) · 21:73
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/21/73
> وَ جَعَلْنَاهُمْ‌ أَئِمَّةً يَهْدُونَ‌ بِأَمْرِنَا وَ أَوْحَيْنَا إِلَيْهِمْ‌ فِعْلَ‌ الْخَيْرَاتِ‌ وَ إِقَامَ‌ الصَّلاَةِ وَ إِيتَاءَ الزَّکَاةِ وَ کَانُوا لَنَا عَابِدِينَ‌
>
> We made them imams, guiding by Our command, and We revealed to them [concerning] the performance of good deeds, the maintenance of prayers, and the giving of zakat, and they used to worship Us.

### Qur'an · Al-Anbiyaa (The Prophets) · 21:80
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/21/80
> وَ عَلَّمْنَاهُ‌ صَنْعَةَ لَبُوسٍ‌ لَکُمْ‌ لِتُحْصِنَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ بَأْسِکُمْ‌ فَهَلْ‌ أَنْتُمْ‌ شَاکِرُونَ‌
>
> We taught him the making of coats of mail for you, to protect you from your [own] violence. Will you then be grateful?

### Qur'an · Al-Anbiyaa (The Prophets) · 21:84
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/21/84
> فَاسْتَجَبْنَا لَهُ‌ فَکَشَفْنَا مَا بِهِ‌ مِنْ‌ ضُرٍّ وَ آتَيْنَاهُ‌ أَهْلَهُ‌ وَ مِثْلَهُمْ‌ مَعَهُمْ‌ رَحْمَةً مِنْ‌ عِنْدِنَا وَ ذِکْرَى‌ لِلْعَابِدِينَ‌
>
> So We answered his prayer and removed his distress, and We gave him [back] his family along with others like them, as a mercy from Us and an admonition for the devout.

### Qur'an · Al-Anbiyaa (The Prophets) · 21:85
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/21/85
> وَ إِسْمَاعِيلَ‌ وَ إِدْرِيسَ‌ وَ ذَا الْکِفْلِ‌ کُلٌ‌ مِنَ‌ الصَّابِرِينَ‌
>
> And [remember] Ishmael, Idris, and Dhul-Kifl—each of them was among the patient.

### Qur'an · Al-Anbiyaa (The Prophets) · 21:88
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/21/88
> فَاسْتَجَبْنَا لَهُ‌ وَ نَجَّيْنَاهُ‌ مِنَ‌ الْغَمِ‌ وَ کَذٰلِکَ‌ نُنْجِي‌ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌
>
> So We answered his prayer and delivered him from the agony; and thus do We deliver the faithful.

### Qur'an · Al-Anbiyaa (The Prophets) · 21:90
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/21/90
> فَاسْتَجَبْنَا لَهُ‌ وَ وَهَبْنَا لَهُ‌ يَحْيَى‌ وَ أَصْلَحْنَا لَهُ‌ زَوْجَهُ‌ إِنَّهُمْ‌ کَانُوا يُسَارِعُونَ‌ فِي‌ الْخَيْرَاتِ‌ وَ يَدْعُونَنَا رَغَباً وَ رَهَباً وَ کَانُوا لَنَا خَاشِعِينَ‌
>
> So We answered his prayer, and gave him John, and cured for him his wife [of infertility]. Indeed, they were active in [performing] good works, and they would supplicate Us with eagerness and awe and were humble before Us.

### Qur'an · Al-Anbiyaa (The Prophets) · 21:102
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/21/102
> لاَ يَسْمَعُونَ‌ حَسِيسَهَا وَ هُمْ‌ فِي‌ مَا اشْتَهَتْ‌ أَنْفُسُهُمْ‌ خَالِدُونَ‌
>
> They will not hear even its faint sound and they will remain [forever] in what their souls desire.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/14
> إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ يُدْخِلُ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا وَ عَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ‌ جَنَّاتٍ‌ تَجْرِي‌ مِنْ‌ تَحْتِهَا الْأَنْهَارُ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ يَفْعَلُ‌ مَا يُرِيدُ
>
> Allah will indeed admit those who have faith and do righteous deeds into gardens with streams running in them. Indeed Allah does whatever He desires.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:16
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/16
> وَ کَذٰلِکَ‌ أَنْزَلْنَاهُ‌ آيَاتٍ‌ بَيِّنَاتٍ‌ وَ أَنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ يَهْدِي‌ مَنْ‌ يُرِيدُ
>
> Thus have We sent it down as manifest signs, and indeed Allah guides whomever He desires.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:22
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/22
> کُلَّمَا أَرَادُوا أَنْ‌ يَخْرُجُوا مِنْهَا مِنْ‌ غَمٍ‌ أُعِيدُوا فِيهَا وَ ذُوقُوا عَذَابَ‌ الْحَرِيقِ‌
>
> Whenever they desire to leave it out of anguish, they will be turned back into it [and told]: ‘Taste the punishment of the burning!’

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:26
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/26
> وَ إِذْ بَوَّأْنَا لِإِبْرَاهِيمَ‌ مَکَانَ‌ الْبَيْتِ‌ أَنْ‌ لاَ تُشْرِکْ‌ بِي‌ شَيْئاً وَ طَهِّرْ بَيْتِيَ‌ لِلطَّائِفِينَ‌ وَ الْقَائِمِينَ‌ وَ الرُّکَّعِ‌ السُّجُودِ
>
> When We settled for Abraham the site of the House [saying], Do not ascribe any partners to Me, and purify My House for those who circle around it, and those who stand [in it for prayer], and those who bow and prostrate themselves.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:35
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/35
> الَّذِينَ‌ إِذَا ذُکِرَ اللَّهُ‌ وَجِلَتْ‌ قُلُوبُهُمْ‌ وَ الصَّابِرِينَ‌ عَلَى‌ مَا أَصَابَهُمْ‌ وَ الْمُقِيمِي‌ الصَّلاَةِ وَ مِمَّا رَزَقْنَاهُمْ‌ يُنْفِقُونَ‌
>
> —those whose hearts tremble with awe when Allah is mentioned, and who are patient through whatever visits them, and who maintain the prayer and spend out of what We have provided them.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:36
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/36
> وَ الْبُدْنَ‌ جَعَلْنَاهَا لَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ شَعَائِرِ اللَّهِ‌ لَکُمْ‌ فِيهَا خَيْرٌ فَاذْکُرُوا اسْمَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ عَلَيْهَا صَوَافَ‌ فَإِذَا وَجَبَتْ‌ جُنُوبُهَا فَکُلُوا مِنْهَا وَ أَطْعِمُوا الْقَانِعَ‌ وَ الْمُعْتَرَّ کَذٰلِکَ‌ سَخَّرْنَاهَا لَکُمْ‌ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> We have appointed for you the [sacrificial] camels as part of Allah’s sacraments. There is good for you in them. So mention the Name of Allah over them as they stand. And when they have fallen on their flanks, eat from them, and feed the self-contained needy and the mendicant. Thus have We disposed them for your benefit so that you may give thanks.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:38
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/38
> إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ يُدَافِعُ‌ عَنِ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لاَ يُحِبُ‌ کُلَ‌ خَوَّانٍ‌ کَفُورٍ
>
> Allah will indeed defend those who have faith. Indeed Allah does not like any ingrate traitor.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:41
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/41
> الَّذِينَ‌ إِنْ‌ مَکَّنَّاهُمْ‌ فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ أَقَامُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ آتَوُا الزَّکَاةَ وَ أَمَرُوا بِالْمَعْرُوفِ‌ وَ نَهَوْا عَنِ‌ الْمُنْکَرِ وَ لِلَّهِ‌ عَاقِبَةُ الْأُمُورِ
>
> Those who, if We granted them power in the land, will maintain the prayer, give the zakat, bid what is right and forbid what is wrong. And with Allah rests the outcome of all matters.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:50
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/50
> فَالَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا وَ عَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ‌ لَهُمْ‌ مَغْفِرَةٌ وَ رِزْقٌ‌ کَرِيمٌ‌
>
> As for those who have faith and do righteous deeds, for them will be forgiveness and a noble provision.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:55
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/55
> وَ لاَ يَزَالُ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا فِي‌ مِرْيَةٍ مِنْهُ‌ حَتَّى‌ تَأْتِيَهُمُ‌ السَّاعَةُ بَغْتَةً أَوْ يَأْتِيَهُمْ‌ عَذَابُ‌ يَوْمٍ‌ عَقِيمٍ‌
>
> Those who are faithless persist in their doubt about it, until either the Hour overtakes them suddenly, or they are overtaken by the punishment of an inauspicious day.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:59
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/59
> لَيُدْخِلَنَّهُمْ‌ مُدْخَلاً يَرْضَوْنَهُ‌ وَ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لَعَلِيمٌ‌ حَلِيمٌ‌
>
> He will admit them into an abode they are pleased with. Indeed Allah is all-knowing, all-forbearing.

### Qur'an · Al-Hajj (The Pilgrimage) · 22:60
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/22/60
> ذٰلِکَ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ عَاقَبَ‌ بِمِثْلِ‌ مَا عُوقِبَ‌ بِهِ‌ ثُمَ‌ بُغِيَ‌ عَلَيْهِ‌ لَيَنْصُرَنَّهُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لَعَفُوٌّ غَفُورٌ
>
> So will it be; and whoever retaliates with the like of what he has been made to suffer, and is aggressed against [again] thereafter, Allah will surely help him. Indeed Allah is all-excusing, all-forgiving.

### Qur'an · Al-Muminoon (The Believers) · 23:2
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/23/2
> الَّذِينَ‌ هُمْ‌ فِي‌ صَلاَتِهِمْ‌ خَاشِعُونَ‌
>
> —those who are humble in their prayers,

### Qur'an · Al-Muminoon (The Believers) · 23:9
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/23/9
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ هُمْ‌ عَلَى‌ صَلَوَاتِهِمْ‌ يُحَافِظُونَ‌
>
> and are watchful of their prayers.

### Qur'an · Al-Muminoon (The Believers) · 23:71
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/23/71
> وَ لَوِ اتَّبَعَ‌ الْحَقُ‌ أَهْوَاءَهُمْ‌ لَفَسَدَتِ‌ السَّمَاوَاتُ‌ وَ الْأَرْضُ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ فِيهِنَ‌ بَلْ‌ أَتَيْنَاهُمْ‌ بِذِکْرِهِمْ‌ فَهُمْ‌ عَنْ‌ ذِکْرِهِمْ‌ مُعْرِضُونَ‌
>
> Had the truth followed their desires, the heavens and the earth would have surely fallen apart [along] with those who are in them. Indeed, We have brought them their Reminder, but they are disregardful of their Reminder.

### Qur'an · Al-Muminoon (The Believers) · 23:75
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/23/75
> وَ لَوْ رَحِمْنَاهُمْ‌ وَ کَشَفْنَا مَا بِهِمْ‌ مِنْ‌ ضُرٍّ لَلَجُّوا فِي‌ طُغْيَانِهِمْ‌ يَعْمَهُونَ‌
>
> Should We have mercy upon them and remove their distress, they would surely persist, bewildered in their rebellion.

### Qur'an · Al-Muminoon (The Believers) · 23:78
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/23/78
> وَ هُوَ الَّذِي‌ أَنْشَأَ لَکُمُ‌ السَّمْعَ‌ وَ الْأَبْصَارَ وَ الْأَفْئِدَةَ قَلِيلاً مَا تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> It is He who made for you hearing, eyesight, and hearts. Little do you thank.

### Qur'an · Al-Muminoon (The Believers) · 23:109
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/23/109
> إِنَّهُ‌ کَانَ‌ فَرِيقٌ‌ مِنْ‌ عِبَادِي‌ يَقُولُونَ‌ رَبَّنَا آمَنَّا فَاغْفِرْ لَنَا وَ ارْحَمْنَا وَ أَنْتَ‌ خَيْرُ الرَّاحِمِينَ‌
>
> Indeed there was a part of My servants who would say, ‘‘Our Lord! We have believed. So forgive us, and have mercy on us, and You are the best of the merciful.’’

### Qur'an · Al-Muminoon (The Believers) · 23:111
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/23/111
> إِنِّي‌ جَزَيْتُهُمُ‌ الْيَوْمَ‌ بِمَا صَبَرُوا أَنَّهُمْ‌ هُمُ‌ الْفَائِزُونَ‌
>
> Indeed I have rewarded them today for their patience. They are indeed the triumphant.’

### Qur'an · Al-Muminoon (The Believers) · 23:118
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/23/118
> وَ قُلْ‌ رَبِ‌ اغْفِرْ وَ ارْحَمْ‌ وَ أَنْتَ‌ خَيْرُ الرَّاحِمِينَ‌
>
> Say, ‘My Lord, forgive and have mercy, and You are the best of the merciful.’

### Qur'an · An-Noor (The Light) · 24:5
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/24/5
> إِلاَّ الَّذِينَ‌ تَابُوا مِنْ‌ بَعْدِ ذٰلِکَ‌ وَ أَصْلَحُوا فَإِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> excepting those who repent after that and reform, for Allah is indeed all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · An-Noor (The Light) · 24:22
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/24/22
> وَ لاَ يَأْتَلِ‌ أُولُوا الْفَضْلِ‌ مِنْکُمْ‌ وَ السَّعَةِ أَنْ‌ يُؤْتُوا أُولِي‌ الْقُرْبَى‌ وَ الْمَسَاکِينَ‌ وَ الْمُهَاجِرِينَ‌ فِي‌ سَبِيلِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ لْيَعْفُوا وَ لْيَصْفَحُوا أَ لاَ تُحِبُّونَ‌ أَنْ‌ يَغْفِرَ اللَّهُ‌ لَکُمْ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> The well-off and the opulent among you should not vow that they will not give [any longer] to the relatives and the needy, and to those who have migrated in the way of Allah, and let them excuse and forbear. Do you not love that Allah should forgive you? Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · An-Noor (The Light) · 24:26
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/24/26
> الْخَبِيثَاتُ‌ لِلْخَبِيثِينَ‌ وَ الْخَبِيثُونَ‌ لِلْخَبِيثَاتِ‌ وَ الطَّيِّبَاتُ‌ لِلطَّيِّبِينَ‌ وَ الطَّيِّبُونَ‌ لِلطَّيِّبَاتِ‌ أُولٰئِکَ‌ مُبَرَّءُونَ‌ مِمَّا يَقُولُونَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ مَغْفِرَةٌ وَ رِزْقٌ‌ کَرِيمٌ‌
>
> Vicious women are for vicious men, and vicious men for vicious women. Good women are for good men, and good men for good women. These are absolved of what they say [about them]. For them is forgiveness and a noble provision.

### Qur'an · An-Noor (The Light) · 24:31
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance, self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/24/31
> وَ قُلْ‌ لِلْمُؤْمِنَاتِ‌ يَغْضُضْنَ‌ مِنْ‌ أَبْصَارِهِنَ‌ وَ يَحْفَظْنَ‌ فُرُوجَهُنَ‌ وَ لاَ يُبْدِينَ‌ زِينَتَهُنَ‌ إِلاَّ مَا ظَهَرَ مِنْهَا وَ لْيَضْرِبْنَ‌ بِخُمُرِهِنَ‌ عَلَى‌ جُيُوبِهِنَ‌ وَ لاَ يُبْدِينَ‌ زِينَتَهُنَ‌ إِلاَّ لِبُعُولَتِهِنَ‌ أَوْ آبَائِهِنَ‌ أَوْ آبَاءِ بُعُولَتِهِنَ‌ أَوْ أَبْنَائِهِنَ‌ أَوْ أَبْنَاءِ بُعُولَتِهِنَ‌ أَوْ إِخْوَانِهِنَ‌ أَوْ بَنِي‌ إِخْوَانِهِنَ‌ أَوْ بَنِي‌ أَخَوَاتِهِنَ‌ أَوْ نِسَائِهِنَ‌ أَوْ مَا مَلَکَتْ‌ أَيْمَانُهُنَ‌ أَوِ التَّابِعِينَ‌ غَيْرِ أُولِي‌ الْإِرْبَةِ مِنَ‌ الرِّجَالِ‌ أَوِ الطِّفْلِ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ لَمْ‌ يَظْهَرُوا عَلَى‌ عَوْرَاتِ‌ النِّسَاءِ وَ لاَ يَضْرِبْنَ‌ بِأَرْجُلِهِنَ‌ لِيُعْلَمَ‌ مَا يُخْفِينَ‌ مِنْ‌ زِينَتِهِنَ‌ وَ تُوبُوا إِلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ جَمِيعاً أَيُّهَا الْمُؤْمِنُونَ‌ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تُفْلِحُونَ‌
>
> And tell the faithful women to cast down their looks and to guard their private parts, and not to display their charms, beyond what is [acceptably] visible, and let them draw their scarfs over their bosoms, and not display their charms except to their husbands, or their fathers, or their husband’s fathers, or their sons, or their husband’s sons, or their brothers, or their brothers’ sons, or their sisters’ sons, or their women, or their slave girls, or male dependants lacking [sexual] desire, or children who are not yet conscious of female sexuality. And let them not thump their feet to make known their hidden ornaments. Rally to Allah in repentance, O faithful, so that you may be felicitous.

### Qur'an · An-Noor (The Light) · 24:37
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/24/37
> رِجَالٌ‌ لاَ تُلْهِيهِمْ‌ تِجَارَةٌ وَ لاَ بَيْعٌ‌ عَنْ‌ ذِکْرِ اللَّهِ‌ وَ إِقَامِ‌ الصَّلاَةِ وَ إِيتَاءِ الزَّکَاةِ يَخَافُونَ‌ يَوْماً تَتَقَلَّبُ‌ فِيهِ‌ الْقُلُوبُ‌ وَ الْأَبْصَارُ
>
> by men whom neither trade nor bargaining distracts from the remembrance of Allah and the maintenance of prayer and the giving of zakat. They are fearful of a day wherein the hearts and the sights will be transformed,

### Qur'an · An-Noor (The Light) · 24:41
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/24/41
> أَ لَمْ‌ تَرَ أَنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ يُسَبِّحُ‌ لَهُ‌ مَنْ‌ فِي‌ السَّمَاوَاتِ‌ وَ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ الطَّيْرُ صَافَّاتٍ‌ کُلٌ‌ قَدْ عَلِمَ‌ صَلاَتَهُ‌ وَ تَسْبِيحَهُ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ عَلِيمٌ‌ بِمَا يَفْعَلُونَ‌
>
> Have you not regarded that Allah is glorified by everyone in the heavens and the earth, and the birds spreading their wings. Each knows his prayer and glorification, and Allah knows best what they do.

### Qur'an · An-Noor (The Light) · 24:56
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/24/56
> وَ أَقِيمُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ آتُوا الزَّکَاةَ وَ أَطِيعُوا الرَّسُولَ‌ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تُرْحَمُونَ‌
>
> Maintain the prayer and give the zakat, and obey the Apostle so that you may receive [Allah’s] mercy.

### Qur'an · Al-Furqaan (The Criterion) · 25:6

>
> Say, ‘It has been sent down by Him who knows the hidden in the heavens and the earth. Indeed He is all-forgiving, all-merciful.’

### Qur'an · Al-Furqaan (The Criterion) · 25:13
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī

>
> And when they are cast into a narrow place in it, bound together [in chains], they will pray for [their own] annihilation.

### Qur'an · Al-Furqaan (The Criterion) · 25:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī

>
> [They will be told:] ‘Do not pray for a single annihilation today, but pray for many annihilations!’

### Qur'an · Al-Furqaan (The Criterion) · 25:20
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī

> وَ مَا أَرْسَلْنَا قَبْلَکَ‌ مِنَ‌ الْمُرْسَلِينَ‌ إِلاَّ إِنَّهُمْ‌ لَيَأْکُلُونَ‌ الطَّعَامَ‌ وَ يَمْشُونَ‌ فِي‌ الْأَسْوَاقِ‌ وَ جَعَلْنَا بَعْضَکُمْ لِبَعْضٍ‌ فِتْنَةً أَ تَصْبِرُونَ‌ وَ کَانَ‌ رَبُّکَ‌ بَصِيراً
>
> We did not send any apostles before you but that they indeed ate food and walked in marketplaces. We have made you a [means of] test for one another, [to see] if you will be patient and steadfast, and your Lord is all-seeing.

### Qur'an · An-Naml (The Ant) · 27:19
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/27/19
> فَتَبَسَّمَ‌ ضَاحِکاً مِنْ‌ قَوْلِهَا وَ قَالَ‌ رَبِ‌ أَوْزِعْنِي‌ أَنْ‌ أَشْکُرَ نِعْمَتَکَ‌ الَّتِي‌ أَنْعَمْتَ‌ عَلَيَ‌ وَ عَلَى‌ وَالِدَيَ‌ وَ أَنْ‌ أَعْمَلَ‌ صَالِحاً تَرْضَاهُ‌ وَ أَدْخِلْنِي‌ بِرَحْمَتِکَ‌ فِي‌ عِبَادِکَ‌ الصَّالِحِينَ‌
>
> Whereat he smiled, amused at its words, and he said, ‘My Lord! Inspire me to give thanks for Your blessing with which You have blessed me and my parents, and that I may do righteous deeds which please You, and admit me, by Your mercy, among Your righteous servants.’

### Qur'an · An-Naml (The Ant) · 27:40
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/27/40
> قَالَ‌ الَّذِي‌ عِنْدَهُ‌ عِلْمٌ‌ مِنَ‌ الْکِتَابِ‌ أَنَا آتِيکَ‌ بِهِ‌ قَبْلَ‌ أَنْ‌ يَرْتَدَّ إِلَيْکَ‌ طَرْفُکَ‌ فَلَمَّا رَآهُ‌ مُسْتَقِرّاً عِنْدَهُ‌ قَالَ‌ هٰذَا مِنْ‌ فَضْلِ‌ رَبِّي‌ لِيَبْلُوَنِي‌ أَ أَشْکُرُ أَمْ‌ أَکْفُرُ وَ مَنْ‌ شَکَرَ فَإِنَّمَا يَشْکُرُ لِنَفْسِهِ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ کَفَرَ فَإِنَ‌ رَبِّي‌ غَنِيٌ‌ کَرِيمٌ‌
>
> The one who had knowledge of the Book said, ‘I will bring it to you in the twinkling of an eye.’ So when he saw it set near him, he said, ‘This is by the grace of my Lord, to test me if I will give thanks or be ungrateful. Whoever gives thanks, gives thanks only for his own sake. And whoever is ungrateful [should know that] my Lord is indeed all-sufficient, all-generous.’

### Qur'an · An-Naml (The Ant) · 27:46
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/27/46
> قَالَ‌ يَا قَوْمِ‌ لِمَ‌ تَسْتَعْجِلُونَ‌ بِالسَّيِّئَةِ قَبْلَ‌ الْحَسَنَةِ لَوْ لاَ تَسْتَغْفِرُونَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تُرْحَمُونَ‌
>
> He said, ‘O My people! Why do you press for evil sooner than for good? Why do you not plead to Allah for forgiveness so that you may receive His mercy?’

### Qur'an · An-Naml (The Ant) · 27:55
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī

>
> Do you approach men with [sexual] desire instead of women?! Indeed, you are a senseless lot!’

>

### Qur'an · Al-Qasas (The Stories) · 28:16

>
> He said, ‘My Lord! I have wronged myself. Forgive me!’ So He forgave him. Indeed, He is the All-forgiving, the All-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Qasas (The Stories) · 28:19
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/28/19
> فَلَمَّا أَنْ‌ أَرَادَ أَنْ‌ يَبْطِشَ‌ بِالَّذِي‌ هُوَ عَدُوٌّ لَهُمَا قَالَ‌ يَا مُوسَى‌ أَ تُرِيدُ أَنْ‌ تَقْتُلَنِي‌ کَمَا قَتَلْتَ‌ نَفْساً بِالْأَمْسِ‌ إِنْ‌ تُرِيدُ إِلاَّ أَنْ‌ تَکُونَ‌ جَبَّاراً فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ مَا تُرِيدُ أَنْ‌ تَکُونَ‌ مِنَ‌ الْمُصْلِحِينَ‌
>
> But when he wanted to strike him who was an enemy of both of them, he said, ‘Moses, do you want to kill me, just like the one you killed yesterday? You only want to be a tyrant in the land, and you do not desire to be one who brings about reform.’

### Qur'an · Al-Qasas (The Stories) · 28:27
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/28/27
> قَالَ‌ إِنِّي‌ أُرِيدُ أَنْ‌ أُنْکِحَکَ‌ إِحْدَى‌ ابْنَتَيَ‌ هَاتَيْنِ‌ عَلَى‌ أَنْ‌ تَأْجُرَنِي‌ ثَمَانِيَ‌ حِجَجٍ‌ فَإِنْ‌ أَتْمَمْتَ‌ عَشْراً فَمِنْ‌ عِنْدِکَ‌ وَ مَا أُرِيدُ أَنْ‌ أَشُقَ‌ عَلَيْکَ‌ سَتَجِدُنِي‌ إِنْ‌ شَاءَ اللَّهُ‌ مِنَ‌ الصَّالِحِينَ‌
>
> He said, ‘Indeed I desire to marry you to one of these two daughters of mine, on condition that you hire yourself to me for eight years. And if you complete ten, that will be up to you, and I do not want to be hard on you. God willing, you will find me to a righteous person.’

themes: self-control

>
> Then if they do not respond to you[r] [summons], know that they only follow their desires, and who is more astray than him who follows his desires without any guidance from Allah? Indeed Allah does not guide the wrongdoing lot.

> Those who were given knowledge said [to them], ‘Woe to you! Allah’s reward is better for someone who has faith and acts righteously, and no one will receive it except the patient.’

themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/28/83
> تِلْکَ‌ الدَّارُ الْآخِرَةُ نَجْعَلُهَا لِلَّذِينَ‌ لاَ يُرِيدُونَ‌ عُلُوّاً فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ لاَ فَسَاداً وَ الْعَاقِبَةُ لِلْمُتَّقِينَ‌
>
> This is the abode of the Hereafter, which We shall grant to those who do not desire to domineer in the earth nor to cause corruption, and the outcome will be in favour of the Godwary.

### Qur'an · Al-Ankaboot (The Spider) · 29:6
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/29/6
> وَ مَنْ‌ جَاهَدَ فَإِنَّمَا يُجَاهِدُ لِنَفْسِهِ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لَغَنِيٌ‌ عَنِ‌ الْعَالَمِينَ‌
>
> Whoever strives, strives only for his own sake. Indeed Allah has no need of the creatures.

### Qur'an · Al-Ankaboot (The Spider) · 29:17
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/29/17
> إِنَّمَا تَعْبُدُونَ‌ مِنْ‌ دُونِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ أَوْثَاناً وَ تَخْلُقُونَ‌ إِفْکاً إِنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ تَعْبُدُونَ‌ مِنْ‌ دُونِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ لاَ يَمْلِکُونَ‌ لَکُمْ‌ رِزْقاً فَابْتَغُوا عِنْدَ اللَّهِ‌ الرِّزْقَ‌ وَ اعْبُدُوهُ‌ وَ اشْکُرُوا لَهُ‌ إِلَيْهِ‌ تُرْجَعُونَ‌
>
> What you worship instead of Allah are mere idols, and you invent a lie. Indeed those whom you worship besides Allah have no control over your provision. So seek all [your] provision from Allah, and worship Him and thank Him, and to Him you shall be brought back.’

### Qur'an · Al-Ankaboot (The Spider) · 29:23
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/29/23
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا بِآيَاتِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ لِقَائِهِ‌ أُولٰئِکَ‌ يَئِسُوا مِنْ‌ رَحْمَتِي‌ وَ أُولٰئِکَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ عَذَابٌ‌ أَلِيمٌ‌
>
> Those who deny the signs of Allah and the encounter with Him—they have despaired of My mercy, and for such there is a painful punishment.

### Qur'an · Al-Ankaboot (The Spider) · 29:45
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/29/45
> اتْلُ‌ مَا أُوحِيَ‌ إِلَيْکَ‌ مِنَ‌ الْکِتَابِ‌ وَ أَقِمِ‌ الصَّلاَةَ إِنَ‌ الصَّلاَةَ تَنْهَى‌ عَنِ‌ الْفَحْشَاءِ وَ الْمُنْکَرِ وَ لَذِکْرُ اللَّهِ‌ أَکْبَرُ وَ اللَّهُ‌ يَعْلَمُ‌ مَا تَصْنَعُونَ‌
>
> Recite what has been revealed to you of the Book, and maintain the prayer. Indeed the prayer restrains from indecent and wrongful conduct, and the remembrance of Allah is surely greater. And Allah knows whatever [deeds] you do.

### Qur'an · Al-Ankaboot (The Spider) · 29:59
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/29/59
> الَّذِينَ‌ صَبَرُوا وَ عَلَى‌ رَبِّهِمْ‌ يَتَوَکَّلُونَ‌
>
> —Those who are patient and who put their trust in their Lord.

### Qur'an · Al-Ankaboot (The Spider) · 29:69
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/29/69
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ جَاهَدُوا فِينَا لَنَهْدِيَنَّهُمْ‌ سُبُلَنَا وَ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لَمَعَ‌ الْمُحْسِنِينَ‌
>
> As for those who strive in Us, We shall surely guide them in Our ways, and Allah is indeed with the virtuous.

### Qur'an · Ar-Room (The Romans) · 30:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/30/12
> وَ يَوْمَ‌ تَقُومُ‌ السَّاعَةُ يُبْلِسُ‌ الْمُجْرِمُونَ‌
>
> And when the Hour sets in, the guilty will despair.

### Qur'an · Ar-Room (The Romans) · 30:24
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/30/24
> وَ مِنْ‌ آيَاتِهِ‌ يُرِيکُمُ‌ الْبَرْقَ‌ خَوْفاً وَ طَمَعاً وَ يُنَزِّلُ‌ مِنَ‌ السَّمَاءِ مَاءً فَيُحْيِي‌ بِهِ‌ الْأَرْضَ‌ بَعْدَ مَوْتِهَا إِنَ‌ فِي‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ لَآيَاتٍ‌ لِقَوْمٍ‌ يَعْقِلُونَ‌
>
> And of His signs is that He shows you the lightning, arousing fear and hope, and He sends down water from the sky, and with it revives the earth after its death. There are indeed signs in that for people who exercise their reason.

### Qur'an · Ar-Room (The Romans) · 30:29
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/30/29
> بَلِ‌ اتَّبَعَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ ظَلَمُوا أَهْوَاءَهُمْ‌ بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ‌ فَمَنْ‌ يَهْدِي‌ مَنْ‌ أَضَلَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ وَ مَا لَهُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ نَاصِرِينَ‌
>
> Indeed, the wrongdoers follow their own desires without any knowledge. So who will guide those whom Allah has led astray? They will have no helpers.

### Qur'an · Ar-Room (The Romans) · 30:31
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/30/31
> مُنِيبِينَ‌ إِلَيْهِ‌ وَ اتَّقُوهُ‌ وَ أَقِيمُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ لاَ تَکُونُوا مِنَ‌ الْمُشْرِکِينَ‌
>
> —turning to Him in penitence, and be wary of Him, and maintain the prayer, and do not be one of the polytheists

### Qur'an · Ar-Room (The Romans) · 30:33
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/30/33
> وَ إِذَا مَسَ‌ النَّاسَ‌ ضُرٌّ دَعَوْا رَبَّهُمْ‌ مُنِيبِينَ‌ إِلَيْهِ‌ ثُمَ‌ إِذَا أَذَاقَهُمْ‌ مِنْهُ‌ رَحْمَةً إِذَا فَرِيقٌ‌ مِنْهُمْ‌ بِرَبِّهِمْ‌ يُشْرِکُونَ‌
>
> When distress befalls people, they supplicate their Lord, turning to Him in penitence. Then, when He lets them taste His mercy, behold, a part of them ascribe partners to their Lord,

### Qur'an · Ar-Room (The Romans) · 30:46
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/30/46
> وَ مِنْ‌ آيَاتِهِ‌ أَنْ‌ يُرْسِلَ‌ الرِّيَاحَ‌ مُبَشِّرَاتٍ‌ وَ لِيُذِيقَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ رَحْمَتِهِ‌ وَ لِتَجْرِيَ‌ الْفُلْکُ‌ بِأَمْرِهِ‌ وَ لِتَبْتَغُوا مِنْ‌ فَضْلِهِ‌ وَ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> And of His signs is that He sends the winds as bearers of good news and to let you taste of His mercy, and that the ships may sail by His command, and that you may seek of His bounty, and so that you may give [Him] thanks.

### Qur'an · Ar-Room (The Romans) · 30:60
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/30/60
> فَاصْبِرْ إِنَ‌ وَعْدَ اللَّهِ‌ حَقٌ‌ وَ لاَ يَسْتَخِفَّنَّکَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ لاَ يُوقِنُونَ‌
>
> So be patient! Allah’s promise is indeed true. And do not let yourself be upset by those who have no conviction.

### Qur'an · Luqman (Luqman) · 31:4
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/31/4
> الَّذِينَ‌ يُقِيمُونَ‌ الصَّلاَةَ وَ يُؤْتُونَ‌ الزَّکَاةَ وَ هُمْ‌ بِالْآخِرَةِ هُمْ‌ يُوقِنُونَ‌
>
> who maintain the prayer, pay the zakat, and are certain of the Hereafter.

### Qur'an · Luqman (Luqman) · 31:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/31/12
> وَ لَقَدْ آتَيْنَا لُقْمَانَ‌ الْحِکْمَةَ أَنِ‌ اشْکُرْ لِلَّهِ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ يَشْکُرْ فَإِنَّمَا يَشْکُرُ لِنَفْسِهِ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ کَفَرَ فَإِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَنِيٌ‌ حَمِيدٌ
>
> Certainly We gave Luqman wisdom, saying, ‘Give thanks to Allah; and whoever gives thanks, gives thanks only for his own sake. And whoever is ungrateful, [let him know that] Allah is indeed all-sufficient, all-laudable.’

### Qur'an · Luqman (Luqman) · 31:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/31/14
> وَ وَصَّيْنَا الْإِنْسَانَ‌ بِوَالِدَيْهِ‌ حَمَلَتْهُ‌ أُمُّهُ‌ وَهْناً عَلَى‌ وَهْنٍ‌ وَ فِصَالُهُ‌ فِي‌ عَامَيْنِ‌ أَنِ‌ اشْکُرْ لِي‌ وَ لِوَالِدَيْکَ‌ إِلَيَ‌ الْمَصِيرُ
>
> We have enjoined man concerning his parents: His mother carried him through weakness upon weakness, and his weaning takes two years. Give thanks to Me and to your parents. To Me is the return.

### Qur'an · Luqman (Luqman) · 31:31
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/31/31
> أَ لَمْ‌ تَرَ أَنَ‌ الْفُلْکَ‌ تَجْرِي‌ فِي‌ الْبَحْرِ بِنِعْمَةِ اللَّهِ‌ لِيُرِيَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ آيَاتِهِ‌ إِنَ‌ فِي‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ لَآيَاتٍ‌ لِکُلِ‌ صَبَّارٍ شَکُورٍ
>
> Have you not regarded that the ships sail at sea with Allah’s blessing, that He may show you some of His signs? There are indeed signs in that for every patient and grateful [servant].

### Qur'an · As-Sajda (The Prostration) · 32:9
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/32/9
> ثُمَ‌ سَوَّاهُ‌ وَ نَفَخَ‌ فِيهِ‌ مِنْ‌ رُوحِهِ‌ وَ جَعَلَ‌ لَکُمُ‌ السَّمْعَ‌ وَ الْأَبْصَارَ وَ الْأَفْئِدَةَ قَلِيلاً مَا تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> Then He proportioned him and breathed into him of His Spirit, and made for you hearing, sight, and hearts. Little do you thank.

### Qur'an · As-Sajda (The Prostration) · 32:16
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/32/16
> تَتَجَافَى‌ جُنُوبُهُمْ‌ عَنِ‌ الْمَضَاجِعِ‌ يَدْعُونَ‌ رَبَّهُمْ‌ خَوْفاً وَ طَمَعاً وَ مِمَّا رَزَقْنَاهُمْ‌ يُنْفِقُونَ‌
>
> Their sides vacate their beds to supplicate their Lord in fear and hope, and they spend out of what We have provided them.

### Qur'an · As-Sajda (The Prostration) · 32:24
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/32/24
> وَ جَعَلْنَا مِنْهُمْ‌ أَئِمَّةً يَهْدُونَ‌ بِأَمْرِنَا لَمَّا صَبَرُوا وَ کَانُوا بِآيَاتِنَا يُوقِنُونَ‌
>
> When they had been patient and had conviction in Our signs, We appointed amongst them imams to guide [the people] by Our command.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:5
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/5
> ادْعُوهُمْ‌ لِآبَائِهِمْ‌ هُوَ أَقْسَطُ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ‌ فَإِنْ‌ لَمْ‌ تَعْلَمُوا آبَاءَهُمْ‌ فَإِخْوَانُکُمْ‌ فِي‌ الدِّينِ‌ وَ مَوَالِيکُمْ‌ وَ لَيْسَ‌ عَلَيْکُمْ‌ جُنَاحٌ‌ فِيمَا أَخْطَأْتُمْ‌ بِهِ‌ وَ لٰکِنْ‌ مَا تَعَمَّدَتْ‌ قُلُوبُکُمْ‌ وَ کَانَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُوراً رَحِيماً
>
> Call them after their fathers. That is more just with Allah. If you do not know their fathers, then they are your brethren in the faith and your kinsmen. Excepting what your hearts may intend deliberately, there will be no sin upon you for any mistake that you may make therein. And Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:17
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/17
> قُلْ‌ مَنْ‌ ذَا الَّذِي‌ يَعْصِمُکُمْ‌ مِنَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ إِنْ‌ أَرَادَ بِکُمْ‌ سُوءاً أَوْ أَرَادَ بِکُمْ‌ رَحْمَةً وَ لاَ يَجِدُونَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ دُونِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَلِيّاً وَ لاَ نَصِيراً
>
> Say, ‘Who is it that can protect you from Allah if He desires to cause you harm or desires to grant you His mercy?’ Besides Allah they will not find for themselves any protector or helper.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:24
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/24
> لِيَجْزِيَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ الصَّادِقِينَ‌ بِصِدْقِهِمْ‌ وَ يُعَذِّبَ‌ الْمُنَافِقِينَ‌ إِنْ‌ شَاءَ أَوْ يَتُوبَ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ کَانَ‌ غَفُوراً رَحِيماً
>
> that Allah may reward the true for their truthfulness, and punish the hypocrites, if He wishes, or accept their repentance. Indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:28
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/28
> يَا أَيُّهَا النَّبِيُ‌ قُلْ‌ لِأَزْوَاجِکَ‌ إِنْ‌ کُنْتُنَ‌ تُرِدْنَ‌ الْحَيَاةَ الدُّنْيَا وَ زِينَتَهَا فَتَعَالَيْنَ‌ أُمَتِّعْکُنَ‌ وَ أُسَرِّحْکُنَ‌ سَرَاحاً جَمِيلاً
>
> O Prophet! Say to your wives, ‘If you desire the life of the world and its glitter, come, I will provide for you and release you in a graceful manner.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:29
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/29
> وَ إِنْ‌ کُنْتُنَ‌ تُرِدْنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ وَ رَسُولَهُ‌ وَ الدَّارَ الْآخِرَةَ فَإِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ أَعَدَّ لِلْمُحْسِنَاتِ‌ مِنْکُنَ‌ أَجْراً عَظِيماً
>
> But if you desire Allah and His Apostle and the abode of the Hereafter, then Allah has indeed prepared a great reward for the virtuous among you.’

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:33
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/33
> وَ قَرْنَ‌ فِي‌ بُيُوتِکُنَ‌ وَ لاَ تَبَرَّجْنَ‌ تَبَرُّجَ‌ الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ الْأُولَى‌ وَ أَقِمْنَ‌ الصَّلاَةَ وَ آتِينَ‌ الزَّکَاةَ وَ أَطِعْنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ وَ رَسُولَهُ‌ إِنَّمَا يُرِيدُ اللَّهُ‌ لِيُذْهِبَ‌ عَنْکُمُ‌ الرِّجْسَ‌ أَهْلَ‌ الْبَيْتِ‌ وَ يُطَهِّرَکُمْ‌ تَطْهِيراً
>
> Stay in your houses and do not flaunt your finery like the former [days of pagan] ignorance. Maintain the prayer and pay the zakat, and obey Allah and His Apostle. Indeed Allah desires to repel all impurity from you, O People of the Household, and purify you with a thorough purification.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:35
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/35
> إِنَ‌ الْمُسْلِمِينَ‌ وَ الْمُسْلِمَاتِ‌ وَ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌ وَ الْمُؤْمِنَاتِ‌ وَ الْقَانِتِينَ‌ وَ الْقَانِتَاتِ‌ وَ الصَّادِقِينَ‌ وَ الصَّادِقَاتِ‌ وَ الصَّابِرِينَ‌ وَ الصَّابِرَاتِ‌ وَ الْخَاشِعِينَ‌ وَ الْخَاشِعَاتِ‌ وَ الْمُتَصَدِّقِينَ‌ وَ الْمُتَصَدِّقَاتِ‌ وَ الصَّائِمِينَ‌ وَ الصَّائِمَاتِ‌ وَ الْحَافِظِينَ‌ فُرُوجَهُمْ‌ وَ الْحَافِظَاتِ‌ وَ الذَّاکِرِينَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ کَثِيراً وَ الذَّاکِرَاتِ‌ أَعَدَّ اللَّهُ‌ لَهُمْ‌ مَغْفِرَةً وَ أَجْراً عَظِيماً
>
> Indeed the muslim men and the muslim women, the faithful men and the faithful women, the obedient men and the obedient women, the truthful men and the truthful women, the patient men and the patient women, the humble men and the humble women, the charitable men and the charitable women, the men who fast and the women who fast, the men who guard their private parts and the women who guard, the men who remember Allah greatly and the women who remember [Allah greatly]—Allah holds in store for them forgiveness and a great reward.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:51
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/51
> تُرْجِي‌ مَنْ‌ تَشَاءُ مِنْهُنَ‌ وَ تُؤْوِي‌ إِلَيْکَ‌ مَنْ‌ تَشَاءُ وَ مَنِ‌ ابْتَغَيْتَ‌ مِمَّنْ‌ عَزَلْتَ‌ فَلاَ جُنَاحَ‌ عَلَيْکَ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ أَدْنَى‌ أَنْ‌ تَقَرَّ أَعْيُنُهُنَ‌ وَ لاَ يَحْزَنَ‌ وَ يَرْضَيْنَ‌ بِمَا آتَيْتَهُنَ‌ کُلُّهُنَ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ يَعْلَمُ‌ مَا فِي‌ قُلُوبِکُمْ‌ وَ کَانَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَلِيماً حَلِيماً
>
> You may put off whichever of them you wish and consort with whichever of them you wish, and there is no sin upon you [in receiving again] any [of them] whom you may seek [to consort with] from among those you have set aside [earlier]. That makes it likelier that they will be comforted and not feel unhappy, and all of them will be pleased with what you give them. Allah knows what is in your hearts, and Allah is all-knowing, all-forbearing.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:59
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/59
> يَا أَيُّهَا النَّبِيُ‌ قُلْ‌ لِأَزْوَاجِکَ‌ وَ بَنَاتِکَ‌ وَ نِسَاءِ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌ يُدْنِينَ‌ عَلَيْهِنَ‌ مِنْ‌ جَلاَبِيبِهِنَ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ أَدْنَى‌ أَنْ‌ يُعْرَفْنَ‌ فَلاَ يُؤْذَيْنَ‌ وَ کَانَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُوراً رَحِيماً
>
> O Prophet! Tell your wives and your daughters and the women of the faithful to draw closely over themselves their chadors [when going out]. That makes it likely for them to be recognized and not be troubled, and Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:71
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/71
> يُصْلِحْ‌ لَکُمْ‌ أَعْمَالَکُمْ‌ وَ يَغْفِرْ لَکُمْ‌ ذُنُوبَکُمْ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ يُطِعِ‌ اللَّهَ‌ وَ رَسُولَهُ‌ فَقَدْ فَازَ فَوْزاً عَظِيماً
>
> He will rectify your conduct for you and forgive you your sins. Whoever obeys Allah and His Apostle will certainly achieve a great success.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahzaab (The Clans) · 33:73
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/33/73
> لِيُعَذِّبَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ الْمُنَافِقِينَ‌ وَ الْمُنَافِقَاتِ‌ وَ الْمُشْرِکِينَ‌ وَ الْمُشْرِکَاتِ‌ وَ يَتُوبَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَلَى‌ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌ وَ الْمُؤْمِنَاتِ‌ وَ کَانَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُوراً رَحِيماً
>
> Allah will surely punish the hypocrites, men and women, and the polytheists, men and women, and Allah will turn clemently to the faithful, men and women, and Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Saba (Sheba) · 34:2
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/34/2
> يَعْلَمُ‌ مَا يَلِجُ‌ فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ مَا يَخْرُجُ‌ مِنْهَا وَ مَا يَنْزِلُ‌ مِنَ‌ السَّمَاءِ وَ مَا يَعْرُجُ‌ فِيهَا وَ هُوَ الرَّحِيمُ‌ الْغَفُورُ
>
> He knows whatever enters into the earth and whatever emerges from it, and whatever descends from the sky and whatever ascends into it, and He is the All-merciful, the All-forgiving.

### Qur'an · Saba (Sheba) · 34:4
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/34/4
> لِيَجْزِيَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا وَ عَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ‌ أُولٰئِکَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ مَغْفِرَةٌ وَ رِزْقٌ‌ کَرِيمٌ‌
>
> that He may reward those who have faith and do righteous deeds.’ For such there will be forgiveness and a noble provision.

### Qur'an · Saba (Sheba) · 34:15
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance, gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/34/15
> لَقَدْ کَانَ‌ لِسَبَإٍ فِي‌ مَسْکَنِهِمْ‌ آيَةٌ جَنَّتَانِ‌ عَنْ‌ يَمِينٍ‌ وَ شِمَالٍ‌ کُلُوا مِنْ‌ رِزْقِ‌ رَبِّکُمْ‌ وَ اشْکُرُوا لَهُ‌ بَلْدَةٌ طَيِّبَةٌ وَ رَبٌ‌ غَفُورٌ
>
> There was certainly a sign for Sheba in their habitation: two gardens, to the right and to the left. ‘Eat of the provision of your Lord and give Him thanks: a good land and an all-forgiving Lord!’

### Qur'an · Saba (Sheba) · 34:17
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/34/17
> ذٰلِکَ‌ جَزَيْنَاهُمْ‌ بِمَا کَفَرُوا وَ هَلْ‌ نُجَازِي‌ إِلاَّ الْکَفُورَ
>
> We requited them with that for their ingratitude. Do We not requite ingrates?

### Qur'an · Saba (Sheba) · 34:19
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/34/19
> فَقَالُوا رَبَّنَا بَاعِدْ بَيْنَ‌ أَسْفَارِنَا وَ ظَلَمُوا أَنْفُسَهُمْ‌ فَجَعَلْنَاهُمْ‌ أَحَادِيثَ‌ وَ مَزَّقْنَاهُمْ‌ کُلَ‌ مُمَزَّقٍ‌ إِنَ‌ فِي‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ لَآيَاتٍ‌ لِکُلِ‌ صَبَّارٍ شَکُورٍ
>
> But they said, ‘Our Lord! Make the stages between our journeys far apart,’ and they wronged themselves. So We turned them into folktales and caused them to disintegrate totally. There are indeed signs in that for every patient and grateful [servant].

### Qur'an · Saba (Sheba) · 34:43
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/34/43
> وَ إِذَا تُتْلَى‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ آيَاتُنَا بَيِّنَاتٍ‌ قَالُوا مَا هٰذَا إِلاَّ رَجُلٌ‌ يُرِيدُ أَنْ‌ يَصُدَّکُمْ‌ عَمَّا کَانَ‌ يَعْبُدُ آبَاؤُکُمْ‌ وَ قَالُوا مَا هٰذَا إِلاَّ إِفْکٌ‌ مُفْتَرًى‌ وَ قَالَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا لِلْحَقِ‌ لَمَّا جَاءَهُمْ‌ إِنْ‌ هٰذَا إِلاَّ سِحْرٌ مُبِينٌ‌
>
> When Our manifest signs are recited to them, they say, ‘This is just a man who desires to keep you from what your fathers used to worship.’ And they say, ‘This is nothing but a fabricated lie.’ The faithless say of the truth when it comes to them: ‘This is nothing but plain magic,’

### Qur'an · Saba (Sheba) · 34:54
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/34/54
> وَ حِيلَ‌ بَيْنَهُمْ‌ وَ بَيْنَ‌ مَا يَشْتَهُونَ‌ کَمَا فُعِلَ‌ بِأَشْيَاعِهِمْ‌ مِنْ‌ قَبْلُ‌ إِنَّهُمْ‌ کَانُوا فِي‌ شَکٍ‌ مُرِيبٍ‌
>
> and a barrier is set between them and what they desire, as was done aforetime with their likes, who had remained in grave doubt?

### Qur'an · Faatir (The Originator) · 35:7
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/35/7
> الَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا لَهُمْ‌ عَذَابٌ‌ شَدِيدٌ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا وَ عَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ‌ لَهُمْ‌ مَغْفِرَةٌ وَ أَجْرٌ کَبِيرٌ
>
> There is a severe punishment for the faithless; but for those who have faith and do righteous deeds, there will be forgiveness and a great reward.

### Qur'an · Faatir (The Originator) · 35:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/35/12
> وَ مَا يَسْتَوِي‌ الْبَحْرَانِ‌ هٰذَا عَذْبٌ‌ فُرَاتٌ‌ سَائِغٌ‌ شَرَابُهُ‌ وَ هٰذَا مِلْحٌ‌ أُجَاجٌ‌ وَ مِنْ‌ کُلٍ‌ تَأْکُلُونَ‌ لَحْماً طَرِيّاً وَ تَسْتَخْرِجُونَ‌ حِلْيَةً تَلْبَسُونَهَا وَ تَرَى‌ الْفُلْکَ‌ فِيهِ‌ مَوَاخِرَ لِتَبْتَغُوا مِنْ‌ فَضْلِهِ‌ وَ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> Not alike are the two seas: this one sweet and agreeable, pleasant to drink, and that one briny and bitter, and from each you eat fresh meat and obtain ornaments, which you wear. And you see the ships plowing through them, that you may seek of His bounty, and so that you may give thanks.

### Qur'an · Faatir (The Originator) · 35:18
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/35/18
> وَ لاَ تَزِرُ وَازِرَةٌ وِزْرَ أُخْرَى‌ وَ إِنْ‌ تَدْعُ‌ مُثْقَلَةٌ إِلَى‌ حِمْلِهَا لاَ يُحْمَلْ‌ مِنْهُ‌ شَيْ‌ءٌ وَ لَوْ کَانَ‌ ذَا قُرْبَى‌ إِنَّمَا تُنْذِرُ الَّذِينَ‌ يَخْشَوْنَ‌ رَبَّهُمْ‌ بِالْغَيْبِ‌ وَ أَقَامُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ مَنْ‌ تَزَکَّى‌ فَإِنَّمَا يَتَزَکَّى‌ لِنَفْسِهِ‌ وَ إِلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ الْمَصِيرُ
>
> No bearer shall bear another’s burden, and should someone heavily burdened call [another] to carry it, nothing of it will be carried [by anyone] even if he should be a near relative. You can only warn those who fear their Lord in secret and maintain the prayer. Whoever purifies himself, purifies only for his own sake, and to Allah is the return.

### Qur'an · Faatir (The Originator) · 35:28
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/35/28
> وَ مِنَ‌ النَّاسِ‌ وَ الدَّوَابِ‌ وَ الْأَنْعَامِ‌ مُخْتَلِفٌ‌ أَلْوَانُهُ‌ کَذٰلِکَ‌ إِنَّمَا يَخْشَى‌ اللَّهَ‌ مِنْ‌ عِبَادِهِ‌ الْعُلَمَاءُ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ عَزِيزٌ غَفُورٌ
>
> And of humans and beasts and cattle there are likewise diverse hues. Only those of Allah’s servants having knowledge fear Him. Indeed Allah is all-mighty, all-forgiving.

### Qur'an · Faatir (The Originator) · 35:29
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/35/29
> إِنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ يَتْلُونَ‌ کِتَابَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ أَقَامُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ أَنْفَقُوا مِمَّا رَزَقْنَاهُمْ‌ سِرّاً وَ عَلاَنِيَةً يَرْجُونَ‌ تِجَارَةً لَنْ‌ تَبُورَ
>
> Indeed those who recite the Book of Allah and maintain the prayer, and spend secretly and openly out of what We have provided them, expect a commerce that will never go bankrupt,

### Qur'an · Faatir (The Originator) · 35:30
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/35/30
> لِيُوَفِّيَهُمْ‌ أُجُورَهُمْ‌ وَ يَزِيدَهُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ فَضْلِهِ‌ إِنَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ شَکُورٌ
>
> so that He may pay them their full reward and enhance them out of His bounty. Indeed He is all-forgiving, all-appreciative.

### Qur'an · Faatir (The Originator) · 35:34
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/35/34
> وَ قَالُوا الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ‌ الَّذِي‌ أَذْهَبَ‌ عَنَّا الْحَزَنَ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبَّنَا لَغَفُورٌ شَکُورٌ
>
> They will say, ‘All praise belongs to Allah, who has removed all grief from us. Indeed Our Lord is all-forgiving, all-appreciative,

### Qur'an · Faatir (The Originator) · 35:36
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/35/36
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا لَهُمْ‌ نَارُ جَهَنَّمَ‌ لاَ يُقْضَى‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ فَيَمُوتُوا وَ لاَ يُخَفَّفُ‌ عَنْهُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ عَذَابِهَا کَذٰلِکَ‌ نَجْزِي‌ کُلَ‌ کَفُورٍ
>
> As for the faithless, there is for them the fire of hell: they will neither be done away with so that they may die, nor shall its punishment be lightened for them. Thus do We requite every ingrate.

### Qur'an · Faatir (The Originator) · 35:41
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/35/41
> إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ يُمْسِکُ‌ السَّمَاوَاتِ‌ وَ الْأَرْضَ‌ أَنْ‌ تَزُولاَ وَ لَئِنْ‌ زَالَتَا إِنْ‌ أَمْسَکَهُمَا مِنْ‌ أَحَدٍ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِهِ‌ إِنَّهُ‌ کَانَ‌ حَلِيماً غَفُوراً
>
> Indeed Allah sustains the heavens and the earth lest they should fall apart, and if they were to fall apart, there is none who can sustain them except Him. Indeed He is all-forbearing, all-forgiving.

### Qur'an · Yaseen (Yaseen) · 36:11
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/36/11
> إِنَّمَا تُنْذِرُ مَنِ‌ اتَّبَعَ‌ الذِّکْرَ وَ خَشِيَ‌ الرَّحْمٰنَ‌ بِالْغَيْبِ‌ فَبَشِّرْهُ‌ بِمَغْفِرَةٍ وَ أَجْرٍ کَرِيمٍ‌
>
> You can only warn someone who follows the Reminder and fears the All-beneficent in secret; so give him the good news of forgiveness and a noble reward.

### Qur'an · Yaseen (Yaseen) · 36:35
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/36/35
> لِيَأْکُلُوا مِنْ‌ ثَمَرِهِ‌ وَ مَا عَمِلَتْهُ‌ أَيْدِيهِمْ‌ أَ فَلاَ يَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> so that they may eat of its fruit and what their hands have cultivated. Will they not then give thanks?

### Qur'an · Yaseen (Yaseen) · 36:73
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī

>
> There are other benefits for them therein, and drinks. Will they not then give thanks?

>
> Is it a lie, gods other than Allah, that you desire?

### Qur'an · As-Saaffaat (Those drawn up in Ranks) · 37:101
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī

> فَبَشَّرْنَاهُ‌ بِغُلاَمٍ‌ حَلِيمٍ‌
>
> So We gave him the good news of a forbearing son.

### Qur'an · As-Saaffaat (Those drawn up in Ranks) · 37:102
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/37/102
> فَلَمَّا بَلَغَ‌ مَعَهُ‌ السَّعْيَ‌ قَالَ‌ يَا بُنَيَ‌ إِنِّي‌ أَرَى‌ فِي‌ الْمَنَامِ‌ أَنِّي‌ أَذْبَحُکَ‌ فَانْظُرْ مَا ذَا تَرَى‌ قَالَ‌ يَا أَبَتِ‌ افْعَلْ‌ مَا تُؤْمَرُ سَتَجِدُنِي‌ إِنْ‌ شَاءَ اللَّهُ‌ مِنَ‌ الصَّابِرِينَ‌
>
> When he was old enough to assist in his endeavour, he said, ‘My son! I see in dreams that I am sacrificing you. See what you think.’ He said, ‘Father! Do whatever you have been commanded. If Allah wishes, you will find me to be patient.’

### Qur'an · Saad (The letter Saad) · 38:17
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/38/17
> اصْبِرْ عَلَى‌ مَا يَقُولُونَ‌ وَ اذْکُرْ عَبْدَنَا دَاوُدَ ذَا الْأَيْدِ إِنَّهُ‌ أَوَّابٌ‌
>
> Be patient over what they say, and remember Our servant, David, [the man] of strength. Indeed he was a penitent [soul].

### Qur'an · Saad (The letter Saad) · 38:24
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/38/24
> قَالَ‌ لَقَدْ ظَلَمَکَ‌ بِسُؤَالِ‌ نَعْجَتِکَ‌ إِلَى‌ نِعَاجِهِ‌ وَ إِنَ‌ کَثِيراً مِنَ‌ الْخُلَطَاءِ لَيَبْغِي‌ بَعْضُهُمْ‌ عَلَى‌ بَعْضٍ‌ إِلاَّ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا وَ عَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ‌ وَ قَلِيلٌ‌ مَا هُمْ‌ وَ ظَنَ‌ دَاوُدُ أَنَّمَا فَتَنَّاهُ‌ فَاسْتَغْفَرَ رَبَّهُ‌ وَ خَرَّ رَاکِعاً وَ أَنَابَ‌
>
> He said, ‘He has certainly wronged you by asking your ewe in addition to his own ewes, and indeed many partners bully one another, except such as have faith and do righteous deeds, and few are they.’ Then David knew that We had tested him, whereat he pleaded with his Lord for forgiveness, and fell down in prostration and repented.

### Qur'an · Saad (The letter Saad) · 38:35
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/38/35
> قَالَ‌ رَبِ‌ اغْفِرْ لِي‌ وَ هَبْ‌ لِي‌ مُلْکاً لاَ يَنْبَغِي‌ لِأَحَدٍ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِي‌ إِنَّکَ‌ أَنْتَ‌ الْوَهَّابُ‌
>
> He said, ‘My Lord! Forgive me, and grant me a kingdom that will not befit anyone except me. Indeed You are the All-munificent.’

### Qur'an · Saad (The letter Saad) · 38:44
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/38/44
> وَ خُذْ بِيَدِکَ‌ ضِغْثاً فَاضْرِبْ‌ بِهِ‌ وَ لاَ تَحْنَثْ‌ إِنَّا وَجَدْنَاهُ‌ صَابِراً نِعْمَ‌ الْعَبْدُ إِنَّهُ‌ أَوَّابٌ‌
>
> [We told him:] ‘Take a faggot in your hand and then strike [your wife] with it, but do not break [your] oath.’ Indeed, We found him to be patient. What an excellent servant! Indeed he was a penitent [soul].

### Qur'an · Saad (The letter Saad) · 38:52
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/38/52
> وَ عِنْدَهُمْ‌ قَاصِرَاتُ‌ الطَّرْفِ‌ أَتْرَابٌ‌
>
> and there will be with them maidens of restrained glances, of a like age.

### Qur'an · Az-Zumar (The Groups) · 39:3
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/39/3
> أَلاَ لِلَّهِ‌ الدِّينُ‌ الْخَالِصُ‌ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ اتَّخَذُوا مِنْ‌ دُونِهِ‌ أَوْلِيَاءَ مَا نَعْبُدُهُمْ‌ إِلاَّ لِيُقَرِّبُونَا إِلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ زُلْفَى‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ يَحْکُمُ‌ بَيْنَهُمْ‌ فِي‌ مَا هُمْ‌ فِيهِ‌ يَخْتَلِفُونَ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لاَ يَهْدِي‌ مَنْ‌ هُوَ کَاذِبٌ‌ کَفَّارٌ
>
> Indeed, only exclusive faith is worthy of Allah, and those who take other as awliya besides Him [claiming,] ‘We only worship them so that they may bring us near to Allah,’ Allah will judge between them concerning that about which they differ. Indeed Allah does not guide someone who is a liar and an ingrate.

### Qur'an · Az-Zumar (The Groups) · 39:7
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/39/7
> إِنْ‌ تَکْفُرُوا فَإِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَنِيٌ‌ عَنْکُمْ‌ وَ لاَ يَرْضَى‌ لِعِبَادِهِ‌ الْکُفْرَ وَ إِنْ‌ تَشْکُرُوا يَرْضَهُ‌ لَکُمْ‌ وَ لاَ تَزِرُ وَازِرَةٌ وِزْرَ أُخْرَى‌ ثُمَ‌ إِلَى‌ رَبِّکُمْ‌ مَرْجِعُکُمْ‌ فَيُنَبِّئُکُمْ‌ بِمَا کُنْتُمْ‌ تَعْمَلُونَ‌ إِنَّهُ‌ عَلِيمٌ‌ بِذَاتِ‌ الصُّدُورِ
>
> If you are ungrateful, Allah has indeed no need of you, though He does not approve ingratitude for His servants; and if you give thanks, He approves that for you. No bearer shall bear another’s burden; then your return will be to your Lord, whereat He will inform you concerning what you used to do. Indeed, He knows best what is in the breasts.

### Qur'an · Az-Zumar (The Groups) · 39:8
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/39/8
> وَ إِذَا مَسَ‌ الْإِنْسَانَ‌ ضُرٌّ دَعَا رَبَّهُ‌ مُنِيباً إِلَيْهِ‌ ثُمَ‌ إِذَا خَوَّلَهُ‌ نِعْمَةً مِنْهُ‌ نَسِيَ‌ مَا کَانَ‌ يَدْعُو إِلَيْهِ‌ مِنْ‌ قَبْلُ‌ وَ جَعَلَ‌ لِلَّهِ‌ أَنْدَاداً لِيُضِلَ‌ عَنْ‌ سَبِيلِهِ‌ قُلْ‌ تَمَتَّعْ‌ بِکُفْرِکَ‌ قَلِيلاً إِنَّکَ‌ مِنْ‌ أَصْحَابِ‌ النَّارِ
>
> When distress befalls man, he supplicates his Lord, turning to Him penitently. Then, when He grants him a blessing from Himself, he forgets that for which he had supplicated Him earlier and sets up equals to Allah, that he may lead [people] astray from His way. Say, ‘Revel in your ingratitude for a while. Indeed you are among the inmates of the Fire.’

### Qur'an · Az-Zumar (The Groups) · 39:9
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/39/9
> أَمَّنْ‌ هُوَ قَانِتٌ‌ آنَاءَ اللَّيْلِ‌ سَاجِداً وَ قَائِماً يَحْذَرُ الْآخِرَةَ وَ يَرْجُو رَحْمَةَ رَبِّهِ‌ قُلْ‌ هَلْ‌ يَسْتَوِي‌ الَّذِينَ‌ يَعْلَمُونَ‌ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ لاَ يَعْلَمُونَ‌ إِنَّمَا يَتَذَکَّرُ أُولُوا الْأَلْبَابِ‌
>
> Is he who supplicates in the watches of the night, prostrating and standing, apprehensive of the Hereafter and expecting the mercy of his Lord...? Say, ‘Are those who know equal to those who do not know?’ Only those who possess intellect take admonition.

### Qur'an · Az-Zumar (The Groups) · 39:10
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/39/10
> قُلْ‌ يَا عِبَادِ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا اتَّقُوا رَبَّکُمْ‌ لِلَّذِينَ‌ أَحْسَنُوا فِي‌ هٰذِهِ‌ الدُّنْيَا حَسَنَةٌ وَ أَرْضُ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَاسِعَةٌ إِنَّمَا يُوَفَّى‌ الصَّابِرُونَ‌ أَجْرَهُمْ‌ بِغَيْرِ حِسَابٍ‌
>
> Say, ‘[Allah declares:] “O My servants who have faith! Be wary of your Lord. For those who do good in this world there will be a good [reward], and Allah’s earth is vast. Indeed the patient will be paid in full their reward without any reckoning.” ’

### Qur'an · Az-Zumar (The Groups) · 39:38
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/39/38
> وَ لَئِنْ‌ سَأَلْتَهُمْ‌ مَنْ‌ خَلَقَ‌ السَّمَاوَاتِ‌ وَ الْأَرْضَ‌ لَيَقُولُنَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ قُلْ‌ أَ فَرَأَيْتُمْ‌ مَا تَدْعُونَ‌ مِنْ‌ دُونِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ إِنْ‌ أَرَادَنِيَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ بِضُرٍّ هَلْ‌ هُنَ‌ کَاشِفَاتُ‌ ضُرِّهِ‌ أَوْ أَرَادَنِي‌ بِرَحْمَةٍ هَلْ‌ هُنَ‌ مُمْسِکَاتُ‌ رَحْمَتِهِ‌ قُلْ‌ حَسْبِيَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَلَيْهِ‌ يَتَوَکَّلُ‌ الْمُتَوَکِّلُونَ‌
>
> If you ask them, ‘Who created the heavens and the earth?’ they will surely say, ‘Allah.’ Say, ‘Have you considered what you invoke besides Allah? Should Allah desire some distress for me, can they remove the distress visited by Him? Or should He desire some mercy for me, can they withhold His mercy?’ Say, ‘Allah is sufficient for me. In Him let all the trusting put their trust.’

### Qur'an · Az-Zumar (The Groups) · 39:49
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/39/49
> فَإِذَا مَسَ‌ الْإِنْسَانَ‌ ضُرٌّ دَعَانَا ثُمَ‌ إِذَا خَوَّلْنَاهُ‌ نِعْمَةً مِنَّا قَالَ‌ إِنَّمَا أُوتِيتُهُ‌ عَلَى‌ عِلْمٍ‌ بَلْ‌ هِيَ‌ فِتْنَةٌ وَ لٰکِنَ‌ أَکْثَرَهُمْ‌ لاَ يَعْلَمُونَ‌
>
> When distress befalls man, he supplicates Us. Then, when We grant him a blessing from Us, he says, ‘I was given it by virtue of [my] knowledge.’ Indeed, it is a test, but most of them do not know.

### Qur'an · Az-Zumar (The Groups) · 39:53
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance, hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/39/53
> قُلْ‌ يَا عِبَادِيَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ أَسْرَفُوا عَلَى‌ أَنْفُسِهِمْ‌ لاَ تَقْنَطُوا مِنْ‌ رَحْمَةِ اللَّهِ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ يَغْفِرُ الذُّنُوبَ‌ جَمِيعاً إِنَّهُ‌ هُوَ الْغَفُورُ الرَّحِيمُ‌
>
> Say [that Allah declares,] ‘O My servants who have committed excesses against their own souls, do not despair of the mercy of Allah. Indeed Allah will forgive all sins. Indeed, He is the All-forgiving, the All-merciful.

### Qur'an · Az-Zumar (The Groups) · 39:66
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/39/66
> بَلِ‌ اللَّهَ‌ فَاعْبُدْ وَ کُنْ‌ مِنَ‌ الشَّاکِرِينَ‌
>
> No, worship Allah, and be among the grateful!’

### Qur'an · Al-Ghaafir (The Forgiver) · 40:3
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/40/3
> غَافِرِ الذَّنْبِ‌ وَ قَابِلِ‌ التَّوْبِ‌ شَدِيدِ الْعِقَابِ‌ ذِي‌ الطَّوْلِ‌ لاَ إِلٰهَ‌ إِلاَّ هُوَ إِلَيْهِ‌ الْمَصِيرُ
>
> forgiver of sins and acceptor of repentance, severe in retribution, [yet] all-bountiful; there is no god except Him, [and] toward Him is the destination.

### Qur'an · Al-Ghaafir (The Forgiver) · 40:7
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/40/7
> الَّذِينَ‌ يَحْمِلُونَ‌ الْعَرْشَ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ حَوْلَهُ‌ يُسَبِّحُونَ‌ بِحَمْدِ رَبِّهِمْ‌ وَ يُؤْمِنُونَ‌ بِهِ‌ وَ يَسْتَغْفِرُونَ‌ لِلَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا رَبَّنَا وَسِعْتَ‌ کُلَ‌ شَيْ‌ءٍ رَحْمَةً وَ عِلْماً فَاغْفِرْ لِلَّذِينَ‌ تَابُوا وَ اتَّبَعُوا سَبِيلَکَ‌ وَ قِهِمْ‌ عَذَابَ‌ الْجَحِيمِ‌
>
> Those who bear the Throne, and those who are around it, celebrate the praise of their Lord and have faith in Him, and they plead for forgiveness for the faithful: ‘Our Lord! You embrace all things in mercy and knowledge. So forgive those who repent and follow Your way and save them from the punishment of hell.

### Qur'an · Al-Ghaafir (The Forgiver) · 40:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/40/14
> فَادْعُوا اللَّهَ‌ مُخْلِصِينَ‌ لَهُ‌ الدِّينَ‌ وَ لَوْ کَرِهَ‌ الْکَافِرُونَ‌
>
> So supplicate Allah, putting exclusive faith in Him, though the faithless should be averse.

### Qur'an · Al-Ghaafir (The Forgiver) · 40:31
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/40/31
> مِثْلَ‌ دَأْبِ‌ قَوْمِ‌ نُوحٍ‌ وَ عَادٍ وَ ثَمُودَ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِهِمْ‌ وَ مَا اللَّهُ‌ يُرِيدُ ظُلْماً لِلْعِبَادِ
>
> like the case of the people of Noah, of Ad and Thamud, and those who came after them, and Allah does not desire any wrong for [His] servants.

### Qur'an · Al-Ghaafir (The Forgiver) · 40:55
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/40/55
> فَاصْبِرْ إِنَ‌ وَعْدَ اللَّهِ‌ حَقٌ‌ وَ اسْتَغْفِرْ لِذَنْبِکَ‌ وَ سَبِّحْ‌ بِحَمْدِ رَبِّکَ‌ بِالْعَشِيِ‌ وَ الْإِبْکَارِ
>
> So be patient! Allah’s promise is indeed true. Plead [Allah] for forgiveness of your sin, and celebrate the praise of your Lord morning and evening.

### Qur'an · Al-Ghaafir (The Forgiver) · 40:61
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/40/61
> اللَّهُ‌ الَّذِي‌ جَعَلَ‌ لَکُمُ‌ اللَّيْلَ‌ لِتَسْکُنُوا فِيهِ‌ وَ النَّهَارَ مُبْصِراً إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لَذُو فَضْلٍ‌ عَلَى‌ النَّاسِ‌ وَ لٰکِنَ‌ أَکْثَرَ النَّاسِ‌ لاَ يَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> It is Allah who made the night for you, that you may rest in it, and the day to provide visibility. Indeed Allah is gracious to mankind, but most people do not give thanks.

### Qur'an · Al-Ghaafir (The Forgiver) · 40:65
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/40/65
> هُوَ الْحَيُ‌ لاَ إِلٰهَ‌ إِلاَّ هُوَ فَادْعُوهُ‌ مُخْلِصِينَ‌ لَهُ‌ الدِّينَ‌ الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ‌ رَبِ‌ الْعَالَمِينَ‌
>
> He is the Living One, there is no god except Him. So supplicate Him, putting exclusive faith in Him. All praise belongs to Allah, Lord of all the worlds.

### Qur'an · Al-Ghaafir (The Forgiver) · 40:77
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/40/77
> فَاصْبِرْ إِنَ‌ وَعْدَ اللَّهِ‌ حَقٌ‌ فَإِمَّا نُرِيَنَّکَ‌ بَعْضَ‌ الَّذِي‌ نَعِدُهُمْ‌ أَوْ نَتَوَفَّيَنَّکَ‌ فَإِلَيْنَا يُرْجَعُونَ‌
>
> So be patient! Allah’s promise is indeed true. Whether We show you a part of what We promise them, or take you away [before that], [in any case] they will be brought back to Us.

### Qur'an · Fussilat (Explained in detail) · 41:6
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/41/6
> قُلْ‌ إِنَّمَا أَنَا بَشَرٌ مِثْلُکُمْ‌ يُوحَى‌ إِلَيَ‌ أَنَّمَا إِلٰهُکُمْ‌ إِلٰهٌ‌ وَاحِدٌ فَاسْتَقِيمُوا إِلَيْهِ‌ وَ اسْتَغْفِرُوهُ‌ وَ وَيْلٌ‌ لِلْمُشْرِکِينَ‌
>
> Say, ‘I am just a human like you. It has been revealed to me that your God is the One God. So worship Him single-mindedly and plead to Him for forgiveness.’ And woe to the polytheists

### Qur'an · Fussilat (Explained in detail) · 41:24
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/41/24
> فَإِنْ‌ يَصْبِرُوا فَالنَّارُ مَثْوًى‌ لَهُمْ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ يَسْتَعْتِبُوا فَمَا هُمْ‌ مِنَ‌ الْمُعْتَبِينَ‌
>
> Should they be patient, the Fire is their abode; and should they seek to propitiate [Allah], they will not be redeemed.

### Qur'an · Fussilat (Explained in detail) · 41:30
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/41/30
> إِنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ قَالُوا رَبُّنَا اللَّهُ‌ ثُمَ‌ اسْتَقَامُوا تَتَنَزَّلُ‌ عَلَيْهِمُ‌ الْمَلاَئِکَةُ أَلاَّ تَخَافُوا وَ لاَ تَحْزَنُوا وَ أَبْشِرُوا بِالْجَنَّةِ الَّتِي‌ کُنْتُمْ‌ تُوعَدُونَ‌
>
> Indeed those who say, ‘Our Lord is Allah!’ and then remain steadfast, the angels descend upon them, [saying,] ‘Do not fear, nor be grieved! Receive the good news of the paradise, which you have been promised.

### Qur'an · Fussilat (Explained in detail) · 41:31
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/41/31
> نَحْنُ‌ أَوْلِيَاؤُکُمْ‌ فِي‌ الْحَيَاةِ الدُّنْيَا وَ فِي‌ الْآخِرَةِ وَ لَکُمْ‌ فِيهَا مَا تَشْتَهِي‌ أَنْفُسُکُمْ‌ وَ لَکُمْ‌ فِيهَا مَا تَدَّعُونَ‌
>
> We are your friends in the life of this world and in the Hereafter, and you will have in it whatever your souls desire, and you will have in it whatever you ask for,

### Qur'an · Fussilat (Explained in detail) · 41:32
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
