> نُزُلاً مِنْ‌ غَفُورٍ رَحِيمٍ‌
>
> as a hospitality from One all-forgiving, all-merciful.’

### Qur'an · Fussilat (Explained in detail) · 41:35
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/41/35
> وَ مَا يُلَقَّاهَا إِلاَّ الَّذِينَ‌ صَبَرُوا وَ مَا يُلَقَّاهَا إِلاَّ ذُو حَظٍّ عَظِيمٍ‌
>
> But none is granted it except those who are patient, and none is granted it except the greatly endowed.

### Qur'an · Fussilat (Explained in detail) · 41:43
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/41/43
> مَا يُقَالُ‌ لَکَ‌ إِلاَّ مَا قَدْ قِيلَ‌ لِلرُّسُلِ‌ مِنْ‌ قَبْلِکَ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ لَذُو مَغْفِرَةٍ وَ ذُو عِقَابٍ‌ أَلِيمٍ‌
>
> Nothing is said to you except what has already been said to the apostles before you. Indeed your Lord is One who forgives and also metes out a painful retribution.

### Qur'an · Fussilat (Explained in detail) · 41:49
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope, prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/41/49
> لاَ يَسْأَمُ‌ الْإِنْسَانُ‌ مِنْ‌ دُعَاءِ الْخَيْرِ وَ إِنْ‌ مَسَّهُ‌ الشَّرُّ فَيَئُوسٌ‌ قَنُوطٌ
>
> Man is never wearied of supplicating for good, and should any ill befall him, he becomes hopeless and despondent.

### Qur'an · Fussilat (Explained in detail) · 41:51
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/41/51
> وَ إِذَا أَنْعَمْنَا عَلَى‌ الْإِنْسَانِ‌ أَعْرَضَ‌ وَ نَأَى‌ بِجَانِبِهِ‌ وَ إِذَا مَسَّهُ‌ الشَّرُّ فَذُو دُعَاءٍ عَرِيضٍ‌
>
> When We bless man, he is disregardful and turns aside; but when an ill befalls him, he makes protracted supplications.

### Qur'an · Ash-Shura (Consultation) · 42:5
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/42/5
> تَکَادُ السَّمَاوَاتُ‌ يَتَفَطَّرْنَ‌ مِنْ‌ فَوْقِهِنَ‌ وَ الْمَلاَئِکَةُ يُسَبِّحُونَ‌ بِحَمْدِ رَبِّهِمْ‌ وَ يَسْتَغْفِرُونَ‌ لِمَنْ‌ فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ أَلاَ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ هُوَ الْغَفُورُ الرَّحِيمُ‌
>
> The heavens are about to be rent apart from above them, while the angels celebrate the praise of their Lord and plead for forgiveness for those [faithful] who are on the earth. Indeed, Allah is the All-forgiving, the All-merciful!

### Qur'an · Ash-Shura (Consultation) · 42:15
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control, perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/42/15
> فَلِذٰلِکَ‌ فَادْعُ‌ وَ اسْتَقِمْ‌ کَمَا أُمِرْتَ‌ وَ لاَ تَتَّبِعْ‌ أَهْوَاءَهُمْ‌ وَ قُلْ‌ آمَنْتُ‌ بِمَا أَنْزَلَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ مِنْ‌ کِتَابٍ‌ وَ أُمِرْتُ‌ لِأَعْدِلَ‌ بَيْنَکُمُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ رَبُّنَا وَ رَبُّکُمْ‌ لَنَا أَعْمَالُنَا وَ لَکُمْ‌ أَعْمَالُکُمْ‌ لاَ حُجَّةَ بَيْنَنَا وَ بَيْنَکُمُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ يَجْمَعُ‌ بَيْنَنَا وَ إِلَيْهِ‌ الْمَصِيرُ
>
> So summon to this [unity of religion], and be steadfast, just as you have been commanded, and do not follow their desires, and say, ‘I believe in whatever Book Allah has sent down. I have been commanded to do justice among you. Allah is our Lord and your Lord. Our deeds belong to us and your deeds belong to you. There is no quarrel between us and you. Allah will bring us together and toward Him is the destination.’

### Qur'an · Ash-Shura (Consultation) · 42:20
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/42/20
> مَنْ‌ کَانَ‌ يُرِيدُ حَرْثَ‌ الْآخِرَةِ نَزِدْ لَهُ‌ فِي‌ حَرْثِهِ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ کَانَ‌ يُرِيدُ حَرْثَ‌ الدُّنْيَا نُؤْتِهِ‌ مِنْهَا وَ مَا لَهُ‌ فِي‌ الْآخِرَةِ مِنْ‌ نَصِيبٍ‌
>
> Whoever desires the tillage of the Hereafter, We will enhance for him his tillage, and whoever desires the tillage of the world, We will give it to him, but he will have no share in the Hereafter.

### Qur'an · Ash-Shura (Consultation) · 42:23
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/42/23
> ذٰلِکَ‌ الَّذِي‌ يُبَشِّرُ اللَّهُ‌ عِبَادَهُ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا وَ عَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ‌ قُلْ‌ لاَ أَسْأَلُکُمْ‌ عَلَيْهِ‌ أَجْراً إِلاَّ الْمَوَدَّةَ فِي‌ الْقُرْبَى‌ وَ مَنْ‌ يَقْتَرِفْ‌ حَسَنَةً نَزِدْ لَهُ‌ فِيهَا حُسْناً إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ شَکُورٌ
>
> Such is the good news that Allah gives to His servants who have faith and do righteous deeds! Say, ‘I do not ask you any reward for it except the love of [my] relatives.’ Whoever performs a good deed, We shall enhance its goodness for him. Indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-appreciative.

### Qur'an · Ash-Shura (Consultation) · 42:25
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/42/25
> وَ هُوَ الَّذِي‌ يَقْبَلُ‌ التَّوْبَةَ عَنْ‌ عِبَادِهِ‌ وَ يَعْفُو عَنِ‌ السَّيِّئَاتِ‌ وَ يَعْلَمُ‌ مَا تَفْعَلُونَ‌
>
> It is He who accepts the repentance of His servants, and excuses their misdeeds and knows what you do.

### Qur'an · Ash-Shura (Consultation) · 42:26
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/42/26
> وَ يَسْتَجِيبُ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا وَ عَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ‌ وَ يَزِيدُهُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ فَضْلِهِ‌ وَ الْکَافِرُونَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ عَذَابٌ‌ شَدِيدٌ
>
> He answers [the supplications of] those who have faith and do righteous deeds and enhances them out of His grace. But as for the faithless, there is a severe punishment for them.

### Qur'an · Ash-Shura (Consultation) · 42:33
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/42/33
> إِنْ‌ يَشَأْ يُسْکِنِ‌ الرِّيحَ‌ فَيَظْلَلْنَ‌ رَوَاکِدَ عَلَى‌ ظَهْرِهِ‌ إِنَ‌ فِي‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ لَآيَاتٍ‌ لِکُلِ‌ صَبَّارٍ شَکُورٍ
>
> If He wishes He stills the wind, whereat they remain standstill on its surface. There are indeed signs in that for every patient and grateful [servant].

### Qur'an · Ash-Shura (Consultation) · 42:37
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/42/37
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ يَجْتَنِبُونَ‌ کَبَائِرَ الْإِثْمِ‌ وَ الْفَوَاحِشَ‌ وَ إِذَا مَا غَضِبُوا هُمْ‌ يَغْفِرُونَ‌
>
> —those who avoid major sins and indecencies, and forgive when angered;

### Qur'an · Ash-Shura (Consultation) · 42:38
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/42/38
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ اسْتَجَابُوا لِرَبِّهِمْ‌ وَ أَقَامُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ أَمْرُهُمْ‌ شُورَى‌ بَيْنَهُمْ‌ وَ مِمَّا رَزَقْنَاهُمْ‌ يُنْفِقُونَ‌
>
> those who answer their Lord, maintain the prayer, and [conduct] their affairs by counsel among themselves, and they spend out of what We have provided them;

### Qur'an · Ash-Shura (Consultation) · 42:43
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/42/43
> وَ لَمَنْ‌ صَبَرَ وَ غَفَرَ إِنَ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ لَمِنْ‌ عَزْمِ‌ الْأُمُورِ
>
> As for someone who endures patiently and forgives—that is indeed the steadiest of courses.

### Qur'an · Az-Zukhruf (Ornaments of gold) · 43:15
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/43/15
> وَ جَعَلُوا لَهُ‌ مِنْ‌ عِبَادِهِ‌ جُزْءاً إِنَ‌ الْإِنْسَانَ‌ لَکَفُورٌ مُبِينٌ‌
>
> They ascribe to Him offspring from among His servants! Man is indeed a manifest ingrate.

### Qur'an · Az-Zukhruf (Ornaments of gold) · 43:71
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/43/71
> يُطَافُ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ بِصِحَافٍ‌ مِنْ‌ ذَهَبٍ‌ وَ أَکْوَابٍ‌ وَ فِيهَا مَا تَشْتَهِيهِ‌ الْأَنْفُسُ‌ وَ تَلَذُّ الْأَعْيُنُ‌ وَ أَنْتُمْ‌ فِيهَا خَالِدُونَ‌
>
> They will be served around with golden dishes and goblets, and therein will be whatever the souls desire and eyes delight in. ‘You will remain in it [forever].

### Qur'an · Al-Jaathiya (Crouching) · 45:8
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/45/8
> يَسْمَعُ‌ آيَاتِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ تُتْلَى‌ عَلَيْهِ‌ ثُمَ‌ يُصِرُّ مُسْتَکْبِراً کَأَنْ‌ لَمْ‌ يَسْمَعْهَا فَبَشِّرْهُ‌ بِعَذَابٍ‌ أَلِيمٍ‌
>
> who hears the signs of Allah being recited to him, yet persists arrogantly as if he had not heard them. So inform him of a painful punishment.

### Qur'an · Al-Jaathiya (Crouching) · 45:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/45/12
> اللَّهُ‌ الَّذِي‌ سَخَّرَ لَکُمُ‌ الْبَحْرَ لِتَجْرِيَ‌ الْفُلْکُ‌ فِيهِ‌ بِأَمْرِهِ‌ وَ لِتَبْتَغُوا مِنْ‌ فَضْلِهِ‌ وَ لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> It is Allah who disposed the sea for you[r benefit] so that the ships may sail in it by His command, that you may seek of His bounty and that you may give thanks.

### Qur'an · Al-Jaathiya (Crouching) · 45:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/45/14
> قُلْ‌ لِلَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا يَغْفِرُوا لِلَّذِينَ‌ لاَ يَرْجُونَ‌ أَيَّامَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ لِيَجْزِيَ‌ قَوْماً بِمَا کَانُوا يَکْسِبُونَ‌
>
> Say to the faithful to forgive those who do not expect Allah’s days, that He may [Himself] requite every people for what they used to earn.

### Qur'an · Al-Jaathiya (Crouching) · 45:18
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/45/18
> ثُمَ‌ جَعَلْنَاکَ‌ عَلَى‌ شَرِيعَةٍ مِنَ‌ الْأَمْرِ فَاتَّبِعْهَا وَ لاَ تَتَّبِعْ‌ أَهْوَاءَ الَّذِينَ‌ لاَ يَعْلَمُونَ‌
>
> Then We set you upon a clear course of the Law; so follow it, and do not follow the desires of those who do not know.

### Qur'an · Al-Jaathiya (Crouching) · 45:23
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/45/23
> أَ فَرَأَيْتَ‌ مَنِ‌ اتَّخَذَ إِلٰهَهُ‌ هَوَاهُ‌ وَ أَضَلَّهُ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَلَى‌ عِلْمٍ‌ وَ خَتَمَ‌ عَلَى‌ سَمْعِهِ‌ وَ قَلْبِهِ‌ وَ جَعَلَ‌ عَلَى‌ بَصَرِهِ‌ غِشَاوَةً فَمَنْ‌ يَهْدِيهِ‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِ اللَّهِ‌ أَ فَلاَ تَذَکَّرُونَ‌
>
> Have you seen him who has taken his desire to be his god and whom Allah has led astray knowingly, set a seal upon his hearing and heart, and put a blindfold on his sight? So who will guide him after Allah [has consigned him to error]? Will you not then take admonition?

### Qur'an · Al-Ahqaf (The Dunes) · 46:8
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/46/8
> أَمْ‌ يَقُولُونَ‌ افْتَرَاهُ‌ قُلْ‌ إِنِ‌ افْتَرَيْتُهُ‌ فَلاَ تَمْلِکُونَ‌ لِي‌ مِنَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ شَيْئاً هُوَ أَعْلَمُ‌ بِمَا تُفِيضُونَ‌ فِيهِ‌ کَفَى‌ بِهِ‌ شَهِيداً بَيْنِي‌ وَ بَيْنَکُمْ‌ وَ هُوَ الْغَفُورُ الرَّحِيمُ‌
>
> Or they say, ‘He has fabricated it.’ Say, ‘Should I have fabricated it, you would not avail me anything against Allah. He best knows what you gossip concerning it. He suffices as a witness between me and you, and He is the All-forgiving, the All-merciful.’

### Qur'an · Al-Ahqaf (The Dunes) · 46:13
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/46/13
> إِنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ قَالُوا رَبُّنَا اللَّهُ‌ ثُمَ‌ اسْتَقَامُوا فَلاَ خَوْفٌ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ وَ لاَ هُمْ‌ يَحْزَنُونَ‌
>
> Those who say, ‘Our Lord is Allah,’ and then remain steadfast, they will have no fear, nor will they grieve.

### Qur'an · Al-Ahqaf (The Dunes) · 46:15
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/46/15
> وَ وَصَّيْنَا الْإِنْسَانَ‌ بِوَالِدَيْهِ‌ إِحْسَاناً حَمَلَتْهُ‌ أُمُّهُ‌ کُرْهاً وَ وَضَعَتْهُ‌ کُرْهاً وَ حَمْلُهُ‌ وَ فِصَالُهُ‌ ثَلاَثُونَ‌ شَهْراً حَتَّى‌ إِذَا بَلَغَ‌ أَشُدَّهُ‌ وَ بَلَغَ‌ أَرْبَعِينَ‌ سَنَةً قَالَ‌ رَبِ‌ أَوْزِعْنِي‌ أَنْ‌ أَشْکُرَ نِعْمَتَکَ‌ الَّتِي‌ أَنْعَمْتَ‌ عَلَيَ‌ وَ عَلَى‌ وَالِدَيَ‌ وَ أَنْ‌ أَعْمَلَ‌ صَالِحاً تَرْضَاهُ‌ وَ أَصْلِحْ‌ لِي‌ فِي‌ ذُرِّيَّتِي‌ إِنِّي‌ تُبْتُ‌ إِلَيْکَ‌ وَ إِنِّي‌ مِنَ‌ الْمُسْلِمِينَ‌
>
> We have enjoined man to be kind to his parents. His mother has carried him in travail, and bore him in travail, and his gestation and weaning take thirty months. When he comes of age and reaches forty years, he says, ‘My Lord! Inspire me to give thanks for Your blessing with which You have blessed me and my parents, and that I may do righteous deeds which please You, and invest my descendants with righteousness. Indeed I have turned to you in penitence, and I am one of the Muslims.’

### Qur'an · Al-Ahqaf (The Dunes) · 46:31
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/46/31
> يَا قَوْمَنَا أَجِيبُوا دَاعِيَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ آمِنُوا بِهِ‌ يَغْفِرْ لَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ ذُنُوبِکُمْ‌ وَ يُجِرْکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ عَذَابٍ‌ أَلِيمٍ‌
>
> O our people! Respond to Allah’s summoner and have faith in Him. He will forgive you some of your sins and shelter you from a painful punishment.’

### Qur'an · Al-Ahqaf (The Dunes) · 46:35
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/46/35
> فَاصْبِرْ کَمَا صَبَرَ أُولُوا الْعَزْمِ‌ مِنَ‌ الرُّسُلِ‌ وَ لاَ تَسْتَعْجِلْ‌ لَهُمْ‌ کَأَنَّهُمْ‌ يَوْمَ‌ يَرَوْنَ‌ مَا يُوعَدُونَ‌ لَمْ‌ يَلْبَثُوا إِلاَّ سَاعَةً مِنْ‌ نَهَارٍ بَلاَغٌ‌ فَهَلْ‌ يُهْلَکُ‌ إِلاَّ الْقَوْمُ‌ الْفَاسِقُونَ‌
>
> So be patient just as the resolute among the apostles were patient, and do not seek to hasten [the punishment] for them. The day when they see what they are promised, [it will be] as though they had remained [in the world] only an hour of a day. This is a proclamation. So shall anyone be destroyed except the transgressing lot?

### Qur'an · Muhammad (Muhammad) · 47:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/47/14
> أَ فَمَنْ‌ کَانَ‌ عَلَى‌ بَيِّنَةٍ مِنْ‌ رَبِّهِ‌ کَمَنْ‌ زُيِّنَ‌ لَهُ‌ سُوءُ عَمَلِهِ‌ وَ اتَّبَعُوا أَهْوَاءَهُمْ‌
>
> Is he who stands on a manifest proof from his Lord like those to whom the evil of their conduct is made to seem decorous and who follow their desires?

### Qur'an · Muhammad (Muhammad) · 47:15
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/47/15
> مَثَلُ‌ الْجَنَّةِ الَّتِي‌ وُعِدَ الْمُتَّقُونَ‌ فِيهَا أَنْهَارٌ مِنْ‌ مَاءٍ غَيْرِ آسِنٍ‌ وَ أَنْهَارٌ مِنْ‌ لَبَنٍ‌ لَمْ‌ يَتَغَيَّرْ طَعْمُهُ‌ وَ أَنْهَارٌ مِنْ‌ خَمْرٍ لَذَّةٍ لِلشَّارِبِينَ‌ وَ أَنْهَارٌ مِنْ‌ عَسَلٍ‌ مُصَفًّى‌ وَ لَهُمْ‌ فِيهَا مِنْ‌ کُلِ‌ الثَّمَرَاتِ‌ وَ مَغْفِرَةٌ مِنْ‌ رَبِّهِمْ‌ کَمَنْ‌ هُوَ خَالِدٌ فِي‌ النَّارِ وَ سُقُوا مَاءً حَمِيماً فَقَطَّعَ‌ أَمْعَاءَهُمْ‌
>
> A description of the paradise promised to the Godwary: therein are streams of unstaling water and streams of milk unchanging in flavour, and streams of wine delicious to the drinkers, and streams of purified honey; there will be every kind of fruit in it for them, and forgiveness from their Lord. [Are such ones] like those who abide in the Fire and are given to drink boiling water which cuts up their bowels?

### Qur'an · Muhammad (Muhammad) · 47:16
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/47/16
> وَ مِنْهُمْ‌ مَنْ‌ يَسْتَمِعُ‌ إِلَيْکَ‌ حَتَّى‌ إِذَا خَرَجُوا مِنْ‌ عِنْدِکَ‌ قَالُوا لِلَّذِينَ‌ أُوتُوا الْعِلْمَ‌ مَا ذَا قَالَ‌ آنِفاً أُولٰئِکَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ طَبَعَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَلَى‌ قُلُوبِهِمْ‌ وَ اتَّبَعُوا أَهْوَاءَهُمْ‌
>
> There are some among them who prick up their ears at you. But when they go out from your presence, they say to those who have been given knowledge, ‘What did he say just now?’ They are the ones on whose hearts Allah has set a seal, and they follow their own desires.

### Qur'an · Muhammad (Muhammad) · 47:19
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/47/19
> فَاعْلَمْ‌ أَنَّهُ‌ لاَ إِلٰهَ‌ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ‌ وَ اسْتَغْفِرْ لِذَنْبِکَ‌ وَ لِلْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌ وَ الْمُؤْمِنَاتِ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ يَعْلَمُ‌ مُتَقَلَّبَکُمْ‌ وَ مَثْوَاکُمْ‌
>
> Know that there is no god except Allah, and plead [to Allah] for forgiveness of your sin and for the faithful, men and women. Allah knows your itinerary and your [final] abode.

### Qur'an · Muhammad (Muhammad) · 47:25
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/47/25
> إِنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ ارْتَدُّوا عَلَى‌ أَدْبَارِهِمْ‌ مِنْ‌ بَعْدِ مَا تَبَيَّنَ‌ لَهُمُ‌ الْهُدَى‌ الشَّيْطَانُ‌ سَوَّلَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ وَ أَمْلَى‌ لَهُمْ‌
>
> Indeed those who turned their backs after the guidance had become clear to them, it was Satan who had seduced them, and he had given them [far-flung] hopes.

### Qur'an · Muhammad (Muhammad) · 47:31
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/47/31
> وَ لَنَبْلُوَنَّکُمْ‌ حَتَّى‌ نَعْلَمَ‌ الْمُجَاهِدِينَ‌ مِنْکُمْ‌ وَ الصَّابِرِينَ‌ وَ نَبْلُوَ أَخْبَارَکُمْ‌
>
> We will surely test you until We ascertain those of you who wage jihad and those who are steadfast, and We shall appraise your record.

### Qur'an · Muhammad (Muhammad) · 47:34
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/47/34
> إِنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا وَ صَدُّوا عَنْ‌ سَبِيلِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ ثُمَ‌ مَاتُوا وَ هُمْ‌ کُفَّارٌ فَلَنْ‌ يَغْفِرَ اللَّهُ‌ لَهُمْ‌
>
> Indeed those who are faithless and bar from the way of Allah and then die faithless, Allah will never forgive them.

### Qur'an · Al-Fath (The Victory) · 48:2
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/48/2
> لِيَغْفِرَ لَکَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ مَا تَقَدَّمَ‌ مِنْ‌ ذَنْبِکَ‌ وَ مَا تَأَخَّرَ وَ يُتِمَ‌ نِعْمَتَهُ‌ عَلَيْکَ‌ وَ يَهْدِيَکَ‌ صِرَاطاً مُسْتَقِيماً
>
> that Allah may forgive you what is past of your sin and what is to come, and that He may perfect His blessing upon you and guide you on a straight path,

### Qur'an · Al-Fath (The Victory) · 48:11
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance, self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/48/11
> سَيَقُولُ‌ لَکَ‌ الْمُخَلَّفُونَ‌ مِنَ‌ الْأَعْرَابِ‌ شَغَلَتْنَا أَمْوَالُنَا وَ أَهْلُونَا فَاسْتَغْفِرْ لَنَا يَقُولُونَ‌ بِأَلْسِنَتِهِمْ‌ مَا لَيْسَ‌ فِي‌ قُلُوبِهِمْ‌ قُلْ‌ فَمَنْ‌ يَمْلِکُ‌ لَکُمْ‌ مِنَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ شَيْئاً إِنْ‌ أَرَادَ بِکُمْ‌ ضَرّاً أَوْ أَرَادَ بِکُمْ‌ نَفْعاً بَلْ‌ کَانَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ‌ خَبِيراً
>
> The Bedouins who had stayed back [from joining the Prophet in his ‘umrah journey to Makkah] will tell you, ‘Our possessions and families kept us occupied. So plead [to Allah] for our forgiveness!’ They will say with their tongues what is not in their hearts. Say, ‘Whether He desires to cause you harm or desires to bring you benefit, who can be of any avail to you against Allah[’s will]? Indeed Allah is well aware of what you do.’

### Qur'an · Al-Fath (The Victory) · 48:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/48/14
> وَ لِلَّهِ‌ مُلْکُ‌ السَّمَاوَاتِ‌ وَ الْأَرْضِ‌ يَغْفِرُ لِمَنْ‌ يَشَاءُ وَ يُعَذِّبُ‌ مَنْ‌ يَشَاءُ وَ کَانَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُوراً رَحِيماً
>
> To Allah belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth: He forgives whomever He wishes, and punishes whomever He wishes, and Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Fath (The Victory) · 48:15
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/48/15
> سَيَقُولُ‌ الْمُخَلَّفُونَ‌ إِذَا انْطَلَقْتُمْ‌ إِلَى‌ مَغَانِمَ‌ لِتَأْخُذُوهَا ذَرُونَا نَتَّبِعْکُمْ‌ يُرِيدُونَ‌ أَنْ‌ يُبَدِّلُوا کَلاَمَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ قُلْ‌ لَنْ‌ تَتَّبِعُونَا کَذٰلِکُمْ‌ قَالَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ مِنْ‌ قَبْلُ‌ فَسَيَقُولُونَ‌ بَلْ‌ تَحْسُدُونَنَا بَلْ‌ کَانُوا لاَ يَفْقَهُونَ‌ إِلاَّ قَلِيلاً
>
> [In the near future] when you will set out to capture booty, those who stayed behind [in this journey] will say, : ‘Let us follow you.’ They desire to change the word of Allah. Say, ‘You will never follow us! Allah has said thus beforehand.’ Then they will say, ‘You are envious of us.’ Indeed, they do not understand but a little.

### Qur'an · Al-Fath (The Victory) · 48:29
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/48/29
> مُحَمَّدٌ رَسُولُ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ مَعَهُ‌ أَشِدَّاءُ عَلَى‌ الْکُفَّارِ رُحَمَاءُ بَيْنَهُمْ‌ تَرَاهُمْ‌ رُکَّعاً سُجَّداً يَبْتَغُونَ‌ فَضْلاً مِنَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ رِضْوَاناً سِيمَاهُمْ‌ فِي‌ وُجُوهِهِمْ‌ مِنْ‌ أَثَرِ السُّجُودِ ذٰلِکَ‌ مَثَلُهُمْ‌ فِي‌ التَّوْرَاةِ وَ مَثَلُهُمْ‌ فِي‌ الْإِنْجِيلِ‌ کَزَرْعٍ‌ أَخْرَجَ‌ شَطْأَهُ‌ فَآزَرَهُ‌ فَاسْتَغْلَظَ فَاسْتَوَى‌ عَلَى‌ سُوقِهِ‌ يُعْجِبُ‌ الزُّرَّاعَ‌ لِيَغِيظَ بِهِمُ‌ الْکُفَّارَ وَعَدَ اللَّهُ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا وَ عَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ‌ مِنْهُمْ‌ مَغْفِرَةً وَ أَجْراً عَظِيماً
>
> Muhammad, the Apostle of Allah, and those who are with him are hard against the faithless and merciful amongst themselves. You see them bowing and prostrating [in worship], seeking Allah’s grace, and [His] pleasure. Their mark is [visible] on their faces, from the effect of prostration. Such is their description in the Torah and their description in the Evangel. Like a tillage that sends out its shoots and builds them up, and they grow stout and settle on their stalks, impressing the sowers, so that He may enrage the faithless by them. Allah has promised those of them who have faith and do righteous deeds forgiveness and a great reward.

### Qur'an · Al-Hujuraat (The Inner Apartments) · 49:3
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/49/3
> إِنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ يَغُضُّونَ‌ أَصْوَاتَهُمْ‌ عِنْدَ رَسُولِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ أُولٰئِکَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ امْتَحَنَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ قُلُوبَهُمْ‌ لِلتَّقْوَى‌ لَهُمْ‌ مَغْفِرَةٌ وَ أَجْرٌ عَظِيمٌ‌
>
> Indeed those who lower their voices in the presence of the Apostle of Allah—they are the ones whose hearts Allah has tested for Godwariness. For them will be forgiveness and a great reward.

### Qur'an · Al-Hujuraat (The Inner Apartments) · 49:5
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance

> وَ لَوْ أَنَّهُمْ‌ صَبَرُوا حَتَّى‌ تَخْرُجَ‌ إِلَيْهِمْ‌ لَکَانَ‌ خَيْراً لَهُمْ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> Had they been patient until you came out for them, it would have been better for them, and Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Hujuraat (The Inner Apartments) · 49:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/49/14
> قَالَتِ‌ الْأَعْرَابُ‌ آمَنَّا قُلْ‌ لَمْ‌ تُؤْمِنُوا وَ لٰکِنْ‌ قُولُوا أَسْلَمْنَا وَ لَمَّا يَدْخُلِ‌ الْإِيمَانُ‌ فِي‌ قُلُوبِکُمْ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ تُطِيعُوا اللَّهَ‌ وَ رَسُولَهُ‌ لاَ يَلِتْکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ أَعْمَالِکُمْ‌ شَيْئاً إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> The Bedouins say, ‘We have faith.’ Say, ‘You do not have faith yet; rather, say, ‘‘We have embraced Islam,’’ for faith has not yet entered into your hearts. Yet if you obey Allah and His Apostle, He will not stint anything of [the reward of] your works. Indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.’

themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/53/23
> إِنْ‌ هِيَ‌ إِلاَّ أَسْمَاءٌ سَمَّيْتُمُوهَا أَنْتُمْ‌ وَ آبَاؤُکُمْ‌ مَا أَنْزَلَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ بِهَا مِنْ‌ سُلْطَانٍ‌ إِنْ‌ يَتَّبِعُونَ‌ إِلاَّ الظَّنَ‌ وَ مَا تَهْوَى‌ الْأَنْفُسُ‌ وَ لَقَدْ جَاءَهُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ رَبِّهِمُ‌ الْهُدَى‌
>
> These are but names, which you have coined—you and your fathers—for which Allah has not sent down any authority. They follow nothing but conjectures and the desires of the [lower] soul, while there has already come to them the guidance from their Lord.

### Qur'an · An-Najm (The Star) · 53:29
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/53/29

>
> So avoid those who turn away from Our remembrance and desire nothing but the life of the world.

### Qur'an · An-Najm (The Star) · 53:32
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/53/32
> الَّذِينَ‌ يَجْتَنِبُونَ‌ کَبَائِرَ الْإِثْمِ‌ وَ الْفَوَاحِشَ‌ إِلاَّ اللَّمَمَ‌ إِنَ‌ رَبَّکَ‌ وَاسِعُ‌ الْمَغْفِرَةِ هُوَ أَعْلَمُ‌ بِکُمْ‌ إِذْ أَنْشَأَکُمْ‌ مِنَ‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ إِذْ أَنْتُمْ‌ أَجِنَّةٌ فِي‌ بُطُونِ‌ أُمَّهَاتِکُمْ‌ فَلاَ تُزَکُّوا أَنْفُسَکُمْ‌ هُوَ أَعْلَمُ‌ بِمَنِ‌ اتَّقَى‌
>
> Those who avoid major sins and indecencies, apart from [minor and occasional] lapses. Indeed your Lord is expansive in [His] forgiveness. He knows you best since [the time] He produced you from the earth and since you were foetuses in the bellies of your mothers. So do not flaunt your piety: He knows best those who are Godwary.

### Qur'an · Al-Qamar (The Moon) · 54:3
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/54/3
> وَ کَذَّبُوا وَ اتَّبَعُوا أَهْوَاءَهُمْ‌ وَ کُلُ‌ أَمْرٍ مُسْتَقِرٌّ
>
> They denied and followed their own desires, and every matter has a setting [appropriate to it].

### Qur'an · Al-Qamar (The Moon) · 54:27
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/54/27
> إِنَّا مُرْسِلُو النَّاقَةِ فِتْنَةً لَهُمْ‌ فَارْتَقِبْهُمْ‌ وَ اصْطَبِرْ
>
> We are sending the She-camel as a trial for them; so watch them and be steadfast.

### Qur'an · Al-Qamar (The Moon) · 54:35
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/54/35
> نِعْمَةً مِنْ‌ عِنْدِنَا کَذٰلِکَ‌ نَجْزِي‌ مَنْ‌ شَکَرَ
>
> as a blessing from Us. Thus do We reward those who give thanks.

### Qur'an · Ar-Rahmaan (The Beneficent) · 55:56
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/55/56
> فِيهِنَ‌ قَاصِرَاتُ‌ الطَّرْفِ‌ لَمْ‌ يَطْمِثْهُنَ‌ إِنْسٌ‌ قَبْلَهُمْ‌ وَ لاَ جَانٌ‌
>
> In them are maidens of restrained glances, whom no human has touched before, nor jinn.

### Qur'an · Al-Waaqia (The Inevitable) · 56:21
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control

> وَ لَحْمِ‌ طَيْرٍ مِمَّا يَشْتَهُونَ‌
>
> and such flesh of fowls as they desire,

### Qur'an · Al-Waaqia (The Inevitable) · 56:46
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/56/46
> وَ کَانُوا يُصِرُّونَ‌ عَلَى‌ الْحِنْثِ‌ الْعَظِيمِ‌
>
> and they used to persist in the great sin.

### Qur'an · Al-Waaqia (The Inevitable) · 56:70
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/56/70
> لَوْ نَشَاءُ جَعَلْنَاهُ‌ أُجَاجاً فَلَوْ لاَ تَشْکُرُونَ‌
>
> If We wish We can make it bitter. Then why do you not give thanks?

### Qur'an · Al-Hadid (The Iron) · 57:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/57/14
> يُنَادُونَهُمْ‌ أَ لَمْ‌ نَکُنْ‌ مَعَکُمْ‌ قَالُوا بَلَى‌ وَ لٰکِنَّکُمْ‌ فَتَنْتُمْ‌ أَنْفُسَکُمْ‌ وَ تَرَبَّصْتُمْ‌ وَ ارْتَبْتُمْ‌ وَ غَرَّتْکُمُ‌ الْأَمَانِيُ‌ حَتَّى‌ جَاءَ أَمْرُ اللَّهِ‌ وَ غَرَّکُمْ‌ بِاللَّهِ‌ الْغَرُورُ
>
> They will call out to them, ‘Did we not use to be with you?’ They will say, ‘Yes! But you cast yourselves into perdition. You awaited and were skeptical, and [false] hopes deceived you until the edict of Allah came, and the Deceiver deceived you concerning Allah.

### Qur'an · Al-Hadid (The Iron) · 57:20
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/57/20
> اعْلَمُوا أَنَّمَا الْحَيَاةُ الدُّنْيَا لَعِبٌ‌ وَ لَهْوٌ وَ زِينَةٌ وَ تَفَاخُرٌ بَيْنَکُمْ‌ وَ تَکَاثُرٌ فِي‌ الْأَمْوَالِ‌ وَ الْأَوْلاَدِ کَمَثَلِ‌ غَيْثٍ‌ أَعْجَبَ‌ الْکُفَّارَ نَبَاتُهُ‌ ثُمَ‌ يَهِيجُ‌ فَتَرَاهُ‌ مُصْفَرّاً ثُمَ‌ يَکُونُ‌ حُطَاماً وَ فِي‌ الْآخِرَةِ عَذَابٌ‌ شَدِيدٌ وَ مَغْفِرَةٌ مِنَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ رِضْوَانٌ‌ وَ مَا الْحَيَاةُ الدُّنْيَا إِلاَّ مَتَاعُ‌ الْغُرُورِ
>
> Know that the life of this world is mere diversion and play, glamour and mutual vainglory among you and rivalry for wealth and children—like rain, whose growth impresses the farmer. Then it withers and you see it turn yellow, then it becomes chaff. Whereas in the Hereafter there is forgiveness from Allah and His approval and a severe punishment. The life of this world is nothing but the wares of delusion.

### Qur'an · Al-Hadid (The Iron) · 57:21
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/57/21
> سَابِقُوا إِلَى‌ مَغْفِرَةٍ مِنْ‌ رَبِّکُمْ‌ وَ جَنَّةٍ عَرْضُهَا کَعَرْضِ‌ السَّمَاءِ وَ الْأَرْضِ‌ أُعِدَّتْ‌ لِلَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا بِاللَّهِ‌ وَ رُسُلِهِ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ فَضْلُ‌ اللَّهِ‌ يُؤْتِيهِ‌ مَنْ‌ يَشَاءُ وَ اللَّهُ‌ ذُو الْفَضْلِ‌ الْعَظِيمِ‌
>
> Take the lead towards forgiveness from your Lord and a paradise as vast as the heavens and the earth, prepared for those who have faith in Allah and His apostles. That is Allah’s grace, which He grants to whomever He wishes, and Allah is dispenser of a great grace.

### Qur'an · Al-Hadid (The Iron) · 57:28
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/57/28
> يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا اتَّقُوا اللَّهَ‌ وَ آمِنُوا بِرَسُولِهِ‌ يُؤْتِکُمْ‌ کِفْلَيْنِ‌ مِنْ‌ رَحْمَتِهِ‌ وَ يَجْعَلْ‌ لَکُمْ‌ نُوراً تَمْشُونَ‌ بِهِ‌ وَ يَغْفِرْ لَکُمْ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> O you who have faith! Be wary of Allah and have faith in His Apostle. He will grant you a double share of His mercy and a light to walk by, and He will forgive you, and Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful;

### Qur'an · Al-Mujaadila (The Pleading Woman) · 58:2
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/58/2
> الَّذِينَ‌ يُظَاهِرُونَ‌ مِنْکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ نِسَائِهِمْ‌ مَا هُنَ‌ أُمَّهَاتِهِمْ‌ إِنْ‌ أُمَّهَاتُهُمْ‌ إِلاَّ اللاَّئِي‌ وَلَدْنَهُمْ‌ وَ إِنَّهُمْ‌ لَيَقُولُونَ‌ مُنْکَراً مِنَ‌ الْقَوْلِ‌ وَ زُوراً وَ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لَعَفُوٌّ غَفُورٌ
>
> As for those of you who repudiate their wives by zihar, they are not their mothers; their mothers are only those who bore them, and indeed they utter an outrage and a lie. Indeed Allah is all-excusing, all-forgiving.

### Qur'an · Al-Mujaadila (The Pleading Woman) · 58:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/58/12
> يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا إِذَا نَاجَيْتُمُ‌ الرَّسُولَ‌ فَقَدِّمُوا بَيْنَ‌ يَدَيْ‌ نَجْوَاکُمْ‌ صَدَقَةً ذٰلِکَ‌ خَيْرٌ لَکُمْ‌ وَ أَطْهَرُ فَإِنْ‌ لَمْ‌ تَجِدُوا فَإِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> O you who have faith! When you converse privately with the Apostle, offer a charity before your private talk. That is better for you and purer. But if you cannot afford [to make the offering], then Allah is indeed all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Mujaadila (The Pleading Woman) · 58:13
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/58/13
> أَ أَشْفَقْتُمْ‌ أَنْ‌ تُقَدِّمُوا بَيْنَ‌ يَدَيْ‌ نَجْوَاکُمْ‌ صَدَقَاتٍ‌ فَإِذْ لَمْ‌ تَفْعَلُوا وَ تَابَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَلَيْکُمْ‌ فَأَقِيمُوا الصَّلاَةَ وَ آتُوا الزَّکَاةَ وَ أَطِيعُوا اللَّهَ‌ وَ رَسُولَهُ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ خَبِيرٌ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ‌
>
> Were you dismayed by having to offer charities before your private talks? Since you did not do it, and Allah has excused you [for not be able to comply], now maintain the prayer and pay the zakat, and obey Allah and His Apostle. Allah is well aware of what you do.

### Qur'an · Al-Hashr (The Exile) · 59:10
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/59/10
> وَ الَّذِينَ‌ جَاءُوا مِنْ‌ بَعْدِهِمْ‌ يَقُولُونَ‌ رَبَّنَا اغْفِرْ لَنَا وَ لِإِخْوَانِنَا الَّذِينَ‌ سَبَقُونَا بِالْإِيمَانِ‌ وَ لاَ تَجْعَلْ‌ فِي‌ قُلُوبِنَا غِلاًّ لِلَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا رَبَّنَا إِنَّکَ‌ رَءُوفٌ‌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> And [also for] those who came in after them, who say, ‘Our Lord, forgive us and our brethren who were our forerunners in the faith, and do not put any rancour in our hearts toward the faithful. Our Lord, You are indeed most kind and merciful.’

### Qur'an · Al-Mumtahana (She that is to be examined) · 60:4
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/60/4
> قَدْ کَانَتْ‌ لَکُمْ‌ أُسْوَةٌ حَسَنَةٌ فِي‌ إِبْرَاهِيمَ‌ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ مَعَهُ‌ إِذْ قَالُوا لِقَوْمِهِمْ‌ إِنَّا بُرَآءُ مِنْکُمْ‌ وَ مِمَّا تَعْبُدُونَ‌ مِنْ‌ دُونِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ کَفَرْنَا بِکُمْ‌ وَ بَدَا بَيْنَنَا وَ بَيْنَکُمُ‌ الْعَدَاوَةُ وَ الْبَغْضَاءُ أَبَداً حَتَّى‌ تُؤْمِنُوا بِاللَّهِ‌ وَحْدَهُ‌ إِلاَّ قَوْلَ‌ إِبْرَاهِيمَ‌ لِأَبِيهِ‌ لَأَسْتَغْفِرَنَ‌ لَکَ‌ وَ مَا أَمْلِکُ‌ لَکَ‌ مِنَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ مِنْ‌ شَيْ‌ءٍ رَبَّنَا عَلَيْکَ‌ تَوَکَّلْنَا وَ إِلَيْکَ‌ أَنَبْنَا وَ إِلَيْکَ‌ الْمَصِيرُ
>
> There is certainly a good exemplar for you in Abraham and those who were with him, when they said to their own people, ‘Indeed we repudiate you and whatever you worship besides Allah. We disown you, and enmity and hate have appeared between you and us for ever, unless you come to have faith in Allah alone,’ apart from Abraham’s saying to his father, ‘I will surely plead forgiveness for you, though I cannot avail you anything against Allah.’ ‘Our Lord! In You do we put our trust, and to You do we turn penitently, and toward You is the destination.

### Qur'an · Al-Mumtahana (She that is to be examined) · 60:5
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/60/5
> رَبَّنَا لاَ تَجْعَلْنَا فِتْنَةً لِلَّذِينَ‌ کَفَرُوا وَ اغْفِرْ لَنَا رَبَّنَا إِنَّکَ‌ أَنْتَ‌ الْعَزِيزُ الْحَکِيمُ‌
>
> Our Lord! Do not make us a test for the faithless, and forgive us. Our Lord! Indeed You are the All-mighty, the All-wise.’

### Qur'an · Al-Mumtahana (She that is to be examined) · 60:7
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/60/7
> عَسَى‌ اللَّهُ‌ أَنْ‌ يَجْعَلَ‌ بَيْنَکُمْ‌ وَ بَيْنَ‌ الَّذِينَ‌ عَادَيْتُمْ‌ مِنْهُمْ‌ مَوَدَّةً وَ اللَّهُ‌ قَدِيرٌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> It may be that Allah will bring about affection between you and those with whom you are at enmity, and Allah is all-powerful, and Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · Al-Mumtahana (She that is to be examined) · 60:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/60/12
> يَا أَيُّهَا النَّبِيُ‌ إِذَا جَاءَکَ‌ الْمُؤْمِنَاتُ‌ يُبَايِعْنَکَ‌ عَلَى‌ أَنْ‌ لاَ يُشْرِکْنَ‌ بِاللَّهِ‌ شَيْئاً وَ لاَ يَسْرِقْنَ‌ وَ لاَ يَزْنِينَ‌ وَ لاَ يَقْتُلْنَ‌ أَوْلاَدَهُنَ‌ وَ لاَ يَأْتِينَ‌ بِبُهْتَانٍ‌ يَفْتَرِينَهُ‌ بَيْنَ‌ أَيْدِيهِنَ‌ وَ أَرْجُلِهِنَ‌ وَ لاَ يَعْصِينَکَ‌ فِي‌ مَعْرُوفٍ‌ فَبَايِعْهُنَ‌ وَ اسْتَغْفِرْ لَهُنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> O Prophet! If faithful women come to you, to take the oath of allegiance to you, [pledging] that they shall not ascribe any partners to Allah, that they shall not steal, nor commit adultery, nor kill their children, nor utter any slander that they may have intentionally fabricated, nor disobey you in what is right, then accept their allegiance, and plead for them to Allah for forgiveness. Indeed Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful

### Qur'an · Al-Mumtahana (She that is to be examined) · 60:13
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: hope
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/60/13
> يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا لاَ تَتَوَلَّوْا قَوْماً غَضِبَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ قَدْ يَئِسُوا مِنَ‌ الْآخِرَةِ کَمَا يَئِسَ‌ الْکُفَّارُ مِنْ‌ أَصْحَابِ‌ الْقُبُورِ
>
> O you who have faith! Do not befriend a people at whom Allah is wrathful: they have despaired of the Hereafter, just as the faithless have despaired of the occupants of the graves.

### Qur'an · As-Saff (The Ranks) · 61:8
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: self-control
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/61/8
> يُرِيدُونَ‌ لِيُطْفِئُوا نُورَ اللَّهِ‌ بِأَفْوَاهِهِمْ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ مُتِمُ‌ نُورِهِ‌ وَ لَوْ کَرِهَ‌ الْکَافِرُونَ‌
>
> They desire to put out the light of Allah with their mouths, but Allah will perfect His light though the faithless should be averse.

### Qur'an · As-Saff (The Ranks) · 61:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/61/12
> يَغْفِرْ لَکُمْ‌ ذُنُوبَکُمْ‌ وَ يُدْخِلْکُمْ‌ جَنَّاتٍ‌ تَجْرِي‌ مِنْ‌ تَحْتِهَا الْأَنْهَارُ وَ مَسَاکِنَ‌ طَيِّبَةً فِي‌ جَنَّاتِ‌ عَدْنٍ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ الْفَوْزُ الْعَظِيمُ‌
>
> He will forgive your sins and admit you into gardens with streams running in them, and into good dwellings in the Gardens of Eden; that is the great success.

### Qur'an · Al-Jumu'a (Friday) · 62:9
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/62/9
> يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا إِذَا نُودِيَ‌ لِلصَّلاَةِ مِنْ‌ يَوْمِ‌ الْجُمُعَةِ فَاسْعَوْا إِلَى‌ ذِکْرِ اللَّهِ‌ وَ ذَرُوا الْبَيْعَ‌ ذٰلِکُمْ‌ خَيْرٌ لَکُمْ‌ إِنْ‌ کُنْتُمْ‌ تَعْلَمُونَ‌
>
> O you who have faith! When the call is made for prayer on Friday, hurry toward the remembrance of Allah, and leave all business. That is better for you, should you know.

### Qur'an · Al-Jumu'a (Friday) · 62:10
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: prayer
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/62/10
> فَإِذَا قُضِيَتِ‌ الصَّلاَةُ فَانْتَشِرُوا فِي‌ الْأَرْضِ‌ وَ ابْتَغُوا مِنْ‌ فَضْلِ‌ اللَّهِ‌ وَ اذْکُرُوا اللَّهَ‌ کَثِيراً لَعَلَّکُمْ‌ تُفْلِحُونَ‌
>
> And when the prayer is finished disperse through the land and seek Allah’s grace, and remember Allah much so that you may be felicitous.

### Qur'an · Al-Munaafiqoon (The Hypocrites) · 63:5
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/63/5
> وَ إِذَا قِيلَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ تَعَالَوْا يَسْتَغْفِرْ لَکُمْ‌ رَسُولُ‌ اللَّهِ‌ لَوَّوْا رُءُوسَهُمْ‌ وَ رَأَيْتَهُمْ‌ يَصُدُّونَ‌ وَ هُمْ‌ مُسْتَکْبِرُونَ‌
>
> When they are told, ‘Come, that Allah’s Apostle may plead for forgiveness for you,’ they twist their heads, and you see them turn away disdainfully.

### Qur'an · Al-Munaafiqoon (The Hypocrites) · 63:6
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/63/6
> سَوَاءٌ عَلَيْهِمْ‌ أَسْتَغْفَرْتَ‌ لَهُمْ‌ أَمْ‌ لَمْ‌ تَسْتَغْفِرْ لَهُمْ‌ لَنْ‌ يَغْفِرَ اللَّهُ‌ لَهُمْ‌ إِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ لاَ يَهْدِي‌ الْقَوْمَ‌ الْفَاسِقِينَ‌
>
> It is the same for them whether you plead for forgiveness for them, or do not plead for forgiveness for them: Allah will never forgive them. Indeed Allah does not guide the transgressing lot.

### Qur'an · At-Taghaabun (Mutual Disillusion) · 64:9
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/64/9
> يَوْمَ‌ يَجْمَعُکُمْ‌ لِيَوْمِ‌ الْجَمْعِ‌ ذٰلِکَ‌ يَوْمُ‌ التَّغَابُنِ‌ وَ مَنْ‌ يُؤْمِنْ‌ بِاللَّهِ‌ وَ يَعْمَلْ‌ صَالِحاً يُکَفِّرْ عَنْهُ‌ سَيِّئَاتِهِ‌ وَ يُدْخِلْهُ‌ جَنَّاتٍ‌ تَجْرِي‌ مِنْ‌ تَحْتِهَا الْأَنْهَارُ خَالِدِينَ‌ فِيهَا أَبَداً ذٰلِکَ‌ الْفَوْزُ الْعَظِيمُ‌
>
> When He will you bring you together for the Day of Gathering, it will be a day of privation [and regret]. As for those who have faith in Allah and act righteously, He shall absolve them of their misdeeds and admit them into gardens with streams running in them, to remain in them forever. That is the great success.

### Qur'an · At-Taghaabun (Mutual Disillusion) · 64:14
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/64/14
> يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا إِنَ‌ مِنْ‌ أَزْوَاجِکُمْ‌ وَ أَوْلاَدِکُمْ‌ عَدُوّاً لَکُمْ‌ فَاحْذَرُوهُمْ‌ وَ إِنْ‌ تَعْفُوا وَ تَصْفَحُوا وَ تَغْفِرُوا فَإِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> O you who have faith! Indeed, you have enemies among your spouses and children; so beware of them. Yet if you excuse, forbear, and forgive, then Allah is indeed all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · At-Taghaabun (Mutual Disillusion) · 64:17
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: patience, repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/64/17
> إِنْ‌ تُقْرِضُوا اللَّهَ‌ قَرْضاً حَسَناً يُضَاعِفْهُ‌ لَکُمْ‌ وَ يَغْفِرْ لَکُمْ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ شَکُورٌ حَلِيمٌ‌
>
> If you lend Allah a good loan, He shall multiply it for you and forgive you, and Allah is all-appreciative, all-forbearing,

### Qur'an · At-Tahrim (The Prohibition) · 66:1
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/66/1
> يَا أَيُّهَا النَّبِيُ‌ لِمَ‌ تُحَرِّمُ‌ مَا أَحَلَ‌ اللَّهُ‌ لَکَ‌ تَبْتَغِي‌ مَرْضَاةَ أَزْوَاجِکَ‌ وَ اللَّهُ‌ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‌
>
> O Prophet! Why do you disallow [yourself] what Allah has made lawful for you, seeking to please your wives? And Allah is all-forgiving, all-merciful.

### Qur'an · At-Tahrim (The Prohibition) · 66:4
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/66/4
> إِنْ‌ تَتُوبَا إِلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ فَقَدْ صَغَتْ‌ قُلُوبُکُمَا وَ إِنْ‌ تَظَاهَرَا عَلَيْهِ‌ فَإِنَ‌ اللَّهَ‌ هُوَ مَوْلاَهُ‌ وَ جِبْرِيلُ‌ وَ صَالِحُ‌ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ‌ وَ الْمَلاَئِکَةُ بَعْدَ ذٰلِکَ‌ ظَهِيرٌ
>
> If the two of you repent to Allah... for your hearts have certainly swerved, and if you back each other against him, then [know that] Allah is indeed his guardian, and his supporters are Gabriel, the righteous among the faithful and, thereafter, the angels.

### Qur'an · At-Tahrim (The Prohibition) · 66:8
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/66/8
> يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا تُوبُوا إِلَى‌ اللَّهِ‌ تَوْبَةً نَصُوحاً عَسَى‌ رَبُّکُمْ‌ أَنْ‌ يُکَفِّرَ عَنْکُمْ‌ سَيِّئَاتِکُمْ‌ وَ يُدْخِلَکُمْ‌ جَنَّاتٍ‌ تَجْرِي‌ مِنْ‌ تَحْتِهَا الْأَنْهَارُ يَوْمَ‌ لاَ يُخْزِي‌ اللَّهُ‌ النَّبِيَ‌ وَ الَّذِينَ‌ آمَنُوا مَعَهُ‌ نُورُهُمْ‌ يَسْعَى‌ بَيْنَ‌ أَيْدِيهِمْ‌ وَ بِأَيْمَانِهِمْ‌ يَقُولُونَ‌ رَبَّنَا أَتْمِمْ‌ لَنَا نُورَنَا وَ اغْفِرْ لَنَا إِنَّکَ‌ عَلَى‌ کُلِ‌ شَيْ‌ءٍ قَدِيرٌ
>
> O you who have faith! Repent to Allah with sincere repentance! Maybe your Lord will absolve you of your misdeeds and admit you into gardens with streams running in them, on the day when Allah will not let down the Prophet and the faithful who are with him. Their light will move swiftly before them and on their right. They will say, ‘Our Lord! Perfect our light for us, and forgive us! Indeed You have power over all things.’

### Qur'an · Al-Mulk (The Sovereignty) · 67:2
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: repentance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/67/2
> الَّذِي‌ خَلَقَ‌ الْمَوْتَ‌ وَ الْحَيَاةَ لِيَبْلُوَکُمْ‌ أَيُّکُمْ‌ أَحْسَنُ‌ عَمَلاً وَ هُوَ الْعَزِيزُ الْغَفُورُ
>
> He, who created death and life that He may test you [to see] which of you is best in conduct. And He is the All-mighty, the All-forgiving.

### Qur'an · Al-Mulk (The Sovereignty) · 67:12
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī

>
> Indeed for those who fear their Lord in secret there will be forgiveness and a great reward.

### Qur'an · Al-Mulk (The Sovereignty) · 67:21

themes: perseverance
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/67/21

>
> Who is it that may provide for you if He withholds His provision? Indeed, they persist in defiance and aversion.

### Qur'an · Al-Mulk (The Sovereignty) · 67:23
grade: Qur'an, translation by ʿAlī Qulī Qarāʾī
themes: gratitude
source: https://thaqalayn.net/quran/67/23

>

> يَغْفِرْ لَکُمْ‌ مِنْ‌ ذُنُوبِکُمْ‌ وَ يُؤَخِّرْکُمْ‌ إِلَى‌ أَجَلٍ‌ مُسَمًّى‌ إِنَ‌ أَجَلَ‌ اللَّهِ‌ إِذَا جَاءَ لاَ يُؤَخَّرُ لَوْ کُنْتُمْ‌ تَعْلَمُونَ‌
>

>
